# v24.11
## ✨ feat
- **Security:** Added compile-time option to disable trace, omitting trace points to reduce memory footprint and attack surface while preserving API/ABI compatibility. Also added a public function to check if trace is enabled at build time. [e7bc451](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e7bc451c996b5882c5d8267725f3d88118009c75)
- **Logging improvements:** Refactored log wrappers in Intel base drivers to use RTE_LOG() directly, replacing custom _RAW macros. Updated logging macros in multiple drivers to include a newline within the macro, preparing for future conversion to RTE_LOG_LINE. Fixed log messages with redundant newline characters. Split multiline log messages in various drivers into separate log calls. Updated logging macros in drivers to use per-line helpers (RTE_LOG_LINE, RTE_LOG_DP_LINE, RTE_LOG_LINE_PREFIX) instead of manual newlines, simplifying macros and ensuring consistent logging behavior. [3cd9f24](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3cd9f24df2b608d879a8980047b69e9c6697dfa7), [e99981a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e99981af34632ecce3bac82d05db97b08308f9b5), [f665790](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f665790a5dbad7b645ff46f31d65e977324e7bfc), [1af8b0b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1af8b0b2747fe6c6267fa7bedb602e569742362e), [2b843ca](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2b843cac232eb3f2fa79e4254e21766817e2019f)
- **Event device enhancements:** Added independent enqueue capability (`RTE_EVENT_DEV_CAP_INDEPENDENT_ENQ`) allowing out-of-order enqueuing of FORWARD or RELEASE events, with support for the DLB2 event driver (disabled by default). Introduced event pre-scheduling with new capability flags and configurable pre-schedule type (none, always, adaptive) at device configuration. Added API for runtime modification of pre-scheduling type on individual event ports, and an API to hint the device to pre-schedule the next event without releasing the current flow context. Added device-level and port-level pre-schedule support for the cnxk event device, removing the previous `gw_mode` parameter. The eventdev test application adds a `--preschedule` command-line option. [79ca24a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/79ca24a41c16445594303a62151ee68156a5a320), [6e2e98d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6e2e98d6775b9d39ef4d5ef75d86416085154c88), [acc65ee](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/acc65ee307f7a18d2d560ebcee750e5407318def), [c1bdd86](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c1bdd86d04d161c07c61ec1be8ef081108d29d2a), [4ade669](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4ade669c2823c0ebcaf7bfb7589db13cb2e4a6d8), [53e736a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/53e736a04dca6725b67dbe1d40b86520b0c28f97), [7a7a04d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7a7a04d3ce8eab9b53d23b673dfdc71b50fd523d)
- **Crypto/security:** Added PMD API to retrieve hardware context pointer (CPTR) from `rte_cryptodev` or `rte_security` sessions, introduced `rte_pmd_cnxk_crypto_cptr_flush` to flush or invalidate a CPT CTX cache entry, and added PMD APIs for reading and writing CPTR (hardware context) with memory ordering. Enables larger packet sizes with TLS by introducing a wrapper function that allocates from both small and large mbuf pools; adds static asserts to validate test vector lengths, defines new macros for proper sizing, and updates mbuf pool creation. Maximum record lengths for TLS 1.2, 1.3, and DTLS 1.2 are set to support larger payloads (up to 17408 bytes plaintext, 18437 bytes ciphertext). [e5abbee](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e5abbeeeefa5760f8516e260f739b1b3dd45bb17), [3ca6074](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3ca607402c4d6b03c0deebc11d087334f31a2736), [0b7f67d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0b7f67de81ecfab8a4d53c4e131b79e02a923abb), [17406ec](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/17406ec24b4ef9c1fc954f774230f461cb037979), [b22cdcc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b22cdccdd366c06e45888713ed865061c3181e3a)
- **Bit manipulation:** Added bit manipulation API for 32-bit and 64-bit words (test, set, clear, assign, flip individual bits) with excellent performance but no memory ordering or atomicity guarantees. Also added a new set of atomic bit operations with memory ordering constraints, using `_Generic`-based macros for C and function overloading for C++. Tests, including parallel tests for multi-core atomic behavior, have been added. [471de10](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/471de107ae234aceea8ee43e153b4d7ec3f3c755), [35326b6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/35326b61aecb8e9653e889c217b775fcbe95e39d)
- **Service cores:** The service cores framework adds two new per-service counters: `RTE_SERVICE_ATTR_IDLE_CALL_COUNT` (invocations with no work) and `RTE_SERVICE_ATTR_ERROR_CALL_COUNT` (invocations resulting in error), enabling calculation of average per-call processing latency for non-idle service calls. [a37e053](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a37e053b2364fc1104988285163261a8c7609dda)
- **Driver-specific:**
  - Support for FLC stashing in the dpaa2 driver, with new functions to configure stashing settings for flow steering actions aligning with RSS. Added `data_stashing_off` flag to queue structure, modified queue setup to use new functions instead of hardcoded values. Environment variable disables data stashing; stashing settings are now per-platform configurable. [c794f2c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c794f2cab6cc04260545784c24e3788204c44e96)
  - Multi-segment packet handling in the Rx inject path now processes non-contiguous packet buffers. [62b5770](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/62b577026d8b6a7059383bc4609d05ca7203f3a5)
- **Breaking change:** RSA padding information moved from the crypto operation parameter to the xform structure, aligning with the virtIO standard specification and ensuring consistency across asymmetric session creation. [8a97564](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8a97564b1c1e035daaa0cdda553edd46178889e2)
- **Breaking change:** Reworked kvargs process API. `rte_kvargs_process()` now only handles key=value pairs, returning -1 if only a key is present. New `rte_kvargs_process_opt()` handles both key=value and only-key cases. Both functions reject NULL kvlist parameter, preventing crashes in drivers that only handle key=value when passed only-key arguments. [de89988](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/de89988365a7ca4087dd451c675320c993910332)
- **Security:** Added compiler attributes (alloc_align, malloc, dealloc) to DPDK's memory allocation functions for compile-time detection of common bugs: using free() on rte_malloc pointers, double freeing, or freeing non-allocated objects. This helps catch memory management errors early in development. [80da7ef](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/80da7efbb4c4216de93b1039b891a6f31fa06f2d)
- Checks if the event device supports pre-scheduling or adaptive pre-scheduling capabilities. If supported, sets the appropriate `preschedule_type` during device configuration to enable event pre-scheduling and potentially improve performance. [6cf329f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6cf329f9d8c2eb97c8f39becd514c14b25251ac1)
- Added a priority field to cryptodev queue pair configuration, allowing priority to be set between highest and lowest values. The implementation may normalize the value; if unsupported, all queue pairs remain at the same level. Testing variable queue pair priority is now supported via `--low-prio-qp-mask` (hexadecimal mask, default highest priority) to verify performance impact on lower priority queues. [6ef8e70](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6ef8e70ecfbd0963a35a301bc9d6d0745891f6e3), [e004aaa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e004aaa83a4afcc9e27b19ad2340a84fdc90f267)
- Added `rte_cryptodev_queue_pair_reset` to reset a specific queue pair without stopping the entire device, avoiding segmentation faults when multiple queues are in use. The caller must ensure no ongoing enqueue/dequeue operations and no inflight packets before calling. Reset a specific queue pair on the cnxk platform. [0a054e8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0a054e8dd5b0960eec4226821137aceb77a2bf22), [3be1df0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3be1df02fb09db123b9372e1fec6b4d741ae53d9)
- Added SM3 hash algorithm support to the crypto driver, and supports the HMAC SM3 algorithm. [9a1d479](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9a1d479742aa5e27313117915f8a465867a2d6e6), [add05a0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/add05a010671f59503198d7e5fe9fc74348aba65)
- Added support for SM4 CBC, SM4 ECB, SM4 CTR, and SM4 in XTS mode algorithms to the AESNI_MB PMD. [0c2f1b0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0c2f1b05ffc7493dada89df10ef0ad704004a870), [4acc862](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4acc862b18a2f1691d1561f7b75542f6a056d41f)
- Adds support for the asymmetric EdDSA algorithm (RFC 8032) to the cryptodev library, including new enum values for Edwards curve instances, a transformation type, and operation parameters for signature generation and verification. Added support for EdDSA in the OpenSSL PMD (sign/verify for Ed25519 and Ed448 curves with context variants) and in the Marvell cnxk crypto driver. Adds test cases for asymmetric EdDSA signing and verification per RFC 8032, with parameters for Ed25519 and Ed448 curves (various message sizes and context variants). Updates test infrastructure to skip EdDSA curves in other tests and handle compressed public keys. Adds a new test function to the OpenSSL and OcteonTX backend test suites. The fips_validation example application adds EdDSA support for signature generation, verification, and key generation, using the DPDK crypto API to process test vectors. Also supports EdDSA using the 25519 curve for signing and verification, including test vectors, session creation, and operation setup functions for performance testing of EdDSA asymmetric operations. [8bd4315](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8bd4315ceba8d9de9dedafdaa963ffecc09cc971), [5a74d7f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5a74d7fd37debc1b4fa1fa44b82fa9cf3b87a291), [a8ebe94](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a8ebe94f8cc11cda874cd0353a47e78279699d10), [2fba523](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2fba5232e77ea72993d282c170bb7be0a48b8110), [12ede9a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/12ede9ac497fed989a1f4d0357e839cbe7d1e45b), [981a1ed](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/981a1ed32a7920bf0f5e2864ab1f78c296bdfaec)
- Added asymmetric operational capability for SM2 and EdDSA, enabling per-operation capability checks. New function checks specific capabilities (e.g., RNG support, pre-hash support). Capability structure extended with operation-specific capabilities array. OpenSSL PMD supports SM2 cryptographic operations: sign, verify, encrypt, and decrypt, with configurable random number generation and pre-hashing. [53c65a3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/53c65a3ce2c6b56cf3fa71621a74b97c41432fc0), [8fdfedb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8fdfedb125beac73e4ebe14f5ffe369d0465ba19)
- Stateless IPsec packet preparation API enables IPsec packet processing without modifying internal session state, allowing users to specify the sequence number for outbound processing. [aae98b8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/aae98b8c6690ccc49d7a1536a1b1ee1264de49a7)
- Included Arm Neoverse N3 SoC part number in build configuration. Support for the Wait for Event with Timeout (WFET) instruction in ARM power management intrinsics. WFET puts the CPU in a low power mode until an event, monitor loss, or timeout. Enabled selectively via FEAT_WFxT in the Linux auxiliary vector; falls back to standard WFE if unavailable. Added descriptive comments to each Arm CPU feature in the enumeration. [cdbcdc5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cdbcdc5573933d709a95668982c7e31eb9249105), [2f1a90f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2f1a90f0455b4920df3a767ab5d9be37dcbf0d12), [c9083da](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c9083daf4426cc386edb3fa4594ce31119ead504)
- Thread registration and unregistration functions simplified by replacing compare-and-exchange loops with atomic fetch-and-or and fetch-and-and operations, improving readability and maintainability while preserving correct synchronization. [8d03152](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8d03152e0b934903d220167c87fe73ea42cc13bf)
- Added total memory size to the output of the memzone and memory segments dump APIs, providing a quick overview of total memory allocated in these structures. [17bb600](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/17bb60044bae68c0f062755527ad8febe9f448d1)
- Added CN20KA A0 model definition, part number, and platform detection macros. Updated model database and header file with new model and associated flags. Mailbox support for the CN20KA platform enables communication between Physical Functions (PF) and the Admin Function (AF). Introduces new register definitions and interrupt vectors specific to CN20K, refactors mailbox initialization to use a platform-specific structure, and handles the new mailbox region aliased to the PF's BAR2 region. Extended mailbox functionality to handle PF to VF messages, and added a handler for PF FLR notify events that invokes the device removal callback to tear down the driver. [966f57a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/966f57a6232ea2efd83cb3d12053390384f645b9), [61deac7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/61deac72abbff3924dd06937887ad399a3a42863), [826da0f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/826da0f56d42e256e647b7d32b5cb567f5263d52)
- **Breaking API changes:** Updated `rte_pmd_cnxk_hw_sa_read` and `rte_pmd_cnxk_hw_sa_write` to accept a port identifier instead of a device pointer, with an added parameter for SA type. SA flush now includes a completion check and error reporting. [a72e156](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a72e15611303cceadc8233b6713e5978089e1587)  
- **New required device argument:** `custom_inb_sa` enables custom inbound Security Association (SA) for inline IPsec processing, making the application responsible for post-processing. Adds a RTE PMD API for configuring inline inbound param1 and param2. This argument must be set for both the inline device and the Ethernet device if an inline device is used. [03b1523](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/03b152389fb15f96e25d9acd87b84c9c22cf8b2b)  
- **Telemetry command changes:** Added a telemetry command to dump SA information for a given port ID and SA index. Modified the existing telemetry info command to report minimum and maximum SA indices instead of dumping all SA details, enabling querying inbound and outbound SA information for a specific SA index. [d74ed16](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d74ed1628f7e3772593be6c48f364816ae85e448)  
- **Device removal detection:** Adds an event callback to detect device removal during probe, terminating execution if removal occurs. Moves global register configuration to `dev_configure()` and validates register reads for invalid hardware return values, including checks for `UINT64_MAX` to handle device removal gracefully. [304ba46](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/304ba46be396d2ec5ddf1d6b02793015785cd823)  
- **New CN20K mailbox and context structures:** New mailbox and context structures for CN20K hardware enable proper NPA configuration. They are the same size as existing structures, allowing typecasting for most fields except where field widths or positions changed. The new mailbox is used conditionally for CN20K; existing hardware continues with the old one. [143a419](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/143a419edf35f3dc093b4f8f7a29163f9c075316)  
- **CN20K NIX register definitions extended:** Includes new register offsets and context structures for receive, send, and completion queues; existing definitions are extended for CN20K platform support; a new mailbox message for CN20K AQ enqueue operations is added; block address discovery logic is adjusted for CN20K. [9a01217](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9a01217e287197cfc2ac778edcec18d84056d244)  
- **CN20K SoC support (mempool, mailbox, NIX, net driver):**  
  - Added CN20K SoC PCI device IDs to the mempool driver. [5ed98ad](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5ed98ad510aeba72dbd3db275cbac555c3fb661b)  
  - Mailbox communication between physical and virtual functions is now supported for cn20k devices, with updated CSR offsets and memory allocation aligned to the next power of two. [9bd368c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9bd368ca311a1ede6c17bb9e39d0e30f6a9abb2d)  
  - Configure NIX receive, send, and completion queues for CN20K with new initialization, configuration, and teardown functions, and update statistics and traffic management to differentiate CN9K, CN10K, and CN20K models. [4785c40](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4785c406c26df4f8c29a84ea89712aa95acde44d)  
  - Support for configuring bandwidth profiles for the Rx policer on CN20K hardware. [43e4281](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/43e42816d36c7348d80104a05a30666eab733da0)  
  - Added CN20K support to NIX debug functionality, including new functions to dump CN20K-specific queue contexts (RQ, SQ, CQ) and integration into telemetry and debug dump infrastructure. The code now differentiates between CN9K, CN10K, and CN20K models with appropriate handlers for each. [db5744d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/db5744d3cd23c485c506bc2a35e91132b1eb9ed0)  
  - Added RSS configuration support for CN20K hardware. [86667e8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/86667e895f5bb78f836cf93fa574c32faf4d36eb)  
  - Add base control path support for the CN20K SoC in the Marvell cnxk net driver. Supports ethdev operations: queue setup, timesync, reassembly, and descriptor dump. Defines offload flags and structures for fast path processing. [1bf0fd7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1bf0fd7e4f5357238e68ab2f53892aad3ba53748)  
  - Selects the appropriate Rx function based on offload flags for the CN20K network device. New functions compute offload flags from device configuration and set the Rx burst function accordingly. Multiple Rx function variants support combinations of RSS, checksum, timestamp, VLAN stripping, security, and scatter. Selection logic chooses between scalar and vectorized paths, and handles reassembly. [8bf5085](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8bf50857ce08f9d330c25820461a2962dc2685d7)  
  - Select transmit function based on offload flags for CN20K. [ec380d4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ec380d45edaa00b5dc3c38d2a3432c6553ee3e30)  
  - Scalar Rx burst support for CN20K, with packet reception, completion handling, and PTP timestamp processing. [493c4fa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/493c4fa524a266d2ee492a86857f2d643ef7acca)  
  - Added vectorized Rx burst support for CN20K using ARM NEON SIMD instructions. The new function handles RSS, checksum, VLAN stripping, timestamp, and multi-segment packets, with a fallback for non-ARM64 architectures. Updated fastpath mode macros to call this vector function instead of returning zero. [9a8b99c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9a8b99cf8822aa4f911da7f3ff512c6259b836ce)  
  - Scalar transmit (Tx) burst support for the CN20K network device is now available, enabling efficient packet transmission without vectorized instructions. The implementation includes new functions for packet preparation, segmentation, security offload, and timestamp handling, along with updates to the transmit queue stop logic and traffic management marking operations. [006c1da](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/006c1daa89b9be6fea53ea49fc34fd382ebe844d)  
  - Added Tx burst vector support for vectorized transmit path performance improvement. New functions for security descriptor preparation, prefree segment handling, LMT store operations, and helper functions for descriptor size and burst parameter calculation. [e829e60](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e829e60c6917de836b4b417961b628b438a9a4b3)  
  - Added multi-segment packet transmission support in the vectorized Tx path for CN20K, including TSO and LSO handling, and improved LMT line management. [98613d3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/98613d32e3dac58d685f4f236cf8cc9733abaaf3)  
- **New PMD APIs and enhancements:**  
  - PMD APIs now allow applications to directly submit CPT instructions to the Inline Device. [de8c60d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/de8c60d113f0f688623f5a93b991f8b0e5dbf5f0)  
  - The PMD API now provides a function to retrieve hardware statistics for CPT queues, including encrypted/decrypted packet and byte counts, enabling direct access to queue-level statistics for improved monitoring and debugging. [d8c8ad3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d8c8ad3a1feef3905c8c3eb27db9d454a6e16856)  
  - `rte_pmd_cnxk_model_str_get()` retrieves the hardware model string of the CNXK device, e.g., 'cn10ka_a1'. [92fa0ac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/92fa0ac7eda65aa0b3c1fd9f8611fe9b297a6e01)  
- **Other features:**  
  - Optional k0 parameter in LDPC decoder API directly specifies the rate matching starting position (3GPP TS 38.212 section 5.4.2.1). When non-null, it overrides the k0 computed from the redundancy version index. [591d38c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/591d38cc83f3ebe69ba44ce136170299d4825fdf)  
  - Adds `enqueue_depth_avail` counter to `rte_bbdev_stats` to track available enqueue batch depth per queue, enabling runtime queue depth monitoring. [f8d8fa0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f8d8fa0eea4b0d4c91da9ff3da03162135c3f706)
- Added basic software live migration support for the NFP vDPA driver, including a software fallback mechanism, interrupt setup for vring relay, VF configuration support, a relay thread for dirty page logging, and enabling VHOST_F_LOG_ALL feature bits. [94fde3a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/94fde3a7f574103ad4ba274bf015bca3879e0408) [10421b0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/10421b0d90751552709498ca15fa25dfa3135495) [e6ac31e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e6ac31e08c7b389994d7968dfe86186647c6f77a) [02fe836](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/02fe8366156a0bc7f3049f92120c185d11bbc217) [adec2a5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/adec2a5ce47fa3fccd82c9796c71eeeb65e99700)
- Added data structures and inline functions to track virtqueue state for split and packed ring formats, enabling backend reconnection without frontend cooperation. VDUSE devices now support reconnection by saving and restoring state to a file. Also restricted `rte_vhost_driver_set_max_queue_num` to only affect VDUSE backends to prevent breaking Vhost-user live-migration. [15677ca](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/15677ca2c751b3be2f02429bb006d859dccae0c0) [da79cc7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/da79cc7fda76a1e1ff9194fc54f1d948d22f4809) [e180899](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e1808999d36bb2e136a649f4651f36030aa468f1)
- Added RTE_FIB_F_NETWORK_ORDER flag for direct use of IPv4 addresses in network byte order, and safe reclamation of tbl8 groups in the DIR24-8 algorithm via RCU QSBR. [e194f3c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e194f3cd5685d5b16c8561a715395a5f579c1bf3) [96c3d06](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/96c3d06a354753cdef9177e0c204c2e9361f31d5)
- Introduced a multi-word bitset API with functions for setting, clearing, testing, flipping, finding, counting, and performing logical operations on bits, as well as shifting, copying, and converting to strings. Added atomic versions of test, set, clear, assign, and flip operations for thread-safe manipulation with specified memory ordering (experimental). [99a1197](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/99a1197647d803e43a676622396ffddf6bf93b62) [c889c03](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c889c037f67342d972ef7a24580d3c24b6f33e26)
- Introduced a new public API to register telemetry command callbacks with an additional private argument, allowing an opaque value to be passed to the callback for purposes like protecting endpoints with a lock. [ceb5914](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ceb5914cd1e153b01bedd9f14a8119355114a21f)
- Added API to dump debug information about a bbdev queue (enqueue status counters, operation parameters) and a new dev op for PMD-level debugging. Also refactored queue allocation in the baseband ACC driver to manage queue memory per operation more flexibly for VRB2 devices, replacing a single shared ring with separate ring buffers per operation type. [353e363](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/353e3639d458f5cdaf3d938aade25579fa490b1b) [3423756](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3423756cf2e33cb222c2f82361c11f5d83a48ba3) [fc65d3d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fc65d3dcabe01770aed9a6e9a57348526f69ca58)
- Corrected PTP initialization for E825C devices by using proper TS PLL parameters, added support for optional flags in the signature segment header for firmware download, and extended firmware capability check to include E830 adapters. [3bb9d73](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3bb9d730d300b33c490289be4bcc5ed388c5d109) [8da382f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8da382fb2d77930e4c4e01f610970ef77bca85b4) [15490b8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/15490b87d9a7c3cabf41b15010f356c2e6de9485)
- Added missing legacy mailbox functions for ixgbe, including `ixgbe_clear_mbx` and its PF implementation. [1be9d85](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1be9d85b13f513f09d1949524255e8fbfa41020a)
- Added VLAN bit to input set for flow director programming, i40e_led_get_blink returns current LED blink status, definitions for DDP package type, and input set mask defines for X722 NIC family. [485464c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/485464cf37247961fe0adbca2172a09ae0a5106b) [dcf9ce7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dcf9ce7d0ef28f0f58adb1a8df7617dda8a41bf8) [4e7dd16](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4e7dd1680896201dedcbfec4f23959198ef48163) [e861ed0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e861ed0b47fec56ecb9a5f8125e48c1048b9fdff)
- Introduced a common function to update queue statistics during enqueue and dequeue, replacing inline stat updates with calls to new inline functions that also update the available enqueue depth, reducing code duplication and improving maintainability. [dded7c6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dded7c68361180a284aaea3e57a9dc01bd41bf2a)
- Cosmetic code changes: moved variable declarations to function beginnings, replaced a ternary operator with a switch statement for clarity, and made minor formatting adjustments. No functional impact. [cbf1bb8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cbf1bb8b47caa0f80dadd555173a29c08441ec4a)
- **VIRTCHNL interface updates**: Added PTP operations for setting/adjusting time and retrieving TX timestamps [286e99f], SyncE support for VFs via new opcodes [886cc4b], VF access to GNSS Console I2C [7025057], HQOS scheduling node management for VSI subtrees (E822/E830) [d00846d], RefSync for DPLL input pin configuration with backward compatibility [a26c659], QGRPS and FLOW_STEER_QGRP capabilities replacing deprecated ADQ [0016b69], and a new `VIRTCHNL_OP_CONFIG_RSS_HFUNC` operation for VF RSS hash function configuration [2381def].
- **rte_flow API enhancements**: Added `rte_flow_async_create_by_index_with_pattern` API with `RTE_FLOW_TABLE_INSERTION_TYPE_INDEX_WITH_PATTERN` [933f18d] and `RTE_FLOW_ACTION_TYPE_JUMP_TO_TABLE_INDEX` action for precise packet steering [2c52a2b].
- **i40e driver enhancements**: Base code now includes named and raw structures in the receive descriptor [35dac96], adds PHY register dump definitions for debugging link issues [10bcd34], FLU register definitions for MAC source pruning [c13b636], and a custom timeout mechanism for NVM acquire operations [d980a40].
- **iavf driver updates**: Introduced an enum for flex descriptor field bit positions (DD, EOP, RXE) [3a988e3].
- **ice driver**: Added support for a custom search path for DDP package files, matching kernel driver behavior [9207f93].
- **GVE driver**: Added TCP Segmentation Offload (TSO) support in the DQO RDA format [403c671].
- **NFP driver**: Now supports multiple Rx queues for flower representor ports, improving load distribution [602792e], and added Ethernet type flow item matching when using flower firmware [7217d25].
- **af_packet driver**: Supports timestamp offloading, extracting packet timestamps from TPACKET_V2 [be10211].
- **Cisco enic driver**: Supports SR-IOV Virtual Functions via an admin channel [00ce431] and adds speed capabilities for newer VIC adapter models (10G to 200G) [543617f].
- **Node and graph statistics**: Added node-specific xstat counters with `rte_node_xstat_increment()` API [070db97] and IPv4 node xstat counters for LPM lookup and reassembly failures [be4c0cb].
- **Queue and tracepoint improvements**: Dump head and tail pointer information for Rx and Tx queues [364a31b] and split RX burst/callback tracepoints into empty and non-empty variants to reduce overhead [e075ca1].
- NFP driver: Updated to use Rx packet offload flag instead of device capability for packet type offload configuration (breaking change). Added `is_pf` flag, support for a new NIC with different configuration BAR size, retrieval of flow steering rules limit from firmware, two new NSP APIs for loading stored firmware and hardware info lookups, firmware loading from flash memory, helper functions for link speed and VF link speed updates, and `idx` field for representor port tracking.  
  [a498019](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a498019d793b3d5ae354a1a947b6bec3bd16fb5f)  
  [c2b4f0d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c2b4f0d5b1c8705ec0a0bcaab37f305ce0b2137e)  
  [19bd7cc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/19bd7cce5705e14f59aeb7bc28dddd9c8cab913f)

  [08ea495](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/08ea495d624b5ef9899e43a62bfe87ecd1c5b1e4)  
  [48de625](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/48de6254632fa248e24b502e56f5336eaca6d30f)  
  [2e3ad18](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2e3ad18750f8f786f3d4e9a5b0f262e01a6386b4)  
  [298f297](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/298f29730a2258df9dd8d2b543e731c4f026cc01)
- Shared MAC and PTP Tx confirmation support: Added TX confirmation queues for PTP, with IEEE1588 support now a device argument (breaking change). Separate Tx confirmation queues for kernel and DPDK to support VSP. Shared MAC interfaces allocate receive queues from VSP schemes and Coarse Classification rules, removing queues allocated without VSP and updating FMC parsing logic.  
  [58e0420](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/58e0420f72f89cb022657253cd1b75d9bc47e5a7)  
  [d11482d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d11482d91b23e2ff2be36a6a0b6b026c5d0bf04e)  
  [9e97abf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9e97abf23766e2853c7a9de86e158540538892ad)
- General features: Added trace points for `rte_flow_async_create_by_index` and `rte_flow_async_create_by_index_with_pattern`. New telemetry command `/ethdev/regs` to dump Ethernet device registers with module name filtering and register names. Configure and query Ethernet link speed lanes. Testpmd now displays Traffic Manager parameters with descriptive labels when adding nodes and shapers. Added helper functions for link speed lookup and VF link speed update.  
  [be5ded2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/be5ded2f96072e887d5155516f8bbe69d1fb07ad)  
  [d916d27](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d916d27e3dca9d2e19e411fff9208929a7c7cbdf)  
  [dd4b8bb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dd4b8bba785faf9d1bb9c4460e75068e2822bdb3)  
  [60bac72](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/60bac72264d8dcec55d58919e4710a70968ae4a8)  
  [52e5e7c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/52e5e7c2d393a244e77997b2b3d2edd6365257b7)
- Broadcom Thor2 NICs: Support configuring fixed link speeds with lane counts, allowing specification of speed and lane parameters for link mode settings.  
  [dc6810a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dc6810a2ab7b4a7c5fc45cb3e82362178a190821)
- DPAA driver: Added BMI statistics and improved extended statistics for the DPAA bus driver. Support for IEEE 1588 PTP via ethdev APIs, including timesync read operations on DPAA1. Packet parsing uses hardware annotation to identify IPSec ESP, GRE, and SCTP packets. Frame display debugging outputs detailed packet parsing with error status, controlled by a new environment variable. Compile-time debugging of mempool corruptions via `rte_mempool_check_cookies`. Added Offline Port (OH) mode and ONIC port mode for inter-application communication.  
  [d2536b0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d2536b006d788039112f9646e4fb2a91ecb6ae45)  
  [7358544](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/73585446921304f543360af104473a955ae46df9)  
  [615352f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/615352f522707e432c1a77f6e8f81a807d43866e)  
  [a350a95](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a350a9543d4e7a842e0ad82ef4ad81c7314f7c2e)  
  [480ec5b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/480ec5b43e51a426bf86759214b4a3b4a70ddb12)  
  [b0827a4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b0827a40f1b9c9562fb14dca69b5e033e8547deb)  
  [a0edbb8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a0edbb8a8e521e0720d31fd910bd9dce41874d9c)  
  [7e5f49a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7e5f49ae767da93486d28142ef53a8fd745f240b)
- **Breaking change:** The ENA net driver's LLQ policy user setting has been restructured: three separate devargs (`enable_llq`, `normal_llq_hdr`, `large_llq_hdr`) are replaced by a single `llq_policy` devarg. This new parameter accepts values 0 to 3: 0 disables LLQ (performance degradation on Nitro v4+ instances), 1 uses the device recommended policy (default), 2 enforces normal LLQ, and 3 enforces large LLQ (required for packets exceeding 96 bytes on Nitro v2 and earlier). This simplifies configuration while preserving the same logic. [d7918d1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d7918d19d25ecfbac7326a28e8ff30c60662e4d7)
- **Breaking change:** Updated `rte_tm_node_add` to mark node parameters pointer as `const`, preventing modification and enabling reuse of the same parameter struct for multiple scheduling nodes, improving const-correctness. [5d49af6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5d49af626c829c465d36dd482ae17abc347f1929)
- Commands read from a file via `--cmdline-file` now output to stdout instead of a single-line success message. [76669d2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/76669d2e7ca9dcf50939882e74b6d06c8ce16e04)
- Added `get_restore_flags()` driver callback to ethdev layer. Previously, `rte_eth_dev_start()` always restored MAC addresses, promiscuous mode, and all multicast mode. Drivers can now implement this callback to specify which parts of the configuration to restore. If undefined, default behavior restores all configuration. [5e46b17](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5e46b176d37787c5536d48b23fff8baf5d674c88)
- Added flow filter resource management: initialization and deinitialization API for flow filter resources, including structures for NIC and Ethernet port device management, resource allocation bitmaps, and reference counting. Implemented functions to create and destroy flow NIC devices, manage Ethernet port devices, and free resources. Integrated flow filter initialization into the ntnic driver's adapter initialization and registered flow filter operations. [1d3f62a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1d3f62a0c4f1f4e2e9ac26aaf840d3e050bc4d86), [36cf85c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/36cf85c8997500c90cd1875bbe43190124660a12), [0e5e289](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0e5e289e4b15ae8032ddb819dce934ae3736a28d), [7917b0d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7917b0d38e92e8b9ec5a870415b791420e10f11a)
- Added high-level interfaces for flow backend initialization and deinitialization, including functions to register and retrieve backend operations, and a done function to cleanly shut down. [0ea00f3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0ea00f33754b342d95623c9af81d827a06c31f58), [8df4a5f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8df4a5f8b10fbc2124a346fd5f2d027ba5718117)
- Added an info flow module for the ntnic driver that tracks hardcoded FPGA parameters and provides an abstraction layer for unsupported parameters. Includes a new backend interface with operations to query hardware capabilities such as port counts, queue numbers, and feature availability. [e3723ca](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e3723ca6f492f3b1df4799ae9248860ddf214be3)
- Added Categorizer (CAT) flow module to net/ntnic driver for protocol-based selection of FPGA pipeline module behavior. Configures sub-modules (CFN, KCE, KCS, FTE, CTE, CTS, COT, CCT, EXO, RCK, LEN, KCC), integrated into flow backend, initialized during device setup. [636b2cf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/636b2cfe0259549bb8df98c30651c90355b679b7), [6e8b7f1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6e8b7f11205f4956756c3c53868b55059e8f6609)
- Added Key Matcher (KM) flow module to net/ntnic driver that checks individual packet field values, supporting exact match via CAM and wildcard matching via TCAM. Includes allocation, reset, and flush functions for RCP, CAM, TCAM, TCI, and TCQ components, along with hardware register definitions and backend integration. [3005c75](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3005c75d6b55c73eeb2c25406b7901bac5b54d6d), [fbe2726](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fbe2726faa592b7ffee79b04083d9c2cc69c77f7)
- Added high-performance stateful SDRAM lookup and programming engine supporting exact match lookups at line rate for up to hundreds of millions of flows. [cec43fa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cec43fab911c9ff28cb2d00a72c306572f1d09e9)
- Added IP Fragmenter (IFR) flow module for net/ntnic driver that fragments outgoing packets based on programmable MTU, supporting IPv4 and IPv6 with configurable MTU and drop behavior. [7d90a44](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7d90a4405e889f7edce73be8b541fc14e0192b61)
- Added Queue Selector module to direct packets to host queues, physical ports, exception paths, or discard. [b95f1cd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b95f1cd053cee23862a0dfc613e95e86dfd5f3aa)
- Added Slicer for Local Retransmit (SLC LR) flow module that cuts off the head of a packet before it leaves the FPGA RX pipeline, used when the TX pipeline adds a new head. Supports initialization, presence check, and RCP flush operations. [8c54532](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8c545325ec2b7a6e787d6ca156cc0b2501c96cac)
- Added Packet Descriptor Builder (PDB) flow module that creates packet meta-data (e.g., virtio-net headers) with hardware definitions, initialization, and backend integration. [7d65ee1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7d65ee1accfb3ee909f4147971738d824243d161)
- Added Header Field Update (HFU) flow module that updates protocol fields (e.g., length, next protocol) in modified packets. Integrated into the flow backend. [a653fa9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a653fa9c505d214ef5dd0c639b793cd281d6d4a6)
- Added RPP local retransmit (RPP LR) flow module to add bytes in FPGA TX pipeline for packet expansion. [f543ca6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f543ca6b9ab2b09750b2e2d44737b0af1ff4df2e)
- Added TX Copy (Tx CPY) flow module that writes data to packet fields using lookups from the FLM module. Supports NAT and other actions via the RTE action MODIFY_FIELD. [d42029a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d42029a54dc9ace2bb1ed1769f9877949730ae67)
- Added Checksum Update (CSU) flow module that updates checksums of modified packets, initialized and cleaned up as part of the flow backend lifecycle. [55f767c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/55f767cb7f66a255159dd5ffb22103fc7ab38072)
- Added TX Inserter module that injects zeros at a specified offset within a packet, expanding it. [689a97b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/689a97b08f1075dc78a3d9ec2cf5802a72f71db2)
- Added TX Replacer flow module that replaces a range of bytes in a packet with data from a table, typically for tunnel data. [0338fef](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0338fef450a4be0f2ccb7cc491e16d6a80caa504)
- Added Tx Packet Editor (TPE) flow module as a software abstraction layer to manage FPGA modules for packet editing in the transmit pipeline, with new data structures, function declarations, and implementations. The TPE tracks and configures FPGA components for operations such as insertion, replacement, copy, header field updates, and checksum updates, enhancing flexibility and control over TX packet processing. [7f05802](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7f058028ff772ef7a413a6fa41e87f41276df110)
- Added API function to adjust Ethernet device clock frequency for PTP high accuracy time synchronization, with ICE driver support for IEEE 1588 PTP frequency adjustment. [be86a68](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/be86a6823f5ae08efbdc11297425fa844508a15d) [c37d505](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c37d50533798987027b89033ac31dac449ccca15)
- Added function to query a node in the traffic manager scheduler tree, returning original parameters, with ice driver implementation for retrieving node details. [25a2a0d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/25a2a0dc3de31ca0a6fbc9371cf3dd85dfd74b07) [bd6bbe6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bd6bbe6db3585d0cb40cebb945a6444f95b4cb88)
- Added multiple FPGA modules for the ntnic driver: configurable hash calculation (Toeplitz, NT-hash), Queue Selector (QSL), Slicer for Local Retransmit (SLC LR), Packet Descriptor Builder (PDB), Tx Packet Editor (TPE), RX MAC Converter (RMC), and DBS register definitions for receive/transmit paths. [afad5ac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/afad5ac406e3896ee8565f7e21da896d2320c427) [0e474ae](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0e474ae51f2a6531b7410b24e80cf12d72a16582) [7fadd2b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7fadd2ba32137dae8cfe1e4634c1b67a6d29e35a) [87ad215](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/87ad21510b751f799286b3c2cb8a62157332f536) [4d4e901](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4d4e9018bea75f85f88c9cff51e80f1b2dbe0c90) [9bd7e52](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9bd7e52d03b1963bda7380f8bceea616bc353d39) [20ab0df](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/20ab0df3dc7c0518d579b6660e531492dad17060)
- Added comprehensive queue management for the ntnic driver: basic operations (config, start, stop, release), eth_dev_close with scatter-gather deallocation, TX/RX queue setup with IOMMU/VFIO mappings, packet handling for virtio queues (split and packed rings), virtual queue initialization in DBS module, split-queue support, availability monitor management, used writer configuration, descriptor reader setup, packed virtqueue ring support, queue release with hardware shutdown, and packet retrieval from virtual queues. [fe91ade](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fe91ade9f5dbda414350a18f6cd8b0b1d9942649) [b0cd36e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b0cd36e9608c89381bda58ba746d39f90202571a) [5284180](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5284180a54988cbf612c66ae864286bb9ce93ea0) [6b0047f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6b0047fadf4116f0e714998d0b82aec910ef6ead) [da25ae3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/da25ae3c88fd38f583d38f9008e5efc4ea6046e9) [576e772](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/576e77213f0d333284d740acdbbe8de510ab7f1c) [e13da07](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e13da07fd9fda5a248abf503d3272a4efcbf44e8) [01e34ed](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/01e34ed9c756c0db67af45639dd1bba1040e8dd2) [67aee0a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/67aee0a69665496f9369f00619d0259c8f89b2fe) [f7b8816](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f7b881659406fcece705eef3fbc042a8e62d3400) [af30088](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/af30088786c2eb65754b258ab8a66d2f212e5dba) [f0fe222](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f0fe222ea9cfe3c8a6972318d02a81a637aefd47) [9c2e6e7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9c2e6e75f695f1c2ab1d2df99f1b4f427357609c)
- Added an alarm-based fallback mechanism to the NFP flower service framework to support DPDK applications without a dedicated service core. [ebb4542](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ebb45428f493df4aed55ac3b31bf6c98de71162e)
- Added testpmd command to write values to a device's EEPROM, enabling testing of the set_eeprom PMD callback. [5478054](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5478054254ca86b6816514b9acd093c51a6d7ffb)
- **Driver enhancements**: Added command to testpmd to control LED on ethdev port (`set port <port-id> led <on/off>`) for physical port identification [045e35a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/045e35aa3fd6a9f82a6b30be81403a77fe4ee551). Improved NFP driver readability with helper functions, consistent port names, and refactored secondary process initialization [eac7eda](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/eac7eda4831ea33749994fa2e49c8a2c39fbed25). Added metadata split support in mlx5 driver for compatibility, generating items/actions/attributes during split without implicit flow rules [821a6a5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/821a6a5cc4951337a7eac64b6cce6a25c01be442). MLX5 net driver now supports input IPv4 and ESP output flex parser arcs [6dfb83f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6dfb83f13f7a6d259e4ecd3d53d40b9ed87e2fe1). Added API to query tunnel mode of a flex item in MLX5 PMD [850233a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/850233aca685ed1142ae2003ec6d4eefe82df4bd). Enhanced NFP driver logging for better probe debugging [aa6b4a8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/aa6b4a80a3afd5c9e3b07f6b516f44cae7505e16).
- **Testpmd shell methods**: Added `start_all_ports` and `stop_all_ports` methods, along with a `ports_started` attribute and decorators [2ecfd12](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2ecfd1267a070ccf13c3f43841e14e62289acc1e). Introduced dynamic TX/RX queue count per port at runtime [07816ea](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/07816ead4d1f660663aa9077b931ffb35f053a82). Added `set_port_mtu` and `set_port_mtu_all` methods with optional verification [9910db3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9910db35962bb22af0b02aa28025720ebf31278a). Created TextParser class to extract verbose information from testpmd output [a91d5f4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a91d5f4748c6feeff571439c5f5c26c4c92edc9a). Added VLAN methods (set vlan filter, rx vlan add/rm, set vlan strip, tx vlan set/reset, set promisc/verbose) and fixed a regex bug [1a7520a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1a7520a1b744b9fff07a5b18c0d0ca0121535844).
- **Capability support**: Added basic capability support for test cases and suites, including a Capability ABC, integration with runner, and skip logic [eebfb5b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/eebfb5bb7b1427e0ba2c48190230cb8701517766). Introduced NIC capability support via `NicCapability` enum and decorators [c89d003](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c89d003806033a5f49befaef758f9cfe43d46d94). Added topology capability with `requires` decorator and inheritance rules [039256d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/039256daa8bfa9d258b57b5fd56c12df9891721f). Added Flag class for RX_OFFLOAD_* capabilities and parser [a79884f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a79884f9baa06eb9220344659ef3d12132428f9d). Added parsing for `show rxq info` command, extracting queue state, scattered packets, thresholds, and Scattered Rx capability detection [c011940](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c0119400ca86fce3cfeca68000e8525ef1d69912). The testpmd `show port info` command now exposes NIC capabilities for runtime queue setup, shared Rx queues, and flow rule persistence [d64f6ba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d64f6ba5f38a847b540fe1e3a592b82ec56fa32c).
- **DTS testing utilities**: Supports sending multiple packets at once via Scapy and efficient packet verification [0bf6796](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0bf6796a8ca351fb79a62841c0afcd35576fcb9a). Added random seed for pseudo-random generation in DTS test runs, with command-line or config file specification [cfe40ba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cfe40bac95fb9c839f1c151f6c101e53e0cbfb63). Added utility to create random L3/L4 packets with configurable payload size, port range, and MTU [f51557f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f51557fb27115ffa4487c5807ebca77023c3f484). Added optional Poetry group for documentation generation using Sphinx [168d6d9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/168d6d94d7309677c539d865693e2e8b422cbbff). The DTS framework now supports testbed topologies with fewer than two links, with dummy ports and topology type checking [0b5ee16](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0b5ee16d1bee1a398aabd31de6597e391969b53e).
- **Breaking change:** ICMP message types and codes from RFC 792 now use the `RTE_ICMP_` namespace prefix instead of `RTE_IP_ICMP_`; old names are deprecated. Deprecated types from RFC 6918 are excluded. [87bde5a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/87bde5ae68732ba62b658411fffd6abce8c80553)
- **Breaking change:** Multiple APIs have been updated to use the new `rte_ipv6_addr` structure (a `uint8_t[16]` array with alignment 1) instead of raw byte arrays or `in6_addr`, improving type safety and consistency. Affected components include: `rte_lpm6`, `rte_fib6`, `rte_rib6`, the cmdline library, IPsec SAD key, `rte_security_ipsec_tunnel_param`, `rte_ipv6_tuple`, and other internal structures. [e1a06e3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e1a06e391ba74f9c4d46a6ecef6d8ee084f4229e), [6cb10a9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6cb10a9bdb6d2d0253e4d022f230371d703d8ac2), [59b9931](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/59b993151ff57e9e8b0fdb1d4b57913243b605fa), [52e04a6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/52e04a6323319ff1a7b4e1d7ed1df2b45d11a0a4), [5ac1abd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5ac1abdd37aa43692603cd8670111c354014766f), [9ac91e2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9ac91e2f7339e66658ef55b756a06b328e336fde), [2ede142](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2ede1422fa57225b0864702083a8c7bea2c5117e), [431e6b9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/431e6b9a618329cc684f6b3db91195757cbae07e)
- Introduced the `rte_ipv6_addr` type and a comprehensive set of utilities: equality check, masking, prefix comparison, mask depth calculation, formatting, literal definition macros (`RTE_IPV6()`), well-known address checks (loopback, link-local, site-local, IPv4-compatible, IPv4-mapped, multicast), multicast scope enum, solicited-node multicast generation from MAC, multicast Ethernet address generation, and IPv6 header version validation (`rte_ipv6_check_version`). Unit tests added. [ca786de](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ca786def84caa9c4f1f36f516477e9a5f58389b5), [3d6d85f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3d6d85f58c1cb88e3906dd3318f232a58be2e10e), [189fdd3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/189fdd3762758486aec347ebdeb9f5bfe74b5600)
- Added strict priority configuration support for DMA devices. A new capability flag allows applications to check if a DMA device supports fixed priority levels; the device info structure now includes the number of supported priority levels, and the configuration structure includes a priority field. [2dff0bc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2dff0bcd3b54bc3279de42123aa618620224ad44)
- Added `rte_ml_dev_queue_pair_count` API to retrieve the number of queue pairs configured on a specific ML device. [804786f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/804786f1012a35ee665e9d0fdf3bee1847a374fc)
- Added data type conversion functions with user-defined scale factor and zero-point for asymmetric/affine integer conversion, enabling flexible quantization and dequantization. [65282e9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/65282e9f8e118a4ca977d1aee2d7f51f44e9bc1b)
- Extracted raw checksum functions into a dedicated header, enabling independent use and preparing for IPv6 symbol separation. [4149b1f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4149b1fb5e0bd3f5339a62c0058125e6305b9122)
- Separated IPv4 and IPv6 symbols into distinct headers for improved code organization; the original header includes both for backward compatibility. [1a2b549](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1a2b549bb482b580424ed40b3b33d0673fcd89b0)
- Added support for outer VLAN flow matching in the hns3 driver. Due to hardware entry width limits, only certain tuples are enabled by default; users can enable outer VLAN matching by disabling other tuples via the `fdir_tuple_config` runtime parameter. [a473284](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a47328471c9a297dbe6caddf9db867cddc902f6d)
- Added `ddp_pkg_file` option to the ice driver, allowing users to specify a custom DDP package file to load at initialization instead of default firmware paths. [9dd6dd1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9dd6dd1db8344cd2e389687d97e5272a98498773)
- Marvell cnxk DMA driver now supports queue priority configuration, enabling fixed priority levels for DMA queues. [fca0bae](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fca0bae93126541c90173d84dae1ead2fe9eeacc)
- Added FFT capabilities including timing offset per CS, timing error, dewindowing, and frequency resampling. [7a875f5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7a875f5697aaab632e45d191888ceda35f78adb0)
- Fixed RSS functionality on VXLAN inner layers by moving VXLAN control flag enable/disable logic into UDP tunnel port add and delete operations. [5b81c1e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5b81c1e2669081c0de8a7bd4b55f2123a7e48c33)
- Corrected a typo in the VLAN match mode devarg macro and registered the `fdir_vlan_match_mode` parameter in the HNS3 PMD parameter string, allowing users to configure VLAN match mode (strict or nostrict) for flow director rules. [bf16032](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bf16032eb1e62338e02b1278e10033366448c5bc)
- Updated representee's RSS rule via PF ports with strict validation that the representee is part of the PF port. [29a8df5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/29a8df5cb664feb6f182c07868c49c0f5c9a4c46)
- Fixed flows installed via single-queue port representors on behalf of multiple-queue representees: an RSS action is now appended to the flow action list when the representee has multiple queues, preserving RSS advantage. [0981a22](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0981a22ec3359793e7515decb07adc5a13d772c0)
- Added missing periods to log messages for consistency. [b6de435](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b6de43530dfa30cbf6b70857e3835099701063d4)
- **Breaking change**: Removed single event enqueue and dequeue operations, retaining only burst functions. This simplifies the fastpath, reducing code complexity and improving maintainability. [a83fc0f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a83fc0f4e118019b2e4fc8f033d59aedce17d7cb)
- **API stability**: Power intrinsics API promoted from experimental to stable, ensuring reliability and long-term support. [86a308f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/86a308fffa7752e460dcc9d8dba632f21ec3a8f3)
- **New API**: Added a new DPDK EAL API for static per-lcore memory allocation (lcore variables), allowing statically allocating small, frequently-accessed data structures with one instance per lcore ID-equipped thread. Lcore variables are similar to thread-local storage but decouple value lifetime from threads, improving performance by organizing data per lcore rather than per module, reducing cache padding and false sharing. Also added a functional test suite, micro benchmark, and programmer's guide covering the API, implementation, and alternatives. [5bce9be](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5bce9bed67ad59aa5aede02256a8490d758b0c29), [b0faa83](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b0faa8330bfdc919c6591850e883b57304bd0fbe), [2cd441b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2cd441bd17fc43768755162bfb218395c795b82d), [776d475](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/776d4753893335d43011f97b08d422b84a54b16c)
- **Log subsystem enhancements**: Unified the log level parser across all OS variants, renamed the existing log option parser, and added a helper function to track all log-related options. Moved the syslog facility property from EAL to the log library, adding syslog support to FreeBSD (defaults to stderr unless `--syslog` is specified, with optional facility argument defaulting to `daemon`). Added a new `--log-timestamp` option with multiple formats (relative, delta, ctime, reltime, ISO) for debugging. The log subsystem now automatically detects and uses the systemd journal protocol when stderr is associated with a journal stream. Log output supports colorization (timestamps in green, subsystem names in yellow, messages in red for urgent, bold for errors, normal for info/debug), disabled by default; enable via `--log-color` with `never`, `auto`, or `always`. [9eeefca](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9eeefca0c0d375dd4c76d3630d5a3913a8f94d96), [9a4276f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9a4276f92d3d9703c3509c67629a0fa3632d53a6), [9851303](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/985130369be32dd68ca104c1ccc86716f6e2bb7b), [62ae114](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/62ae1149f2bdaed3482abb08f2e255f1ac4746e7), [9da0dc6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9da0dc6c0331a65e841d27dde7cae4441294a313), [259f6f7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/259f6f78094d0fa33ce2ffe298b8df526c535f3b)
- **New features**: Added a function to compute IPv4 header checksum for simple cases, skipping the checksum field directly, replacing duplicated manual calculations. Adjusted typecasting to allow compilation without a specific compiler flag. [f9e1d67](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f9e1d67f237a00cf94feb4413e3d978fdd632052)
- Added VDUSE userspace API with ioctls for device creation, IOTLB management, virtqueue configuration, and control messages, enabling Vhost library support for VDUSE devices. [b212c2f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b212c2fc2cd66f21272f48da3bb2e68513c5d92a)
- **Performance**: Optimized thread creation in EAL for Unix by using `pthread_attr_setaffinity_np` to set CPU affinity before thread creation, avoiding a race condition and synchronization overhead. This improves performance of `rte_thread_create()`, especially on ARM systems. The change is conditional on availability of the function (FreeBSD and glibc, not musl). [64f2788](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/64f27886b8bf127cd365a8a3ed5c05852a5ae81d)
- **mlx5 driver improvements**: Refactored unicast control flow rule creation, allowing unicast DMAC and unicast DMAC with VLAN rules to be created independently of `mlx5_traffic_enable()`. Introduced new functions `mlx5_flow_hw_ctrl_flow_dmac` and `mlx5_flow_hw_ctrl_flow_dmac_vlan` with stub implementations for platforms without HWS support. Added internal functions for creating and destroying unicast DMAC flow rules (with and without VLAN) to dynamically add or remove them on address or VLAN changes. Unicast DMAC and unicast DMAC with VLAN ID control flow rules are now registered in a control flow rule list when using Verbs or DV flow engine, with a cleanup function freeing entries before flushing. Added functions for dynamic unicast flow rule management, enabling creation and removal without recreating all control flow rules. Optimizes MAC address and VLAN filter handling: instead of restarting all traffic, only the specific control flow rules are added or removed, making `rte_eth_dev_mac_addr_add()`, `rte_eth_dev_mac_addr_remove()`, and `rte_eth_dev_vlan_filter()` faster. [9660a4e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9660a4e62afd7aef519b306da8eaeb1197da635e), [da7f82b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/da7f82b0af62649612979f6f944ca0fdd8148c4e), [cf99567](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cf99567fe566fb679a2d2485ab7c113a42df04f7), [86d0968](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/86d09686c6c623adf6edd40d1217b48a8e56a425), [d9f2849](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d9f284954a7387789fa6658c284a00de647a692d), [4653286](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/465328609aeca77f455175b12440233dbcc5a826)
- Increased maximum number of supported DV sub-flows from 32 to 64, enabling more complex flow rules, especially with sample actions and RSS. Previously, certain testpmd commands exceeded the sub-flow limit; now they work correctly. [62919d3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/62919d327e58208b21c7972adcf3cdc9c6d7468c)
- STE array matcher isolated under a parent table, not chained to the table matchers chain. Introduced a new jump to matcher action that redirects to another matcher, currently limited to STE array matchers and matchers of size 1. [486f9aa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/486f9aac0cbe2598a76c853890c1d557747f71cf), [efb6249](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/efb62499c623db13b7396231d0da8d1906d23d9b)
- NVIDIA mlx5 net driver: flow rule creation by index with pattern reduces code duplication via a single insertion routine that adapts to insertion type. Jump to flow table index action redirects packets to a specific index. [36c379c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/36c379c82e82bb7a60d17a2bb654988df0ea82ae), [af154d7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/af154d7a00441b54ea1439cbcd4b2d0e9fc0626a)
- Adds support for IP Encapsulating Security Payload (ESP) in transport mode to the mbuf library. Transport mode is detected by parsing the Next Header field (TCP = transport, IP = tunnel). Introduces new packet types RTE_PTYPE_L4_ESP and RTE_PTYPE_INNER_L4_ESP for regular transport mode and ESP over UDP. [2ee0e59](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2ee0e591d24be762a8046307eaeb1dcc87504a11)
- Enhanced DPAA2 DMA driver with multiple hardware queues, scatter-gather (SG) copy operations, silent mode, short frame descriptors (FD) for single transfers, and route-by-port configuration using PCIe port parameters. Refactored to use rte_ring APIs for enqueue/dequeue, moved QDMA header to common dpaax directory, and added a new function to query remaining descriptor ring capacity. Introduced device argument `dpaa_dma_err_check` for driver-level error checking, and ensured TX scatter-gather mempool is created only in the primary process. [3d990fa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3d990faa3f51f4f2ef8198f2971837cfae9ccaa9) [07d679b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/07d679bceee383aa07f508cee4f61ba790030158) [388e888](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/388e888dc082520f3dbe6318ae32fbf99695cf4c) [7cfcce8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7cfcce8e5ed809bd1a1c81ab2b84ab6146a4bbd2) [f1d30e2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f1d30e2786b6c0f2cfd4a6bd2ccc5de5d51ae02c) [1686d80](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1686d80952feaef77c66c5d8ba85d1b70721fc62) [7a7bb89](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7a7bb89e34b33b10eb149bf5934ff9900d19062e) [a77261f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a77261f61245cf1e1880bd1c40511de523618c9f) [a63c642](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a63c6426fdfd9233ba9c7d4503e52bff3732fe69) [748b998](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/748b998046355329c5cb4abc99293c5d925de146)
- DPAA2 net driver: Added PTP one-step timestamping using `dpni_set_single_step_cfg()` MC API, support for DPDMUX method based on C-VLAN and MAC address, enhanced stashing destination control, taildrop configuration for egress queues, soft parser profile management, queue status retrieval, custom TPID, distribution configuration, and table dumping capabilities. Consolidated redundant flow engine code into common functions, added RX parser result dump (enabled via `DPAA2_PRINT_RX_PARSER_RESULT`), and support for combining RAW extract with header extracts from any absolute offset. [2013e30](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2013e3080894eab440c8fbe5a39fe00ca81066d1) [cfe9677](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cfe96771186470ad71d6b0ac9bbadfb1057cd572) [591200e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/591200ef6f32b56adc367ebe3647cc3dbe9362db) [56c1817](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/56c1817d532e0203e85cdce97068dd19deee0e51) [93e41cb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/93e41cb315db171aad78bba09b2983472b7fe8f5) [e21bff6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e21bff64e25667bddb8889e2d34fc7226e21e057)
- fslmc bus: Introduced `rte_fslmc_close` API to close all DPAA2 devices on shutdown, added VFIO DMA mapping API (`rte_fslmc_vfio_mem_dmamap`/`rte_fslmc_vfio_mem_dmaunmap`), dynamic IOVA mode configuration (removed compile-time flag `RTE_LIBRTE_DPAA2_USE_PHYS_IOVA`), multi-process VFIO container/group support via CMSG, and changed DPAA2 devices to use object structure instead of object ID. [274fd92](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/274fd921ff7f2829c4ddb8f488a5a3e17499aad2) [57cb02e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/57cb02edf1224b0464dd1744a7f540c8f7f21a08) [a3c123e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a3c123e22dc685db778de34edb234642cd6e17ac) [4d4399a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4d4399ae859fbb0b1a4390fb8efb7d79a791a4ad) [12dc253](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/12dc2539f7b12b2ec4570197c1e8a16a973d71f6)
- **VXLAN flow matching**: Added VXLAN flow matching with VNI extraction from soft parser context. [39c8044](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/39c8044ffb7bb6956573fc63312e3f93170ce57b)
- **DPAA2 driver flow matching enhancements**: Added support for matching inner tunnel protocols after VXLAN encapsulation, eCPRI flow matching, IPsec AH and ESP flow matching using SPI, and enhanced multiple flow rule extractions with hardware descriptions, IP fragment detection, and improved memory allocation. [9ec2934](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9ec293434e90e95cdbbc4b9fff5370e250e1af20), [a8a6b82](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a8a6b82e80ef3f96ca3370a98c67ae09df940886), [4cc5cf4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4cc5cf4a291d79efe6fd0ffcc9dbdd25549c654a), [25e5845](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/25e5845b5272764d8c2cbf64a9fc5989b34a932c)
- **NT SmartNIC flow offload**: Added API for flow profile configuration, flow operations getter, minimal create/destroy flow operations, internal flow management functions, inline profile using scatter gather, and management functions for flow profile setup and cleanup. [b01eb81](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b01eb812018690b6ec94303a4cdb78bd3d7439a9), [ed01e43](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ed01e43664aaa98b5660972e9b18645080afcc4e), [e526adf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e526adf1fdef0cf12afe5c03c59f7591bd197591), [52fae3f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/52fae3f41b7a4cdd23d61c65e4712982970e1240), [11ea978](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/11ea97805ba167864f318bd32dcd309bf33a4d49), [2005c54](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2005c5493344798df735cfbd1f3b7ac6b97a3b1c), [8385ba0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8385ba0e4008d6a02af5fae8576e119301208b1a)
- **RSS hash key generation improvements**: Replaced static polynomial table with dynamic calculation for better entropy in Toeplitz hash key generation, and added Toeplitz hash key generation for RSS. [f9773e6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f9773e6676950b986c2375d9ac0bcbce8ea1b469), [6addb78](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6addb78158c232bfbb13561c8cbb7be33fb0d4a1)
- **Power management enhancements**: Refactored core and uncore power management libraries for modularity and maintainability, added AMD EPYC uncore driver support, renamed core library source files, introduced per-CPU PM QoS API for resume latency limits, and added PM QoS configuration via `--cpu-resume-latency` option. [6f987b5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6f987b594fa6751b49769755fe1d1bf9f9d15ac4), [ebe99d3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ebe99d351a3f79acf305b882052f286c65cd9b25), [da4d64d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/da4d64d0e80343d54ec22b583c6cc606826e73ba), [f30a1bb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f30a1bbd63f494f5ba623582d7e9166c817794a4), [dd6fd75](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dd6fd75bf662e89edf2cda8f34c37e762e79c274), [4d23d39](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4d23d39fd06ed89b2d2566273b95bbecbd48ed83)
- **Multi-PF firmware support**: Added initialization of PF representor ports, new operations for port start/stop, and updated transmit/receive functions to support multiple PFs with improved performance. [cb6448f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cb6448f173688d8691494925ae2fa9d35546e3ff), [62b6097](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/62b609721dc52ac6b520d77fadd56de50fc6f88d), [636e133](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/636e133ec8913d8a5e964289501060b97e7d4053)
- **Software taildrop support**: Added software taildrop support. [c3ffe74](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c3ffe74d85beb05784607a256ce47d95b91fb1de)
- **DPAA2 endpoint name API**: Added `rte_pmd_dpaa2_ep_name` API to retrieve the endpoint name of a DPAA2 Ethernet device. [a0f8ddc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a0f8ddc41218687134b33f6a462ff0b7eb04df65)
- **Core flow infrastructure**: Added flow create/destroy functions with minimal capabilities using the 'any' item and 'port id' action, including flow API support, group translation, ID table management, and FLM programming for hardware offload [e02fdb6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e02fdb65c2a8a90f4457a04a37a9e7bca4410434). Added infrastructure for flow actions and items, including new error codes, match element structures, flow definition fields, match element handling with CAM/TCAM classification, modify field support for packet manipulation, and conversion functions for flow handle data [6fec9a9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6fec9a9a12e192efdd750509b0f411b63daed75e). Added match and action set database attributes with new data structures and functions for adding, referencing, and dereferencing match and action sets, priority-based allocation of classification function entries, and dynamic activation of filters [032d2b7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/032d2b7603c864ed27935ba2916b052b42c8dadc). Added flow flush support enabling deletion of all flows (including FLM flows) associated with a given caller ID [f7cb842](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f7cb8420e2b004613497048b40baac42f59d6e52). Added human-readable flow dump [6f0fe14](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6f0fe142caedfd0c9dbfb4e1288cfe6b1462c739). Added a thread for handling the flow learn queue [96c8249](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/96c8249be53e94d21e5078cb2f21c3f31bbcd71e).
- **Flow actions**: Added support for RTE_FLOW_ACTION_TYPE_QUEUE with a helper function mapping RX queue indices to hardware IDs, along with support for multiple TX/RX queues, scatter/gather, jumbo frames, and traffic mirroring [b889055](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b8890554b8075bce07d290bef76ebdd76ea55d7b). Added RTE_FLOW_ACTION_TYPE_MARK for packet marking [7495277](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/749527702bd0ac78f4ff8cea56952bbf2c407f4d). Added RTE_FLOW_ACTION_TYPE_JUMP to redirect packet processing to a specified group [8006d07](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8006d07856e01873c9bcf5409c2b9993c7569ae8). Added drop action [ba8c9f9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ba8c9f967b682950de9496dcefef8b25641fa4e5). Added packet modification actions (TTL decrement, DSCP tagging, NAT operations) via RTE_FLOW_ACTION_TYPE_MODIFY_FIELD [339ca12](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/339ca124e659516f35f92d376b24d60af3e3a1e2). Added raw encapsulation and decapsulation actions [c6821ab](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c6821abf58e8e53ca800cbe2cbb30c34536dd682).
- **Flow items**: Added support for Ethernet (destination/source MAC, EtherType with masking) [29584e9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/29584e9dc47b60df55e6e05942d1507ba5db6e9b), IPv4 (source/destination addresses, next protocol ID) [4e2798a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4e2798a722f6b50c103a80d4c16b88ff7ffdfdf1), IPv6 and ICMPv6 [b199509](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b199509a19a05938d38be417639f3c13ad93c87a), ICMP [43999d0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/43999d0d4ddcc5cd252cbff2afc00298ad5b1b18), UDP (source/destination port, outer and tunnel L4) [e872a0c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e872a0c9507a660789b2a7b1fc0f33d633db3b72), TCP (source/destination ports, protocol number 6) [a8fbe91](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a8fbe91974c95497eeec8b18d52f7ee25c4c1bec), SCTP (source/destination ports) [af7ae7a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/af7ae7aa3ca699393fb146d683873b90c69e95e6), VLAN (filtering and matching) [5623282](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/56232827a0d7b0923361353764dc93d28a7bf0e8), port ID [47003b9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/47003b93c375a41889d68e4bf3fc9a2c92c5eb56), and GTP [c6821ab](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c6821abf58e8e53ca800cbe2cbb30c34536dd682).
- **FPGA pipeline modules**: Introduced the Categorizer (CAT) module for selecting behavior of other FPGA pipeline modules based on protocol checks, with set/get/flush support and integration into the inline profile database [833962e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/833962ebb8935089336a0ede3efb1cd8fbb06eb3). Added Packet Description Builder (PDB) module to create packet metadata such as virtio-net headers [ef6e148](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ef6e148b813ca9caf4939b02d609aa9f3728dd6d). Added Key Matcher (KM) module for exact match via CAM and wildcard match via TCAM, enabling up to 140 million flows and policies [9bd46cf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9bd46cf2599ed080b3a64a6b9295b12f61eb553d). Added configurable hash module supporting Toeplitz and NT-hash algorithms [7fa0bf2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7fa0bf29e667c12dc32a8cfd6330a639adcf1358). Added TX Packet Editor (TPE) with set functions for header insertion, replacement, copy, field updates, and checksum updates [0b98e4c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0b98e4c1509c446fac29b53726198e66babded87). Added a high-performance stateful SDRAM lookup and programming engine supporting exact match at line-rate for hundreds of millions of flows [deda5e0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/deda5e0f1c2f1718096319637a3042be7471eb7f). Added Generic MAC Feeder (GMF) module for feeding data directly from FPGA to MAC modules [30b2f87](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/30b2f87ac650e33b9ca34d2f568696016d3cf393). Added Header Field Update (HFU) module registers for updating protocol fields in modified packets [60bc044](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/60bc04468b94a32ddf7cac6d4bd8aacc07be3886). Added IP Fragmenter (IFR) module registers for fragmenting outgoing packets based on programmable MTU [58c2db9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/58c2db9abaa9de58bd8e423cf31e182aabf33263). Added packet head cut before leaving the FPGA RX pipeline when the TX pipeline adds a new head [c4d1272](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c4d1272b279d8c770b6d662db9c5e3d31c7bcf29).
- **Receive Side Scaling (RSS) for the ntnic network driver**: Added support for RSS hash and key update, including RSS based on VLAN or 5‑tuple, various field combinations, multiple hash keys per flow type, Toeplitz hashing, and proper validation of key length and queue count. Handles RSS actions in flow rules, updates configuration, and retrieves current settings. [8eed292](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8eed292b277518a6d969e62b05557331dbec94da)
- **Flow aging support for the ntnic driver**: Added three flow aging functions (`flow_get_aged_flows`, `flow_info_get`, `flow_configure`) to `flow_filter_ops` and `profile_inline_ops`. Extended the age queue module with get, count, and size operations. Added functions to create and free aged event queues, and a scrub profile field in the flow handle. Implemented a flow scanner that periodically checks for aged‑out flows and reports `RTE_ETH_EVENT_FLOW_AGED` (supported only for groups > 0). Added a termination thread to free all FLM info/stat queues and age event queues during deinitialization. [41e430d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/41e430dec4b8369714b196d21b3682264beb2b8a), [57a7d2b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/57a7d2bcff72fdb57192d7cf765f89c059d74840), [e7e01fd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e7e01fd15ddee1eb92d68a3aabe800850a8c757a), [c0d4444](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c0d44442b8315eabda40c1461a85856576b9c728), [4f0f5ab](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4f0f5ab0e4eb5ce654ca315a8faf646bb14c8ef7)
- **Flow metering support for the ntnic driver**: Added meter implementation in the inline profile, enabling flow metering and meter policy API. Supports up to four meter IDs per flow with configurable profiles and policies. Added structures and functions for creating, probing, destroying meters, and reading statistics. Added a meter module with support for adding/removing profiles, creating/destroying flows, adding/removing meter policies, and reading/updating statistics. [4033e05](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4033e0539435c49c10cd17a4837af91fb7e62c57), [c35c06f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c35c06fb4ac1aee609fbca05116655628a844172)
- **Modify actions on existing flows**: Added support for modifying actions on existing flows without recreating them. Extended `flow_filter_ops` with `flow_actions_update` function, delegating to `profile_inline_ops`. [54204ea](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/54204ead942133b853c1a882345a61914ec619ca), [713bf08](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/713bf087cedaf5b77da6eaada9335297675345d0)
- **Statistics support for the ntnic driver**: Added initialization, setup, polling, and reset of counters, plus FPGA register definitions for the statistics module. Added a dedicated polling thread that periodically triggers the statistics module and collects updated values via DMA. Supports port and queue statistics (packet counts, byte counts, error counters, load measurements). Added extended statistics including RMON counters and flow monitoring statistics. Added flow statistics support extending xstats with counters for learn, unlearn, LPS, APS, and FLM statistics, with an event queue system and port event thread. Added FLM stat interface. [effa046](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/effa04693274e59d82b24907c5ee4d1f8eef3cd7), [a1ba8c4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a1ba8c473f5c4b9b3bfb487cc3e18c37c19c8777), [cf6007e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cf6007eac4989cdfc442547f0dd700cc3c76041b), [e7e49ce](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e7e49ce6c760888e62e27084a45446a70305cbfa), [971245a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/971245aa17ca9430b8462f38ad451a27ff909b6c)
- **Time Stamp Module (TSM) for the ntnic driver**: Manages packet timestamping, time synchronization, timestamp format, and PTP protocol. Provides functions for getting timestamps and time, configuring timers, and setting timestamp format. Integrated into FPGA initialization with timers configured for stat toggle and keep‑alive. Extended the FPGA map with TSM module support, adding registers for configuration, sampling, status, and control. [3d600f7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3d600f7d565fc530a411759a080c4039f78fde42), [a9cba85](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a9cba85abe7a8159a1c08af32cf3aa083b112ed2)
- **FPGA module register definitions**: Added MAC Rx module register definitions for receive‑side counters (bad FCS, fragments, undersize packets, total packets, good packets, total bytes, good bytes). Added MAC Tx module registers to track transmitted packet counters. Added RX Packet Process for Local Retransmit module (bytes in FPGA TX pipeline for packet size increases). Added Slicer for Local Retransmit (SLC LR) module registers to cut off packet head before leaving FPGA RX pipeline. Added TX Copy module to write data to packet fields based on FLM lookups (supports NAT and other actions via `RTE_ACTION_MODIFY_FIELD`). Added TX Inserter module to inject zeros at a packet offset, expanding the packet. Added register definitions for TX Replacer module, which replaces a range of bytes using table‑stored data (often for tunnel data). Added Receive Port FIFO (RPF) module controlling the small FPGA FIFO before the packet processor pipeline (initialization, blocking/unblocking, configuring maturing delay and timestamp at end‑of‑frame). Added scrub registers (new scrub fields, control and data registers, product parameter for scrub profiles; removed duplicate macro). Extended the FPGA map with STA module support for statistics functionality.
