[fc777f3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fc777f34f135c36a6fa32e7e0e04980ec86fb207), [3255bfb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3255bfbefb6a5f0fd6314d957e326cc99ab28baa), [f08a116](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f08a1161e52070fbd25529f7ddf615bb408e2a6e), [0beca5d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0beca5d26a883ba2f690956222aee23a266de9ef), [ea5653c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ea5653cfb89e97e299a55442de795c972e63a1dd), [3a747e9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3a747e96c4fed9be947a14f89b85358e62f75cbd), [0214ba6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0214ba672b2559102f141587b33cd43fe1d6a1c3), [21a6609](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/21a66096bb44a4468353782c36fc85913520dc6c), [a7e7728](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a7e77283c2ab6157fd1cbdb86a0e5dcb9e1de550), [672e817](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/672e81740f7c529572f4a3509e7a7df0277e90a7)
- The bonding API functions `rte_eth_bond_member_add`, `rte_eth_bond_member_remove`, `rte_eth_bond_members_get`, `rte_eth_bond_active_members_get`, and `rte_eth_bond_8023ad_member_info` are now stable. [19da63c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/19da63ccfc7e83f06e1bb14d830d4e0de99b13cf)
- Added experimental network driver for ZTE zxdh NX Series Ethernet Controller with basic ethdev initialization, PCI probe, logging, PCI initialization, message channel and hardware lock, device backend information retrieval, interrupt configuration, device information retrieval, and device configure operation. Supports Linux on x86_64 and ARMv8 architectures; datapath and some operations not yet implemented. [29e8928](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/29e89288ff14f153c981bd6658ef80939bc16a05) [425a96e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/425a96e64fb8e1cc5fac9cd2c05abc870c89bd11) [102ac20](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/102ac20e036c53e86ad0b4a3cf1fe5b0ba2f1b3e) [9d80d59](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9d80d5925f35587053373380d74b2a2f4a54c669) [d2fc533](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d2fc5332aa78c4865fd1a4be62531dbdfe613453) [6310e39](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6310e3976bbaa19e03c2918392294bb94af051cd) [3630ac8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3630ac8bd81f09c4cd1d12fe1c5bf84c67141c48) [fea1ddb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fea1ddb0c1bfecd131c43f71b1dddec32845209c) [70d49e4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/70d49e4b97702155b4b4f52623f7a154efddf2c8)
- Added asynchronous flow creation and destruction, along with six new APIs for creating and destroying flow pattern templates, flow actions templates, and flow template tables. Extended the net/ntnic driver with these asynchronous flow template operations. [87b3bb0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/87b3bb06d918af82092b4aadecb9d19ac354606d) [96d92ae](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/96d92ae46407bd381ac4d97d922e588763227897) [8195eb0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8195eb04332ffb2285babe459e997c4087f77100) [1042162](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1042162db4393a19a2eb69722f4e8a7477bf80fd)
- Supports updating flow actions for inline profiles, enabling modification of an existing flow's action list without recreation. This allows dynamic adjustments to packet processing, with the flow handle updated to reflect new actions. [dc52e60](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dc52e60cfae9a93cc748cf350435d31ad1191c47)
- The nfp driver now supports EEPROM function callbacks (getting/setting device EEPROM data and retrieving module EEPROM information) and LED operations to visually identify physical ports by blinking their LEDs, including on/off functions for physical and representor ports. [7f69381](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7f693813cf40f52a428507e01c7dd30648d59ca1) [8bd6f54](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8bd6f5403743ccea850514e29d38fb239312f61d)
- Support for flow rule priority is added to the hns3 driver. Hardware determines priority by the rule's index in the flow director table, where a lower index means higher priority. A new runtime option `fdir_index_config` selects the index strategy: with `hash` (default) the driver generates the index from a hash of the rte_flow key (priority cannot be set); with `priority` the driver uses the rte_flow priority field as the index, enabling priority support. [ac72aae](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ac72aae60f71b8716f65d1e2daf531a5ca38c998)
- Raw device driver enables communication between RVU PF and VF drivers via mailboxes and notifies device interrupts. [318ee1b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/318ee1b0468299e92411ea8616073c477743b34e)
- `--hairpin-map` parameter added to testpmd, selecting Rx and Tx ports and queues for hairpin configuration. It accepts five colon-separated values: Rx port ID, Rx queue, Tx port ID, Tx queue, and number of queues. Multiple parameters can specify different mappings; if omitted, default maps are used. [5334c3f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5334c3feb137ca4eeb4c0f150aae602016b6a5ea)
- The ptpclient example now includes a PI servo controller for frequency adjustment, improving IEEE1588 PTP synchronization accuracy. When enabled with `-c 1`, it calculates master offset and path delay, adjusting clock frequency to converge offset to nanosecond levels. [6d55af6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6d55af611fd51e1e7676ccad620f64cc06ea3ed7)
- Migrated spinlock implementation from pthread mutexes to DPDK's rte_spinlock for improved performance and compatibility. [2407c75](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2407c75530e06fde79e9e0167b162a9253f08b2b)
- Introduced two new compiler/optimizer hints, __rte_unreachable and __rte_assume, to inform the compiler about unreachable code paths and precondition assumptions. Supports GCC, Clang, and other compilers with appropriate fallbacks. [bf7ded9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bf7ded9a07f6c7f9dbf9c6be417302d783e805d6)
- Supports rte_eth_dev_set_mtu API for MTU configuration, including per-port IPv4/IPv6 fragmentation handling with enable/disable options and actions for oversized packets. [6019656](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6019656d6f6848c83591f24867538311545776eb)
- **IPsec offload enhancements**: Initialized IPsec ESN to 1 for ESP packets in DPAA_SEC driver, aligning with RFC4303, and set high/low ESN parts from session config for encapsulation and decapsulation. Added support for copying DSCP and ECN fields in tunnel mode, DF flag copying, TTL decrementing, and updated security capabilities. Added UDP-encapsulated ESP for NAT-T traversal, supporting IPv4/IPv6 tunnels with UDP header/port handling. [123098c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/123098cdd4039858f5ed6941e38c2513ae3940fb) [c253236](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c253236a38843dc4ee2126a397398e8c3a12e9ca) [32d8bc5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/32d8bc55476dcfabf3b3650f75fc19c9b09050ba)
- **RVU LF raw device APIs for out-of-tree drivers**: Added APIs to obtain PF function of NPA and SSO devices, register/unregister interrupt handlers, register/unregister mailbox message handlers, configure message ID ranges (`rte_pmd_rvu_lf_msg_id_range_set()`), process mailbox messages (`rte_pmd_rvu_lf_msg_process()`), retrieve LF device PF function (`rte_pmd_rvu_lf_pf_func_get()`), and retrieve PCI BAR addresses. Also added a self-test function for the cnxk_rvu_lf driver to verify PMD APIs including mailbox communication. [59c1594](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/59c15941bf34887cf75e6286524efa2a5691bfdc) [f4c67d7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f4c67d7218b74b2dab86a6a5d3af12d47e6f55e0) [7396eac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7396eaceaac52e6d66808daaf5ba414a47d2cfe6) [0924cc0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0924cc0b2489f72fcedd92fa4804821c730f7559) [384903e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/384903ed3e6427e1a1a05d3df313a272011e2bf6) [fcac76a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fcac76a874e6419f125e5cf6bbd38bc4097351bd) [79c469d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/79c469df190056eb44ff5c91c6b02d49a1b98884) [aeb8615](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/aeb86158bf15140d49f9904ae40aa0ea9f13ec62)
- **ice driver enhancements**: Added `ddp_load_sched_topo` driver option to load an alternative Tx scheduler topology from DDP package at init. Enhanced Tx scheduler hierarchy to support up to 2000 child nodes per node and expanded layers beyond three. Added `tm_sched_levels` device argument to limit visible scheduler layers (3–8) for backward compatibility. Added support for 200G link speed on E830 devices, including PHY type detection and auto-negotiation. [2a77f64](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2a77f6496fe38dc2f66110d8c0bdf189ef6f0c1c) [715d449](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/715d449a965b104ce95af83211766309e92f33d7) [4ace770](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4ace7701eb443d5dea965ea70fc192c900cbdff9) [36ffcdc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/36ffcdc254be9a6dbdc6cbcbfdb02d5603463e5d)
- **CN20K event device support**: Introduced platform-specific probe, remove, and info get functions for CN20K SoC in Marvell cnxk event driver. Added configuration support to attach requested SSO event ports/queues to RVU PF/VF. Added setup/release for event queues (SSO HWGRPs) and DRAM buffer allocation for inflight events. Added CN20K event port configuration (setup, release, link, unlink) with shared code between CN10K and CN20K. Added fastpath enqueue (new, forward, release) and dequeue (burst, timeout) functions using LMTST and optimized for ARM64. Added quiesce function for port shutdown, profile switching via hardware work descriptor, and prescheduling (none, preschedule, adaptive). Added device start function and flush events API, refactoring common hardware work slot reset and queue stash configuration. [45ce542](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/45ce5425bbbe8c8e4c84134db792243ff0036054) [6976186](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6976186968902cacf3922d5eb17c7f8b8a0041a8) [d2e685b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d2e685b20dcbb93bac61976eb2620a8c01c05d4d) [9736df4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9736df4f1851d8170723cf2820387a86615d2259) [b08b193](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b08b193bceab2202aee3928a0fa2df5e5aa3ee92) [7473a65](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7473a65f2f08006e92c57bf4e411cedc80647b77) [638fe88](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/638fe881ea0f4c78b48004877026b12690bde630) [33da486](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/33da486cd3676aa39bd83f92385e189f6e2c8ccd) [1e2d9b3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1e2d9b3dfc9a475eefa472cbcd3adbd7f43e3f04) [97b495c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/97b495c912e11167e51a58daa01e4aa88a4770dc)
- **SSO hardware capabilities mailbox**: Added a new mailbox message to retrieve SSO hardware capabilities dynamically, replacing hardcoded values. Hardware feature information is stored in the device structure, enabling adaptation to different configurations; redundant headers cleaned up. [8252652](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/82526521ca123dc67361905e0e6e96bf2fa2602c)
- **bbdev-test enhancements**: Added queue dump information capture using the API, with a new flag to dump queue operations to a local file after each test case. Added interrupt disable API call for test coverage. [067fae4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/067fae411cfe5c966f5122afad4b766fa3159cc1) [9235155](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/92351557d57637431807f46f5433886a10efcc91)
- **Breaking changes:**
  - Removed external Exact Match (EM) support no longer functional on Wh+ hardware. Deleted associated source files, removed build system entries, set related function pointers to NULL in device operation structures, added doxygen markers to hide internal structures and macros, and removed associated firmware message functions. [aa49b38](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/aa49b38fa7c350da251b70f455224ae8e3f79d0b)
  - TCAM entry allocation changed from static to dynamic, reducing stack memory usage. Entries now use abstract entry IDs associated with the session instead of hardware-location-based indices. Data structures restructured to be session-based; static arrays removed; dynamic allocation and deallocation functions added. Session management module removed; function signatures updated to use a `struct tf *` pointer instead of a session index. [580fcb3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/580fcb3d718069a8058f4395dd64d19fed0c1f65)
  - Removed selection logic between firmware-based and driver-based TCAM resource allocation; all TCAM operations now use the driver's TCAM manager, eliminating conditional paths and simplifying code. [5873bd3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5873bd31dc8393b17fb7b45bd1f714c227dad3f5)
  - VF to VF flow offload on Whitney now uses custom L2 encapsulation instead of VLAN tags. [032d49e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/032d49ef310179bc0bd165dde4c018884bc5a3e2)
  - Duplicate EM flow entries are now rejected to prevent duplicates in wildcard TCAM. [be4732e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/be4732e8bc48dff2050500825a0d1d601ec68d4b)
- **CN20K event device enhancements:**
  - Added stop and close callbacks for proper shutdown and resource cleanup. [a0fae00](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a0fae00a033b58302ae50dfd0bf1197f1a46bbeb)
  - Added selftest and xstats support. [6305afe](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6305afee038f47b64236069eeb4355d28ef8fec9)
  - Added event Ethernet Rx adapter support, including capabilities detection, queue management, and timestamp handling for PTP. [7c011b0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7c011b0223cc54e0a478dda24c544ccc55967f16)
  - Rx adapter fast path now supports efficient event dequeue operations with various offload configurations (segmented, reassembly modes, with/without timeout). [d8f53c1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d8f53c18203eb2b889eaf154af9552145353e0d1)
  - Added event Ethernet Tx adapter support for event-driven packet transmission. [2dab300](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2dab30000ba66464a3c9f35c041ad0994cd3ec2c)
  - Added fast path support for Tx adapter, including new enqueue functions for multi-segment and all-offload configurations, helper functions for metadata extraction, flow control, and transmit operations. Build system updated. [54101f8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/54101f84f718f583fad6dcbc81858dd5f1f4d3c1)
  - Configuration APIs for the SSO event aggregator now available, enabling event vector generation via new interfaces for managing aggregation contexts, including a function to find an aggregation queue from a tag, helper for optimal alignment size, and queue configuration cleanup. [62afdd8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/62afdd8d493d8f563c053a4afccb3c5acd1acf54)
  - Added event vector support for Rx/Tx adapter, enabling efficient processing of multiple packets as a single event vector. Includes new functions for queue add/delete, vector limits, and handling of vectorized transmit and receive operations. [b775abd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b775abdd412bab2bfa698f7598979155bbbb24b0)
  - Support hardware-accelerated timer arm and cancel operations. [f3c7b60](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f3c7b60769f997be0c49788d7bfc515c59910f83)
  - Added event timer adapter support including HWWQE insertion, functions for arming, canceling, and handling timers, worker modifications, device operations updates, error handling improvements, and configuration options. [822d4ef](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/822d4ef519f66b4f1184a1e708ed758e44b99b3a)
- **bnxt TrueFlow (TF) and ULP layer improvements:**
  - Added flow scale query feature for bnxt TF core, allowing OVS applications to track resource usage for WC TCAM, EM, Action, Counter, Meter, ACT_ENCAP, and SP_SMAC. Updates usage states on both RX and TX directions for default rules, updates only regular flows, and adds a buffer dirty state to avoid unnecessary firmware sync. Disabled by default; enable with `-DTF_FLOW_SCALE_QUERY`. [19f3ac6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/19f3ac618ab2d309e24a3034fcfdacaa6f31c718)
  - Support for VXLAN-GPE in bnxt TrueFlow ULP layer, including header handling and template infrastructure updates. [74cab00](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/74cab005e74976adbc800ab514291bb4c02d11f2)
  - Added support for custom L2 Ethernet type (etype) tunnels in the bnxt driver for OVS. Introduces HWRM and ULP hooks to allocate, free, and manage custom tunnel headers. Adds fields to bnxt structure for tunnel count, ID, and UPAR usage. Updates ULP layer to recognize L2 etype capability and handle tunnel allocation/deallocation during port init/deinit. [5c275d6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5c275d61e19b08740c50f99258e6e4088bf1b663)
  - Added support for Wh+ mirroring, including fixes for mirroring primitives, multi-port input handling, ingress/egress template changes, ADD and SUB operations, multi-action port handling, and port normalization for correct VF-REP placement. [987f2ec](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/987f2ec9a0cfcb835cb62fe4c4abde6a584735a8)
  - A series of miscellaneous fixes and enhancements to the bnxt/tf_ulp driver, including improved VxLAN and VxLAN-GPE handling, support for custom VxLAN ports, conditional parsing of UDP port 4789, API for hot upgrade configuration, correct default parif handling for single-port cards, fixes for crash scenarios and resource management issues, support for up to 256 VF representors, and adjusted log levels. [2921498](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2921498c88ba84f9b0cb69b9a2cfb02ec2e41a0f)
  - Thor2 support added to the ULP layer, including initialization, MPC support for DRAM access, VF support, VF-to-VF traffic offload, renaming of debug macros, error condition handling in flow create, Geneve header and set TTL action parsing, and MPC batching. [dd0191d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dd0191d5e70d0e65a7f041a88af480fc673160e1)
  - Overlapping flows are handled. Flow scale improves by falling back to wildcard TCAM when exact match table insertion fails. Conditional list processing for functional operations. Thor2 platform supports flow overlap. Pipeline supports VFR and non-VFR templates without recompilation. Fixed compilation errors and build warnings. [af50070](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/af50070ef4f994196b09c96b52826e80395bbaea)
  - Set default destination MAC address for ingress flows without one; function operand size support ensures correct data size usage. [32bdbf4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/32bdbf441469b4ade6f34037afae02b159d56b8c)
  - Added endpoint (efid) to session, writes l2 context id in generic tables, allows efid to be added to sid in tfc session code. Releases both rfid and efid from afm. Counter get can clear on request: caller can clear on read or just read. [be4732e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/be4732e8bc48dff2050500825a0d1d601ec68d4b)
- **Breaking:** Several ULP utility functions now return 0 on success and negative error codes on failure. Mapper code condition checks updated accordingly. [c569279](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c569279aded4bb8fdf83b09e0726fa07028dcef8)
- **Breaking:** Template files now support dynamic VF representor mode, replacing the configurable mode. Action records are only populated for packets sent to VF representors, not for network transmissions. Thor2 priority for L2 TCAM records is now properly adhered to. [f5760d7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f5760d720f67863da68ad024ea89f23173bc2cf8)
- **Breaking:** Use default mask values when application does not specify a mask; stats collection for shared sessions uses correct session type; ULP flow database employs 64-bit handles for all resource types; template rejection logic includes table even on unconditional rejection to prevent segmentation fault for VF to VF traffic on Thor 2; protocol header parsing handles cases when both spec and mask are not given; several fixes address Thor 2 priority for L2 TCAM records including L2 context record priority, flow handle mask offset, CPU to LE16 macro issues, and misspellings in code. [f6e1201](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f6e1201540603ced7cbaf8c883b03d859df62923)
- **Breaking:** Added track type field to mapper tables for resource tracking by session or function ID (default: session ID). Consolidated Thor and Thor2 action templates to reduce duplication and simplify action permutations. Corrected GPE flow creation and deletion, including tunnel type and port number. [2aa7099](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2aa70990392930426d92192c59d237dd16de31e5)
- VXLAN tunnel flow statistics support for Thor2. Introduces device feature flags for software and parent aggregation of flow stats, updates templates with new table entries and conditions for tunnel cache and statistics, and modifies the flow counter manager. Enables efficient aggregation via software counters and parent flow aggregation, improving performance and flexibility. [49cdf04](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/49cdf04367be38dec1433ea21ef98d6acd151047)
- Added support for the jump action in the bnxt/tf_ulp driver. The jump action uses a group ID to chain flows with that group attribute. Updated template files and added allocator table support for managing jump indices. [83f916b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/83f916bddb17f2bed168a1093ff898a08cd3008b)
- The bnxt net driver now supports flow priority. Flows with a priority attribute are placed in the TCAM at the correct location, ensuring higher priority flows are matched first. The implementation validates priority ranges and handles default priority flows. [22b6561](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/22b65613909ee32b69d1cceea6485180a6353999)
- Dynamic tunnel UDP ports are supported, allowing runtime configuration of the tunnel UDP destination port based on the flow, including handling of explicit tunnel port configurations, a fix for a segmentation fault in context access, setting a default VxLAN tunnel port, and breaking tunnel flows into outer and inner matches to improve scalability. [94dbd6c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/94dbd6cf36688f36872b6878879442889baac997)
- Adds metering support for Thor2 in the bnxt/tf_ulp driver, including Thor2 meter template tables and ULP support. The rte_mtr API implementation is made device independent to adapt to Thor2 hardware changes. Also fixes a rounding issue in the xir calculation. [80d760e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/80d760e129ae8bf928b3aa77800cd1bc5609d4b0)
- Added flow scale query support in bnxt/tf_ulp driver. Tracks resource usage (WC TCAM, EM, Action, Counter, Meter, ACT_ENCAP, SP_SMAC) when opening a TF session or adding/deleting flows. Users can query usage via niccli. Disabled by default; enable with build flag `-DTF_FLOW_SCALE_QUERY`. Improvements: update usage states for RX and TX default rules, update only for regular flows, and add buffer dirty state to avoid unnecessary firmware sync. [288becf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/288becfb77dfb2d37c80960e3f40f5477f237f6c) [ffbc352](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ffbc3529089ac96517d4065a9b76c730c5586daa)
- Adds support for querying RSS flow information in the bnxt driver's ULP layer, enabling retrieval of RSS parameters (hash function, key, types, queue mapping) for existing flows. [35e03ba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/35e03bafdce10b98fc68383ed323cece507f8bb7)
- Introduces a stats cache table for Thor2 in the bnxt/tf_ulp driver, with new enum values, table parameters, and action templates for meter statistics caching. Updates key info, result field, and ident lists to improve performance. [25b38ed](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/25b38ed0e73f9679225e2c3db9450ac6eca59d80) A stats cache is introduced for Thor2 flows using counters. Flow statistics are periodically harvested in the background, allowing application reads to return cached data instead of directly accessing hardware. Read-clear functionality is added for counter resets, and the stats collection loop is restructured to guarantee full coverage of entries added or removed during the collection period. [0513f0a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0513f0af034df5dc543bb6eb6b17661839491a89)
- Updated template files to support generic template items and fix the generic application template, enabling IPv6 source property cache, VXLAN/Geneve encapsulation enhancements, and improved flow chain handling. Adjusted sizes and indices to accommodate the new functionality. [f76387a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f76387ad8faa5df1ed2d6cd9631893c0b5855930) Template files now support truflow feature configuration, port table write operations, socket direct generic templates, truflow promiscuous mode, metadata for profile tcam entries, and group miss action support. [105126e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/105126eb7e54ad26debbf3d9997c3dce0667aadd) Port table write operations for mirror IDs, socket direct support (configurable via meson), promiscuous mode for unknown MAC addresses, improved metadata usage for profile TCAM entries to better utilize EM entries, group miss action support, and fixes for build failures on certain OS and compiler versions. [d4b36fc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d4b36fc5f0dc59b256441c82e5a9395054026496)
- Meson configuration parameter to enable optional TruFlow capability features, with validation for supported feature bits and rejection of conflicting options. [b413ab0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b413ab0ae7932d2ab27d783870e3d9a698193f08)
- Inlined frequently used utility functions and added likely/unlikely compiler hints for branch prediction. [0c036a1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0c036a1485b9d9163a8fa8059ed5272d060c05e0)
- Optimized CRC32 hash leverages hardware acceleration for improved performance. [b14da65](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b14da6540294be2ecae13b69dbe0b00f93bcc597)
- Buffer split Rx offload in the bnxt driver splits IPv4 or IPv6 packets at a fixed offset to separate headers and data, if hardware supports it. [b5dafa3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b5dafa316ebc9df7da08ae8e98a3776b80ee67c4)
- New network driver for Realtek r8169 NICs with meson build infrastructure, minimal ethdev skeleton, and header with device/vendor IDs. Supports Realtek 2.5 and 5 Gigabit Ethernet NICs (RTL8125 and RTL8126). [9b170cf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9b170cfc6303a9a9a7279149ac6800a72239ad4e) Added logging macros to the driver. [491aa39](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/491aa390728abbf3527d49c4063ebc16d8a47075)
- **r8169 driver: hardware register access, PHY, and configuration**  
  Adds hardware register access routines for the Realtek r8169 network driver, including definitions for registers and bit fields, MMIO read/write, MAC OCP read/write, and CSI read/write functions. Implements macros and functions with polling and delays. Extends hardware structure with an MMIO address pointer. [88f5b65](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/88f5b657aa39dad2451fc48c3b2fd0ece82580d8)  
  PHY register access routines support read/write via OCP and EPHY interfaces, and bit manipulation functions. [c4adac9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c4adac969428db7024a1a160ef630cae250e0523)  
  Hardware-specific functions for multiple RTL8125 chipset variants handle initialization of receive, EPHY, PHY, MAC MCU, and PHY MCU configurations, organized into a structure of function pointers for chip-specific implementations. [8e85226](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8e85226086b45cc60667ad4347a75025fb59e151)  
  Added PHY configuration support including PHY power up/down, PHY and EPHY configuration, and related functionality. [3858997](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/38589978be7d4c3e2ebc6b491c4a145dcfddc07c)  
  Adds hardware configuration support via the `rtl_hw_config` function, which initializes hardware registers during device start. Includes NIC reset, DMA burst sizes, interrupt mitigation, and enabling/disabling ASPM, clock request, and EEE. Also adds helper functions for ERI and OCP register access. [619f6eb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/619f6ebce1152836c44a3e9b783d51f8c431c4a3)  
  Adds hardware initialization support including software variable initialization, NIC reset, and other tasks for proper setup of various Realtek chipset variants. [7d50279](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7d5027916b24efaf6ad974307723054c2f321aff)
- **r8169 driver: Rx/Tx datapath, link status, and statistics**  
  Core logic for Rx/Tx, including function prototypes and stub implementations for initialization and packet burst callbacks. [1bbe869](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1bbe869ee8571aa282a1069c0f2921e13fe58e11)  
  Added RX datapath implementation including RX queue setup, release, info retrieval, receive packet burst functions for normal and scattered packets, RX offload capabilities (checksum offload, VLAN stripping, scatter support), and necessary constants and structures. [2f198f0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2f198f0a20bcf10ee3188588e80b7d5e6965d2fc)  
  Adds transmit (TX) datapath support including TX queue setup/release, TX done cleanup, and the transmit function. Supports TX offloads: VLAN insertion, IPv4/UDP/TCP checksum offload, TCP segmentation offload (TSO), and multi-segment packets. Includes a workaround for PTP packets on specific hardware revisions. [63d37ff](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/63d37ff9a453370a82a28ca8194f38b09aeb8191)  
  Adds link status and interrupt management. Implements functions for updating link status, setting link up/down, and handling interrupts. Supports speed capabilities, link speed configuration, link status events, flow control, PHY speed configuration, and interrupt handling for link changes. [f732767](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f7327670814e69fd5b11871828065940268f664c)  
  Supports hardware statistics (packet counts, error counters) via a new tally counter mechanism. [fa0b0ad](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fa0b0ad6246709184d73fbc944039391c1db22be)  
  Added promiscuous and allmulticast mode support, including enabling and disabling these modes to accept all packets or all multicast packets. [d2b39de](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d2b39de23c1d895627d15acd0c042bb3968c10dd)  
  Adds support for updating the MTU value by implementing the mtu_set callback, setting min/max MTU in device info, and updating the hardware register. [9514e4b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9514e4b6d37db561dd33448c585a9fa42a0e0283)  
  Added support for retrieving the firmware identifier of Realtek r8169 network devices. A new function reads the hardware RAM code, formats it as a hexadecimal string, and registers it in the device operations structure, allowing applications to query this information. The feature is documented. [b2e1725](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b2e17252e6161a1a2c29978bfffbc6f3b3337e8e)  
  Adds driver start and stop support for Realtek RTL8125AP and RTL8125BP network controllers, including functions to notify the DASH subsystem, disable TX/RX, and wait for firmware readiness, ensuring proper communication regardless of DASH status. [b574fb4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b574fb4cc855f4e86659d37ded01e7a218c38865)
- **mlx5: Fixed error CQE handling**  
  Error CQEs were assumed to be 64 bytes, but on platforms with 128-byte cache lines, the CQE size can be configured to 128 bytes, causing incorrect error handling. Introduced a new `mlx5_error_cqe` structure with padding for 128-byte cache lines, updated the syndrome offset, and replaced all references to the old `mlx5_err_cqe` structure, ensuring proper error CQE parsing regardless of cache line size. [3cddeba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3cddeba0ca38b00c7dc646277484d08a4cb2d862)
- **node_dump: Enhanced debug output**  
  node_dump now prints parent ID, init, fini, xstats pointers, and next node details. [0787cdb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0787cdbce54381a3517c24ccec8bbb58215e17b3)
- **Thor2: Counter accumulation and device state check**  
  Counter accumulation in Thor2 for F1F2 VXLAN parent-child flows. Device state check in TF tunnel free API skips resource freeing when device is not started, preventing double freeing. [a089734](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a089734a026a316994674e3f405ee4d56a114efc)
- **bnxt: Rx rate profile selection**  
  Updated HWRM API to support selecting a ring profile, enabling the driver to specify an RX rate profile when allocating RX or RX aggregation rings. Available when the firmware indicates support via the `rx_rate_profile_sel_supported` flag. [57e571c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/57e571c19fe7d9dc02eec6c1ee6379fd393f21fb)  
  Support for Rx profile selection in the bnxt driver. When firmware supports the Rx rate profile selection flag, the driver sets a new capability and configures the Rx rate profile to poll mode during Rx and AGG ring allocation. [7a535f3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7a535f301db655582fe44c26b908a40c9dc4983f)
- **dma-perf: Device-specific parameters and new options**  
  Support for device-specific parameters in the dma-perf application. Each device is now configured individually using `lcore_dma0`, `lcore_dma1`, etc., with parameters such as `lcore`, `dev`, `dir`, `raddr`, `coreid`, `pfid`, and `vfid`. This enables per-device direction and PCIe configuration, allowing mixed direction tests (e.g., mem2dev and dev2mem) within the same test case. Code and documentation are updated accordingly. [533d7e7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/533d7e7f66f39de658ba167aad40837d916c52b4)  
  Added '--burst' command-line option to configure Rx burst size (previously fixed at 32). Supports values 1–512, default 32. Setting to 0 queries PMD for recommended burst size for performance tuning. [d5c4897](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d5c4897ecfb2540dc4990d9b367ddbe5013d0e66)  
  Added `--mbcache` command-line option to configure mempool cache size for mbufs, previously fixed to maximum, now adjustable up to that maximum. [d9f26e5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d9f26e52a55c8a500439d5f0539dfbf5a6b41c3c)
- **kvargs tests: Parameterized and extended**  
  Parameterized existing tests to run with both rte_kvargs_process and rte_kvargs_process_opt. Added a new test for keys without values. Refactored test functions to accept parameters and added a helper handler for keys without values. [0e95a67](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0e95a6777fe3e99b5cd5747a84e6df4aa85c4ec4)
- Enhanced DTS framework: renamed "build target" to "DPDK build" for consistency; added directory copy via tarballs with compression and exclusion; improved DPDK source/build specification from config, CLI, or environment; replaced JSON schema and dictionary-based config with type-hint-based validation and cross-referencing checks; added TestSuiteSpec dataclass and automatic test suite discovery; added test run headers and JSON summary. [ecaff61](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ecaff610f53d7f4771150a99ffb54e27159f6029), [80158fd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/80158fd411bb06d1a1c22f56fd387ecc49eaf15d), [f995766](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f995766758403e13a7b97e6e3e863ac900716844), [9f4e8bd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9f4e8bdc8c500321935580d638f8bf3fecf9a7cc), [c64af3c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c64af3c7a80ee76a771017b56487be1062c7ae1f), [b935bdc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b935bdc3da26ab86ec775dfad3aa63a1a61f5667), [9f8a257](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9f8a257235ac6d7ed5b621428551c88b4408ac26)
- Added GDTC raw device driver for Dinghai SoC GDMA controller, enabling host-to-host communication via rawdev API. Includes queue initialization/release with ring buffer parameters, basic device operations (start, stop, configure, reset, attribute query, queue management), enqueue and dequeue support with error recovery and software ring management. [30495f5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/30495f54583d93d50df02ec5e15cbaad6f6dcc6a), [d373c66](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d373c66ef139ad4fa1847c4ea11f37a50b497fb7), [648001a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/648001aa00a15e48bb7f6a3acdf6fc9b48895dbb), [a73d74c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a73d74c2e30e7111b71b863aadf0c351a0f7ec8c), [81c6bac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/81c6bacb0cdc43bc90d72e66968777653fbc4e31)
- Added a helper script to buildtools that converts a text file into a static const char array in a header file, suitable for embedding text data in C code. [50614eb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/50614ebc112e53d54193f8b14ff7d1b37b5be15b)
- Added code snippets for rule creation using template and non-template APIs, with reusable functions for efficient flow rule implementation. [16158f3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/16158f34900075f2f30b879bf3708e54e07455f4)
- Added test suites: VLAN filtering/stripping/insertion, L2 forwarding with 1/2/4/8 queues, MAC/multicast address management with capability check, dynamic queue runtime configuration (stop/reconfigure individual queues), and layer 3/4 checksum offload (IPv4, IPv6, UDP, TCP, VLAN, SCTP). Also added `csum_set_hw` method and `ChecksumOffloadOptions` flag class for automated checksum testing. [e41fe1f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e41fe1f8df707c7de026eae6b529126469bb955f), [f5418c9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f5418c97fc19a20234ec0cb72ef0ffb5e93fdf2e), [e3b16f4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e3b16f45cb17fe7dc4251e493fa5e9ef235bfd0d), [474ce44](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/474ce443f7fb8f316faa362ecaf3e6841b3ad6b1), [2eef9a8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2eef9a80df4a2662f60ae313d779ef1e02227844), [bee7cf8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bee7cf823cd80f83b026a02508ba05fdac1b4113), [8c9a747](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8c9a7471a0e601b2f7a29a30daa4ec23cb484f77)
- Supplementary macros providing opposite values to existing toggle-like macros: ENABLE_FRAGMENTATION, IPV4_DF_FORWARD, and IPV6_FRAGMENT, enhancing MTU handling configuration options. [190e99b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/190e99be4fd06ab10bc9a05b4740c05c55665529)
- The mlx5 PMD extended statistics counters for the scheduling send on time feature include indicators for interrupt servicing delays, hardware errors on rearm and clock queues, and application timestamp issues (past, future, out-of-order). Additional counters measure clock jitter, long-term stability, and synchronization status; non-zero sync loss requires a port restart to restore proper scheduling. [4843aac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4843aacb0d1201fef37e8a579fcd8baec4acdf98)
- Documentation includes tested Intel platforms and NICs. [cb490d9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cb490d9bf8638fe01b7873bdde67d18dd372feca)
## 🐛 fix
- **Alarm API fixes**: Added parameter validation for `rte_eal_alarm_set` on Windows, ensuring valid expiry intervals and non‑NULL callbacks, and reverted a test workaround that skipped these checks, enabling consistent cross‑platform validation. [7f34ecb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7f34ecb6fef6cd6668d5449aabb26f1d3bce0452)  
  Alarm API is now supported on FreeBSD, allowing the alarm test to run correctly on all supported OSes. [fc7eec2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fc7eec29dd7f9fcdf440ae23a6bc8580418bd9a9)  
  Fixed a starvation issue in the alarm cancel mechanism when two threads concurrently execute `rte_eal_alarm_cancel` and `eal_alarm_callback`. Added `sched_yield()` (or `SwitchToThread()` on Windows) after releasing the lock to allow the callback thread to proceed. [a4835c2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a4835c22ccfb5c5ba0aa5b32ebbafc0df12bf75a)
- **Forbidden token check script fixes**: The script now resets token count between file evaluations, preventing incorrect passes for multi‑file patches, and reports all warnings across multiple files instead of stopping at the first. Removed unused expressions array. [84cf9b7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/84cf9b71fc2d6baf1869ef213daee7ec9f8880bc) [d500e69](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d500e69f644f0f98ed4ea7114481f2612056234c)
- **Logging improvements and fixes**: Fixed logtype token registration where the token was incorrectly quoted, causing escaped quotes. [6cb9465](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6cb94658813b5fcea1e35cbb51cefb5c5b57f33d)  
  Updated drivers to use the default log type from the build framework instead of hardcoded strings, replacing custom prefixes for consistency and to prevent mismatches, and simplified log type definitions. [df47324](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/df47324b6022119e9ce3774795ddb40446762895)  
  Corrected macro definitions to use proper logging macros, fixing double newlines, and adjusted log calls to remove redundant newlines. [6a41e60](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6a41e6070ea6f251987f81f9d610190b9adfb978)  
  The DSW event driver now uses a dedicated logtype instead of the generic EVENTDEV logtype, improving log message clarity and allowing independent log level control. [5727bd6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5727bd63bc6840f2db07043b1ffaa4e1619817ea)  
  Removed redundant newlines from Marvell driver log messages to fix build errors. [8df7165](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8df71650e9fdc6346f09b7a57e86cded7b553152)  
  BP_LOG macro relocated to base driver (only used there). [cd98310](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cd983102803b12cd01ed7bb4c45933165703f56a)
- **TSC estimation accuracy improvements**: Changed rounding granularity from 10 MHz to 100 kHz, reducing drift that could reach a second per 10 minutes. [7268f21](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7268f21aa044309a74592a78955d7051bc7063c1)  
  Timer now uses OS TSC frequency instead of the often inaccurate CPU‑provided value. On Linux x86, uses OS‑calculated value when kernel distrusts CPU frequency; on FreeBSD, directly uses kernel’s tsc_hz for improved accuracy. [dbdf3d5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dbdf3d5581caa1de40b5952e41d54b64e39536d1)
- **Driver fixes**: Removed non‑functional debug macros from the DPAA network driver. [2a0c7fb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2a0c7fb22dcd2081323696ca7dcaa4ce65a00c12)  
  Added a workaround to the Octeon EP driver to suppress a false positive GCC warning about an uninitialized variable when compiling with O3 optimization; the `devinfo` structure is now zero‑initialized. [6f0f106](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6f0f1065cc0e20b73bf69602d1d069a4be76d126)  
  Removed the REX prefix from the movdiri instruction because it was invalid for 32‑bit code and unnecessary for 64‑bit code. [41b09d6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/41b09d64e35b877e8f29c4e5a8cf944e303695dd)
- **Build system and configuration**: Replaces library‑specific AVX‑512 build checks with standard x86 configuration variables, simplifying the build system and ensuring consistency. [82621e2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/82621e2fec8143c50f8847f385d6ee646f556b24)  
  Sets schedule type to SCHED_TYPE_PARALLEL. [adadb55](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/adadb5585bd50260c3fa5495fcbe8baf64386f7e)
- **Memory leak fixes**: Memory pools allocated during test suite setup are now freed during teardown, preventing leaks. [acab7f3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/acab7f3fdfe09bc684ccc7804fdf3c8ff3268878)  
  Fixed memory leak in PDCP session creation when invalid data is provided. [9c0abd2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9c0abd27c3fe7a8b842d6fc254ac1241f4ba8b65)
- **Test and example fixes**: Reintroduces a short delay in a dummy service callback function by refactoring switch cases, restoring intended test behavior and fixing a Coverity issue about unreachable code. [fd1bcb6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fd1bcb6c58b0752f408beaedfdb83a2e82173129)  
  Modified eventdev test application to measure DMA adapter forward latency; DMA operations now use a mempool to prevent reuse before completion. [bca734c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bca734c27e345af500d0d951421584e2567cd107)  
  In OP_FWD mode with internal port support, target event queue is now TEST_APP_EV_QUEUE_ID instead of TEST_DMA_EV_QUEUE_ID. [367fa35](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/367fa3504851ec6c4aef393a7c53638da45a903e)  
  Reset txq to zero in first worker stage to fix segmentation fault in eventdev_pipeline example caused by invalid queue_id from mbuf pre‑written by NIC driver. [f6f2307](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f6f2307931c90d924405ea44b0b4be9d3d01bd17)
- **Code cleanup and style**: Replaced zero‑length arrays with flexible array members in event device code. [29911b3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/29911b323e7a4200b95e2049df08779c0673fbfc)  
  Replaced custom TRUE/FALSE with standard true/false from stdbool.h. [9632654](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9632654250891db798ee154e9f808cc5584011c7)  
  Removed several unused macros to improve code clarity. [e296fe6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e296fe619ce276b645a4fb1243c9be6dc40de9f3)  
  Moved opening braces in function definitions to new lines to comply with coding guidelines. [e04de8d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e04de8d5d1cde53dd71a77811971572996185366)
- **Maintainers file updates**: Updated maintainers file to correct documentation paths after program guide reorganization. [bf0ff8d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bf0ff8df59c7e32f95c0b542cc4a7918f8a3da84)  
  Maintainer with bouncing email address removed. [b384624](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b3846244b2b9245356d398d802ba3c975b2f4f63)
- **Other fixes**: Fix a watchdog issue in PDCP SNOW‑ZUC operations by adding a Jump instruction with the CALM flag to ensure previous processing completes before proceeding. [2369bc1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2369bc1343fa5aac2890b2a3e12d65a2f1a2fd31)  
  Added a new contributor entry to the mailmap. [79e8689](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/79e8689b9d53b3feca235a6e4b661cb98f1432b1)
- **Breaking: Context flush returns error code instead of aborting** – The context flush function now returns an error code instead of aborting, enabling callers to handle store faults gracefully. Callers must check for and handle this error. [e781595](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e78159500d56015064c11f04c43ed2e25c02b5c1)
- **Breaking: PMD API for cnxk crypto driver uses opaque pointer type** – Updated to use opaque pointer type instead of void* for queue pointer and submit functions, improving type safety. [21653af](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/21653af489e7c695c7ddccb317f9957f93409597)
- **Breaking: RSA xform structure updated to support ASN.1 syntax** – Changed private key member from union to struct, allowing simultaneous specification of exponent and quintuple, aligning with ASN.1 requirements and benefiting VirtIO asymmetric implementation. [0e3b2fc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0e3b2fc18c6b9bae9c6d51779bcd2b057ba0e300)
- **Security: Context pointer alignment validation** – Adds validation that the context pointer (CPTR) is 128-byte aligned before a context write, returning an error if not. Also defines a new macro alias to support this alignment check. [d53727d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d53727d0a4244cdff51b6892483a5616f3bb730e)
- **Security: Removed unused ENA_ABORT() macro** – Enforces the rule against `abort()` in drivers and libraries to improve code quality and align with project guidelines. [917b7c7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/917b7c78cee5ea96862fa6351a646bf305a99b6d)
- **Security: Limited packets dequeued from crypto device to maximum burst size** – Prevents stack corruption and ensures routing functions do not exceed allocated array sizes. [88948ff](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/88948ff31f57618a74c8985c59e332676995b438)
- **Security: Fixed device event unregister bug** – Unconditionally removed all callbacks with device_name set to NULL; now only callbacks with matching cb_fn and cb_arg are removed, preventing unintended deletions. [66fd2cc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/66fd2cc2e47c69ee57f0fe32558e55b085c2e32d)
- **dpaa2_sec crypto driver enhancements** – Enhanced IPsec RFLC handling by pointing Response FLC to FLC descriptor context for debug during dequeue, enabling only data stashing while disabling annotation stashing to avoid performance impacts, and fixing memory allocation alignment with improved error handling in session setup. [8c8bbb1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8c8bbb14560ffda3a22de37dcd6a8301dd2508da); PDCP FLC handling: set RFLC with the FLC IOVA address and enable data stashing only. [ac965c9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ac965c980c6164b12540ecf7205f1643ee5757d6); Removed prefetching of mbuf and crypto_op in event mode, as hardware does not access these data structures. [fa95cbb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fa95cbb55c91dbbadb5f8ba1581ddfd603c446ff); Reworked debug logging to output information per mode, replacing simple debug macros with conditional code using sprintf to build detailed strings including buffer pool type, BPID, private data size, and frame descriptor details. [673382d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/673382d4906f16d87fa11ec7e50cc9fe58c30baa); Padding added to passthrough data ensures its length is a multiple of 16 bytes for SM ciphers, including calculating passthrough length, adding padding, and adjusting offset and pointer calculations. Removed error check for offset exceeding 256 bytes; updated offset control value to use padded passthrough length. [f593ed5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f593ed5b0ae9da85936bb4871130c56b92a35b26); Rearranged struct fields to remove a memory hole, improving memory alignment and reducing wasted space. [1c3f756](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1c3f7561503734cb62616324d524ddb6e22e6044)
- **Crypto test and statistics improvements** – Crypto test retries up to 100 times with 1 ms delay when verifying IPsec security stats. [db65855](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/db65855a0317ab5ce3a1f682e64dc61d654afdb3); Removed a 1 millisecond delay when retrieving IPsec statistics per session, which caused significant overhead in systems with many sessions. Statistics are now retrieved without the delay; if accurate stats are needed, applications can call the stats function again after a short delay. [f852c95](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f852c95807f37f390e21874fbfc681442ad865f6); Fixed a bug in the crypto scheduler where session size computation only considered worker session sizes, ignoring the scheduler's own session context size, causing incorrect memory allocation. [b00bf84](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b00bf84f0d3eb4c6a2944c918f697dc17cb3fce5); Freeing mbufs in crypto event vectors now correctly extracts the mbuf pointer from the crypto operation structure instead of assuming the vector's mbufs array contains mbuf pointers. [991b085](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/991b0859151a461fffd114fed36905e9106e6361); Extended packet number (XPN) support is enabled in the l2fwd-macsec example application, allowing traffic to run longer without raising a packet number threshold interrupt. [0d187ba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0d187ba834b1a7f67fa73265743dfff9d8f706fe); Skips IPsec post-process verification for negative inline IPsec tests, checking for SEC_OFFLOAD_FAILED flag and adding assertion to validate packet length. [4677de0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4677de0a4c2ba803d0e1adc26774f2c6c8b5b6df); Reverted the planned update to the `rte_cryptodev_cb_fn` function prototype; new `rte_cryptodev_queue_pair_event_error_query` API allows applications to retrieve the queue pair ID from an error interrupt. [76b354b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/76b354be229926ccdf28951c3dbab4f4bb9570ea); Removed unused list end enumerators that blocked adding new asymmetric algorithms and RSA padding types without ABI breakage, enabling future API extensions. [e17a4a8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e17a4a880550278ad367109be54116b098c0627a)
- **General infrastructure fixes** – Move 'extern "C"' guards after includes so they don't cover included files; remove guards from headers that only define macros or struct types; eliminate a build check that required all headers to have such guards. This allows C++ translation units to properly handle `__Generic` macros and other constructs. [719834a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/719834a6849e1daf4a70ff7742bbcc3ae7e25607); Enhanced bit manipulation API to handle volatile-qualified pointers, enabling use with memory-mapped I/O and other volatile contexts. [0883d73](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0883d736a7e59fc1847e9c8185dc43eb43d32630); Replaced `__attribute__((__may_alias__))` in the cxgbe driver with `__rte_may_alias` macro from `rte_common.h`. [3e49a10](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3e49a10f2ede1d047bbdec9ee56e2227459278f7); Maximum number of file descriptors passed to a secondary process increased from 8 to 253, enabling more queues for software devices like TAP and XDP. [5ff00bb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5ff00bbc04d8338108241b083b7a6238208cfbc6); DSW event driver now supports independent enqueue, enabling applications to set the RTE_EVENT_PORT_CFG_INDEPENDENT_ENQ flag during port setup if the device supports it. [0dab9af](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0dab9afe0c2d4d47f79241050e35158d53f62b01)
- Fixed multiple use-after-free bugs in memzone tracing, baseband driver, QAT device probe, idpf mailbox, error cleanup path, cnxk mempool, e1000 filter flush, debug logs, TAILQ iteration, and net/nfb driver. [a306620](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a306620e357af9d1e2b99a93fabc40b382c3fa88), [6ffb344](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6ffb34498913f84713e98d6a2a21d2a86028a604), [1af60a8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1af60a8ce25a4a1a2ae1da6c00f432ce89a4c2eb), [4baf54e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4baf54ed9dc87b89ea2150578c51120bc0157bb0), [db92f4e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/db92f4e2ce491bb96605621cdd6f6251ea3bde85), [c024de1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c024de17933128f37b1dfe38a0fae9975be1b104), [58196dc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/58196dc411576925a1d66b0da1d11b06072a7ac2), [757b0b6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/757b0b6f207c072a550f43836856235aa41553ad), [1198622](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/11986223b54d981300e9de2d365c494eb274645c), [76da983](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/76da9834ebb6e43e005bd5895ff4568d0e7be78f)
- Fixed memory deallocation mismatches by replacing `free()` with `rte_free()` for pointers allocated with `rte_malloc` or `rte_zmalloc` in BPF conversion, device structure, IDXD DMA, raw ifpga driver, and other areas. [a3923d6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a3923d6bd5c0b9838d8f4678233093ffad036193), [b1703af](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b1703af8e77d9e872e2ead92ab2dbcf290686f78), [91b026f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/91b026fb46d987e68c1152b0bb5f0bc8f1f274db), [ae67f7d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ae67f7d0256687fdfb24d27ee94b20d88c65108e), [d891a59](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d891a597895bb65db42404440660f82092780750)
- Removed duplicate `rte_free` call in NFP flow destroy to prevent heap corruption. [fae5c63](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fae5c633522efd30b6cb2c7a1bdfeb7e19e2f369)
- Fixed invalid free in cpfl JSON parser by removing erroneous `rte_free` call on a non-heap pointer. [1c20cf5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1c20cf5be5c8b3e09673a44da2ce532ec0f35236)
- Reorders structures to keep logically separate blocks together: moves SM2 operation structs alongside other algorithm operation structs, and moves the asymmetric crypto transform data struct accordingly. [6d66f08](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6d66f08e794257cdb30c566bbc1c360b529e4665)
- WFE-related macros (`__RTE_ARM_WFE`, `__RTE_ARM_SEV`, `__RTE_ARM_SEVL`, and `__RTE_ARM_LOAD_EXC_*`) are now independent of the `RTE_WAIT_UNTIL_EQUAL_ARCH_DEFINED` macro, always defined for ARM64. Power monitor and wakeup functions now check for `RTE_ARCH_64` instead of `RTE_ARM_USE_WFE`, making them available on all 64-bit ARM platforms. [990b065](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/990b065f3a3b209282527203663dfb7991723917)
- CPPC support in the DPDK power library is now enabled, fixing initialization failures and adding CPPC mode to supported power management environments. [35220c7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/35220c7cb3aff022b3a41919139496326ef6eecc)
- Detects frequency scaling driver support during initialization, logging an error and failing if unsupported, preventing use of unsupported drivers and improving robustness. [bc5ca53](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bc5ca53efb4b97a943085d625408410b59080bb6)
- Added `STDIN_FILENO`, `STDOUT_FILENO`, `STDERR_FILENO` macros to Windows for POSIX compatibility. [e0bf217](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e0bf217330a89c30505f218ce89005cfb662af7c)
- Attribute check in development tools excludes a common header to prevent false positive warnings when adding attribute wrappers. [93f8d73](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/93f8d73ac18de2df963698d31d8dd819392deade)
- AVX512 lookup function for IPv6 FIB now includes runtime checks for `RTE_CPUFLAG_AVX512DQ` and `RTE_CPUFLAG_AVX512BW`, in addition to the existing AVX512F check, ensuring the vector function is only used when the CPU supports all required AVX512 features. [45ddc56](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/45ddc5660f9830f3b7b39ddaf57af02e80d589a4)
- Fixes handling of chained mbufs by correcting the EPB block length calculation to use `pkt_len` instead of `data_len`, preventing file corruption. [6db3585](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6db358536fee7891b5cb670df94ec87543ddd0fb)
- Fixes a crash in dumpcap when handling jumbo frames in legacy pcap mode. `rte_pktmbuf_read()` on large segmented frames could return NULL, causing a core dump. The fix increases the temporary buffer size to accommodate jumbo frames and adjusts the captured length capping, similar to the pcap PMD. [5c0f970](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5c0f970c0d0e2a963a7a970a71cad4f4244414a5)
- Adds support for dumping malloc heap information in the memory dump option via a call to `rte_malloc_dump_heaps()`, enhancing memory debugging. [c33717c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c33717c6f886b840e2121a14048f59d1c0929621)
- Introduced `sfc_kvargs_process_opt()` to handle keys without values. Removed redundant NULL checks for the value parameter in existing handlers, as `rte_kvargs_process` already handles this, simplifying code and improving robustness. [f33e8c0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f33e8c0e4a80c1456987f96c1ce448d65e7d6dfb)
- The net/tap driver uses the `rte_kvargs_process_opt()` API to handle key-value arguments in `key=value` or `only-key` formats. [4ed3061](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4ed306131f85ef180589169b868f292d9149b0c7)
- Paul Szczepanek and Patrick Robb have been appointed as the new maintainers for the DTS (DPDK Test Suite) project. [7d7a41f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7d7a41f9ec223ede1d749d260e0409713c635f14)
- Inline functions removed from the list of functions subject to interface stability guarantees. The experimental tag removed from several inline function declarations, and their entries deleted from the list. Only functions with actual interface stability implications remain tracked. [909a133](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/909a13331668883b5f997773123d2a34ad5e079d)
- Fixed mbuf_addr corruption when receiving timestamps on a VF attached to a DPDK application with timestamping enabled on the PF in the kernel. The tstamp_dynfield_offset was zero for PTP-enabled PF. The fix registers the Rx timestamp dynamic field and flag in the PTP info update callback, updates the fast path rx_pkt_burst, and expands the condition for registering the timestamp to include the ptp_en flag. [0efd93a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0efd93a2740d1ab13fc55656ce9e55f79e09c4f3)
- Fixes Rx offloads to handle timestamps in VF when PTP is enabled in the respective PF. Updates condition for setting NIX_RX_OFFLOAD_TSTAMP_F to also check dev->ptp_en. Adds callback function to notify SSO event device about timestamp offload changes when PTP status updates. [f12dab8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f12dab814f0898c661d32f6cdaaae6a11bbacb6e)
- Update RX offload flags during link up notification to fix Rx timestamp handling for VF when PTP is enabled before running the application in event mode. [697883b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/697883bcb0a84f06b52064ecbf60c619edbf9083)
- Inbound Out-Of-Place (OOP) processing failed for SKUs that do not support reassembly because the NIX_RX_REAS_F flag was not updated in event mode. Fixed by registering a callback to update the offload flags when a session is created. [d524a55](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d524a5526efa6b4cc01d13d8d50785c08d9b6891)
- Updated event device to use NIX_RX_REAS_F flag, fixing out-of-place handling in event mode for CNXK devices that do not support reassembly. [01a990f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/01a990fe40e827c5f3497f785ce7fd68bff8ef5c)
- Fixes an issue in the network driver where mbuf rearm data was incorrectly updated for Rx inject packets. The driver now avoids setting the mbuf next pointer to NULL for injected packets and conditionally applies rearm data updates only for non-injected packets, preventing data corruption. [0cce86f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0cce86f9966909bd68ade7cfa42ffafb90470ae2)
- Removes a restriction that prevented clearing RPM statistics on cn10k platforms by removing the early return that bypassed the reset logic. [1eb1bb3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1eb1bb394b013b844671bbad0a1f1048d633e8be)
- Changing or adding a MAC address in the LMAC filter table no longer fails when active VFs are present on a PF in a reconfigure state. [2d4505d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2d4505dc6d4b541710f1c178ee0b309fab4d2ee8)
- Context flush operations now read a specific register to verify completion, issuing a warning if the flush fails. [6c3de40](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6c3de40af8362d2d7eede3b4fd12075fce964f4d)
- Corrected initialization of CPT HW word size for outbound SA to two words by setting ctx_hdr_size field appropriately. [9587a32](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9587a324f28e84937c9efef534da542c30ff122b)
- Fix IRQ reconfiguration by unregistering SSO device and NPA IRQs before resizing, cleaning up stale handles and preventing issues. [758b58f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/758b58f06a43564f435e3ecc1a8af994564a6b6b)
- Compile-time platform configuration for the cnxk common driver to differentiate among SoCs. Sets platform-specific defines (ROC_PLATFORM_CN9K, ROC_PLATFORM_CN10K, ROC_PLATFORM_CN20K) based on the target platform, enabling conditional compilation for different hardware variants. [814bede](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/814bedeeed7ccd44191fcc7222254ece5fcc42c6)
- Define new PF/VF bit encoding parameters for the cn20k platform to support more PFs, while maintaining existing parameters for cn9k/10k. Updated internal logic uses platform-specific encoding to ensure correct interpretation of pcifunc values across hardware generations. [7816df7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7816df7911736f7f880db04fa62cdbd20405a63c)
- Reduced aura field width in cn20k from 20 to 17 bits, requiring adjusted bit field shifts for register reads/writes. Shift value for aura handle now conditionally set to 47 for cn20k and 44 for other models, ensuring correct operation of aura allocation, count, limit, and availability functions. [620fc02](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/620fc02bf7ebfb9c8ca8d4a391df9e243397270c)
- Extended CN10K mempool operations to support CN20K, ensuring appropriate mempool ops are selected for CN20K devices. [8d1ddeb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8d1ddeb6c3382e834fbc0a459788a9f58e1ae7fd)
- Renamed error codes and added model-specific error retrieval routines for CN10K devices. [63b82e2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/63b82e242251f8684d46390b9e8bb9dcc5d34147)
- Changed the CNXK base driver log level from NOTICE to INFO to ensure logs appear during application execution. [adc561f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/adc561fc5352bd1f1c8e736a33bb9b03bbb95b3f)
- Updated nfp_parse_class_options() to use rte_kvargs_process_opt() instead of rte_kvargs_process(), enabling handling of both key=value and only-key arguments for improved parsing flexibility. [3977d07](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3977d07df186e6f4c79802db19451f51703a3d60)
- Fixed a typo in the Meson build system where `file` was used instead of `files`, causing an "Unknown function 'file'" error when DPDK is used as a subproject. The correction allows the `dpdk-cmdline-gen.py` script to be overridden correctly in subproject contexts. [672c329](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/672c32999e18d7194de90d12d3166c1a967dcb44)
- Graphviz export readability is enhanced by coloring arrows to sink nodes dark orange, removing their oval shape, and setting text color to dark orange for improved clarity in large graphs. [5b8d861](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5b8d861cfe89ebcbb08760c7817be0b79b9ff6f9)
- Node context stores opaque pointers directly, eliminating intermediate structures. An unnamed union in rte_node enables either a byte array or two void pointers. Fast-path area fields reorganized into a cache-aligned unnamed struct for C/C++ ABI/API compatibility. Static assert ensures fast path area does not exceed 64 bytes. [28f0225](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/28f0225a9c30c6ba168c83abf23fbee9c54f562e)
- Set I/O layout to packed and corrected quantized/dequantized buffer address calculation for TVM models with a single MRVL layer. [c4636d3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c4636d36bc2cc3a370200245da69006d6f5d9852)
- Added optional k0 parameter to specify circular buffer starting position, overriding legacy rv_index-based computation. Applies to both encoding and decoding, using provided k0 when available. [cb9dc56](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cb9dc567af0392850a622e6ba80020bcc65de1e5)
- Removes unnecessary type casts by leveraging the built-in type cast of the `rte_pktmbuf_mtod_offset` macro, which already includes a type cast, making the explicit cast redundant. Simplifies code and improves clarity. [3f9eab1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3f9eab142058e7e4867e9f675889430a0eee8e87)
- Queue numbers for FFT and MLDTS operations are explicitly initialized to zero across baseband PMDs. No functional impact as these operations are not currently supported. [0f4e990](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0f4e9909bc517d845e47da803f8b534691bbe5a3)
- Fixed a potential use-after-free bug in the baseband ACC driver by removing debug logging that accessed operation addresses during queue stop. The operation memory may have been deallocated, so accessing it could cause a crash. The fix removes the debug print functions and their calls from the queue stop routines. [a090b8f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a090b8ffe73ed21d54e17e5d5711d2e817d7229e)
- Explicitly set pointers to NULL after freeing memory to prevent double-free errors. [b460b3b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b460b3bbbbe708bc8ac2426c42bd96f18d04c41c)
- Allocated an extra 4 bytes at the end of the tbl24 table to fix a potential out-of-bounds access in AVX512 vector lookup. [66ed178](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/66ed1786ad067198814e9b2ab54f0cad68a58f1e)
- Fixed a bug where the offset parameter in the mmap call for mapping the log base address was incorrectly set to 0. The offset is now correctly passed as the last argument, ensuring proper mapping of the log area. [bdd96d8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bdd96d8ac76ca412165b2d1bbd3701e978246d8e)
- The soft output bypass RM capability is removed due to hardware limitations; the flag is no longer advertised and is set to zero. [2fd167b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2fd167b61bc6c6f40a6c04085caa56be40451e2a)
- Interrupt support removed from the VRB1 device. [afa685d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/afa685dffeaf0e2cb96dff51963b5af415a6902e)
- Replaced manual bitset implementations with the `rte_bitset` library for service flags and port-to-queue links, enabling support for more than 64 services/ports and improving scalability. [34ec238](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/34ec23845d9e3fe066d8d38fba9ebb0b9e7cdb5e) [fb011cd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fb011cdae56cc698ae76b17635c5b1968f5754b4)
- The `rte_vhost_driver_set_max_queue_num` API has been promoted from experimental to stable. [d8381a8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d8381a8f54cdc789c4260efefb5a17b9612f417e)
- Improved resource utilization and correctness by calculating maximum queues per device based on device variant, and replaced hardcoded size constants with dynamic `sizeof` calculations for bbdev structures. [2135244](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/21352447018b9f055161cd7546197fce82b370e0) [77120a7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/77120a7b9472c6fd41aee66c29561477d7440bb4)
- Fixed software ring alignment in baseband ACC driver to ensure power-of-two size, and reverted LDPC decoder algorithm to MS1 version for improved performance under MU1 fading. [6aea11c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6aea11c12484489f95549a3b952e98c0a32c5c55) [b9cb8e6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b9cb8e68b9bbc4bbb2347b09556207da0a37b398)
- Simplified HARQ memory handling by removing explicit reset check and automatically resetting data length, and improved info ring entry management by resetting only the valid bit. [6cdecfc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6cdecfc1f56bec8d799376a5f8c05a4603c4d6d3) [63dcaf2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/63dcaf20304d24305bf0113f370bea2721307c1f)
- Fixed a bug in the atomic bit operations test where a 64-bit reference word was incorrectly used in the 32-bit test variant by using the correct bit-width type. [899cffe](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/899cffe059b8e7ef602d4e622abc6c34f28a2bef)
- The vDPA used ring relay now correctly updates flags such as VRING_USED_F_NO_NOTIFY by properly copying used flags from the source vring to the vq's used ring, preventing incorrect device behavior. [b3f923f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b3f923fe1710e448c073f03aad2c087ffb6c7a5c)
- Fixed vDPA NFP driver issues: corrected control word reconfiguration and offload handling, and added vring index recovery after migration. [d149827](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d149827203a61da4c8c9e4a13e07bb0260438124) [9725f32](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9725f3260004cffafe8437deb6a6bcdb18794c23)
- Reset the used index counter when the virtio device is reinitialized during ethdev reconfiguration. This ensures proper reset after recreating and repopulating virtio rings, preventing potential issues. Fixes a problem introduced in a previous commit that added support for control VQ for packed rings. [ff11fc6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ff11fc60c5d8d9ae5a0f0114db4c3bc834090548)
- Fixed a hardware initialization issue where NIC reconfiguration failed due to missing queue configuration pointer initialization. [fc470d5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fc470d5e88f848957b8f6d2089210254525e9e13)
- Refactored datapath update logic into queue configuration and VF configuration. [7bd2558](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7bd255833d3d1c7ce558a84117c02d0fd41ad9f4)
- Optimized virtio and vhost-user statistics counters by reducing branches, reordering packet size comparisons based on typical traffic patterns, and streamlining counting logic. [e9dac45](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e9dac45c0d3071bf3c6308a0cfbae93421ac4829) [10be332](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/10be3321d1a8ae4747950344dfa16b00db67f1a6)
- x86 EAL caches queried CPU flags to avoid re-querying hardware, speeding up repeated flag queries and providing a workaround for errors when constantly querying the AVX-512 CPU flag from a non-AVX VM. [4225db8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4225db8dc0fa83c9ba1247ac5aec54ab7f3f8d94)
- Fixed VFIO hotplug with multiprocess by moving MP sync setup registration into `rte_vfio_enable()`, ensuring secondary processes are notified when VFIO is enabled. Removes the unnecessary `rte_eal_vfio_setup()` function and calls `rte_vfio_enable()` directly. [6e18a2d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6e18a2d452b2712930338f482960235687007fd0)
- PCI bus driver retries enabling VFIO if unavailable, so DPDK applications can use VFIO-bound devices without restart when VFIO modules are loaded after initialization. [5f7b981](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5f7b98189de733080656989d63b0e7ffd249830a)
- Ensure the configuration structure is properly initialized to zero, as required by a previous change to the flags field. This addresses a missed initialization when merging the RCU reclaim feature. [19d463a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/19d463aacd5a5345365075db65deb8e9b3003654)
- **Security fix**: Race condition in ethdev telemetry endpoints fixed: telemetry commands accessed partially initialized or uninitialized port data during concurrent port add/remove. Fix reuses existing ethdev lock protecting port allocation/destruction, and uses new telemetry callback registration API with private argument. A wrapper function acquires the lock before invoking the endpoint callback and releases it afterward, ensuring safe concurrent access. [6f96937](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6f96937dada54d5cfc8def0955b0807759b45ac4)
- **Crash fixes**: Fixed a crash in the e1000 driver by preventing link status updates from secondary processes. [84506cf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/84506cfe07326fd6ddb158f3fa57bd678751561a) Added null pointer check before accessing vf_res to prevent crash when link change events occur before virtual function resource allocation. [57ed9ca](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/57ed9ca61f44ffc3801f55c749347bd717834008) Fixed unchecked return value in ixgbe_stop_mac_link_on_d3_82599; the return status from ixgbe_read_eeprom is now captured, and the link disable on D3 logic only executes when the EEPROM read is valid. [eb3684b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/eb3684b191928ebb5d263e3f8ab1e309bfec099e)
- **Other bug fixes**: Add validation checks for flow action types in the CPFL PMD to prevent errors from incorrect use of port_representor and represented_port actions. [8612619](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/86126195768418da56031305cdf3636ceb6650c8) The mask value of the `next_proto_id` field from `recipe.json` was incorrectly parsed as a string instead of an unsigned integer, causing incorrect flow offloading hint parsing. The fix interprets the mask as an unsigned integer for that field; other fields continue to use string masks. [8125fea](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8125fea74b860a71605dfe94dc03ef73c912813e) Corrected format specifiers in debug and info log messages for unsigned integer values; changed `%d` to `%u` for unsigned integers and `0x%x` for bitmask values to prevent large numbers from appearing negative. [c11665b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c11665b25f78bc6c867ce27ff4e359a87db1050e) Fixed a bug where 200G link speed was not advertised during driver initialization, even when supported by the PHY; the link speed mask was updated to include the 200G bit. [e3992ab](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e3992ab377d2879d6c5bfb220865638404b85dba) Fixed iteration issue in Preserved Fields Area (PFA) TLV parsing to prevent reading past the end when a requested TLV is not found; corrected maximum PFA offset calculation and added overflow protection using `check_add_overflow`. [dcb760b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dcb760bf0f951b404bce33a1dd14906154b58c75) Fixed mailbox ACK handling by checking the CTS bit before waiting for acknowledgment, preventing timeouts; added note that the write function should be called while holding a lock to avoid multi-threading issues. [1f119e4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1f119e4e3c36f954a353028d2ce6879b8adc8289) NVM acquire function incorrectly invoked with read-only access type for EEPROM write operation; corrected to write access, ensuring proper acquisition before writing. [c83c62f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c83c62fbf75a4ada591a5c7146d10fe1831f74a6) Fixed a bug in the E610 network driver where media type was incorrectly set to unknown when the link was down; media type is now only updated in the media type detection function. [6c4abcb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6c4abcb0f0b1d587c62dc4d0462665e4e6469b09) Disabled thermal sensor operations for E610 because its datasheet shows no support for reading current from thermal sensors. [d4a87e5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d4a87e512efa6b2e031435e687a7bf2cdd489bba) Fixes an inaccuracy in the documentation. [bf959e4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bf959e45181947a8ee1a4b23d6a32d65198b9a28)
- **Enhancements and new hardware support**: PTP initialization for E825C devices now enables only PHY 1, not PHY 0 or CGU. [b144d0c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b144d0c51782bf2e12e92fc485eba56fd07215a7) When a user changes advertised speed settings while the link is up, the driver now retrieves all speeds supported by the hardware instead of only the active PHY configuration, enabling any supported speed that may have been previously disabled. [b616e64](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b616e645ed1d1b2fd24b0012826c1aa4a6073da4) Added 5G link speed detection for E610 VF devices; previously only X550 VF was checked, now the E610 VF MAC type is also considered. [2a18945](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2a18945801bf6b523af0596454257581f3c7a992) New tag definitions for E830_GLTCLAN_TSYN_REG_RANGE_ENFORCE registers: macros for start, end, VM/VF type, VM/VF number, PF number, and enable fields, supporting E830 hardware. [638604c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/638604cc386b76bc50f926c2a527bfc31a35e5c6) E830 50G branding strings use "E830-L" instead of "E830-XXV". [93eca93](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/93eca93598431bf3fbc0e14c846f2c1b6f80a7f8) Support for Intel Ethernet Network Adapters E830. [589347f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/589347feaf9dde1e5f8fc599858e69d413a5132d) Definitions and code paths for the E610 device, including the E610 VF variant and its capabilities. [0d56299](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0d56299c3b6f13367a381838b358332a4481b275) PCI IDs for several new variants of the E610 Ethernet controller: 10G T, 2.5G T, backplane, SFP, and SGMII. [5662e97](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5662e97457eb1133ec17f6b6060b97cc9b66da41) I understand the instructions. [d725446](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d72544655ea05190f57e9100e1d533d6c7989a42)
- **Code cleanup and maintenance**: Updated variable types and removed an unused function to avoid implicit type conversions; added an author entry. [cc80bd1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cc80bd159c5d9cf7208d44633e3a35424ed2faee) Removed code that reads minimum security revision from NVM, as it was not intended for DPDK. [e0acd76](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e0acd76ad8396e521aece181040700e6da3f1c18) Removes a check only used by certain driver variants and not required by DPDK, simplifying code and eliminating unnecessary validation that could cause warnings or errors. [8e0c388](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8e0c3889de68029fa9385a2f77c27392a4e20240) Adjusts whitespace in several i40e base driver files to align with the shared code, including removing blank lines, adjusting indentation, and reordering device ID definitions. [e10fbcd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e10fbcd8973af3a781044797ce80b531049ff87c) Removed unnecessary define tags and conditional compilation blocks, including VF support now handled by the IAVF driver. [77b4bce](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/77b4bceb6b4a0a59fb8ef96cce4b7518546a92ab) Replaced "master" with "primary" in the i40e base driver. [564c9e4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/564c9e44831b7d3ce06099250562e0385cd478e3) Removed unused variables and macro definitions from the i40e driver's filter validation logic. [d63c082](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d63c0823472340db19aa1103cbfbcd94fefd00ce) Removed redundant flag setting for X722, already handled in a separate function.
