[deb7c44](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/deb7c447d088903d06a76e2c719a8207c94a576e)
- Fixed the PHC main timer for ice PMD enabled drivers to initialize to the current system time when timesync is enabled, preventing incorrect timestamps from zero initialization. [faa310a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/faa310a75f66953dda22887345dc196f8aef28de)
- Moved VF reset command from early reset to device close in the iavf driver, right before admin queue shutdown, preventing issues with admin queue messages. Also replaced hard-coded lower-level message sending with `iavf_vf_reset` from the common iavf library. [b34fe66](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b34fe66ea893c74f09322dc1109e80e81faa7d4f)
- Fixed `rte_fib_rcu_qsbr_add()` to return negative errno values instead of positive 1 with `rte_errno` set, aligning with the `rte_fib` API. [cc8764c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cc8764c6c9af150131f714bbe8d39de861d5e377)
- Fixed the GVE DQO Tx path to correctly set End of Packet (EOP) and checksum offload bits for all segments in a chained mbuf. Previously, only the last descriptor had EOP set and checksum offload applied, causing transmission stalls. Now, EOP is cleared for all but the last segment, and checksum offload is computed once per packet and applied to all segments. [21b1d72](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/21b1d725e5a6cd38fe28d83c1f6cf00d80643b31)
- The Tx/Rx queue setup/stop routines for the GVE driver's DQO format are now called during device start/stop, with conditional checks ensuring the correct DQO-specific functions are used. This fixes issues that occurred when only GQI routines were called. [7174c88](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7174c8891dcfb2a148e03c5fe2f200742b2dadbe)
- Fixed i40e DDP package loading to validate both reserved track IDs, preventing incorrect loading of packages with the other reserved ID. [f646061](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f646061cd9328f1265d8b9996c9b734ab2ce3707)
- Fixed a loop bound issue in the i40e driver by verifying that `next_to_clean` is within valid range, reading the head value only once, and checking for invalid head values to prevent out-of-bounds access. [3e61fe4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3e61fe48412f46daa66f7ccc8f03b1e7620d0b64)
- Reduced `hw_semaphore_timeout` from 64-bit to 32-bit to match the `I40E_GLVFGEN_TIMER` register. Fixed firmware write semaphore expiration by checking the sign of subtraction of two 32-bit values to account for timer wrap-around. [c61390d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c61390d94d46b05b7cd97d34561cb0b360d4e89c)
- LED blinking on X722 with X557 PHY is now always triggered, fixing an issue where LEDs would not blink under certain conditions. [bf0183e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bf0183e9ab98c946e0c7e178149e4b685465b9b1)
- `I40E_PFPM_APM`, `I40E_PFPM_WUC`, `I40E_PFPM_WUFC`, and `I40E_PFPM_WUS` registers now read bits per PF. [221a3d8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/221a3d87a690e6212f2e122fff95aa271fd22cc8)
- Repeated register dumps produce consistent results by making the register list read-only. [efc6a6b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/efc6a6b1facfa160e5e72f55893a301a6b27c628)
- Added missing device support for X710TL 1G Base-T variant, XXV710_N3000, and X722 variants to ensure proper device recognition and MAC type setting. [597e19e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/597e19e7eae17beb820795c3a8a97c547870ba26), [ef8d118](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ef8d118ad5fc38db0fa035bc1eb256843ac7abeb), [b0bad8e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b0bad8e99815d9a95614ff05cbcbd5057082b43c)
- Added missing definitions for the `GL_RXERR1` register's high part, consolidating the receive error counter into the hardware port statistics structure. Removes the manually added define and separate `rx_err1` field, replacing all usages with the new base driver stats member, aligning the driver with the hardware specification. [08f6fed](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/08f6fedee453df7390ed5623d2fe5ec5ab362f53)
- Fixed debug logs and comments in the i40e NVM driver to refer to read operations instead of write operations. [719ec1b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/719ec1bfebde956b661d403ef73ecb1e7483d50f)
- Improved code quality in the i40e base driver: added explicit typecasts to prevent compiler warnings on certain platforms, replaced `I40E_LO_DWORD` with `I40E_LO_WORD` in indirect write/read functions, and reduced time variables from 64-bit to 32-bit. [2c68a61](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2c68a61b8cba07646216ddb3bdf70c83766b1706), [cb593a8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cb593a832630a81403a9fc3e3de0bd06742e4bbb)
- Introduces defines for the "Read alternate - Indirect" command, including a canary value and buffer size constant, to enable reading the trace buffer in certain driver versions. [1f4adfe](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1f4adfeb5ff94a88efb00b742eff8e63671313b6)
- A definition for the 5th Free Provisioning Area pointer in Shadow RAM is introduced for storing Phy Firmware for X710-T2L and X710-T4L devices. [c7cb270](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c7cb270a6e66f2a2e619d8641122de8b7c66693e)
- Fixed an unchecked return value warning from static analysis tools by checking the return status of a function call. [7fb34b9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7fb34b9141aab299c2b84656ec5b12bf41f1c21d)
- Defined constant `VFINT_DYN_MAX_INDEX` as 63, aligning with iAVF 1.1's maximum of 64 interrupts, to check the maximum number of interrupts requestable from the OS. [1cc2ffd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1cc2ffd1e9d763166d0752afc7506c274d40f68b)
- Corrected AVX-512 pointer copy on 32-bit systems: pointers are 4 bytes, so copying 32 pointers now uses two AVX-512 load/store operations instead of four via conditional compilation. [2d040df](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2d040df2437a025ef6d2ecf72de96d5c9fe97439), [da97aea](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/da97aeafca4cdd40892ffb7e628bb15dcf9c0f25), [77608b2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/77608b24bdd840d323ebd9cb6ffffaf5c760983e), [d16364e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d16364e3bdbfd9e07a487bf776a829c565337e3c)
- Added support for indirect counters in tunnel offload SWITCH rules. Indirect counters can replace inline or conntrack-assisted counters. Counter handling logic now tracks implicit counters, prevents conflicts when an indirect counter is already in use by another tunnel context, and adds implicit counters when no user counter is present. [5f70136](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5f701365ca2d867467026d72581f47d3f1ca60e0)
- Documentation updates: i40e README includes snapshot generation date and copyright year; IAVF base driver documentation now includes a copyright year of 2024 and describes the code as a snapshot. [5a87641](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5a87641a443f11f1f63d4a9af64d3d0f8ac64f5a), [79c9190](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/79c919080436d5dfabb48fe96f58a9095c7fece5)
- **Security fix in HNS3 driver**: Removed base address registers for command queues and ring queues from the register dump to address security concerns [c8b7bec]. Additionally, fixed incorrect dump length by using the correct interrupt task queue count [e9b82b4], removed separators between register modules to prepare for reporting register names [7fddd3c], refactored register dump for better code organization [8cdddc2], and added ability to filter register dumps by module name [99d3bd8].
- **Breaking change: revert of memcpy redefinition**: Reverted the redefinition of memcpy as rte_memcpy, as it provided no performance benefit with current compilers and introduced bugs where rte_memcpy was detected as referencing past the destination [966764d].
- **Fixed potential undefined behavior in net/tap driver**: Added a check for data_length > 0 before memcpy to prevent null pointer calls even when length is zero, reported by GCC analyzer [3975d85].
- **PCAP network driver fix**: Configured live interfaces to non-blocking mode, aligning with DPDK's expectation that Poll Mode Drivers poll without blocking. Fixes Bugzilla ID 1526 [60dd5a7].
- **Added ARM64 architecture support to net/mana driver**: Updated the build system for ARM64 Linux compilation and included a missing header file [7649794].
- **Build system fix**: Included required libraries when checking header symbols, allowing driver to be built with custom rdma-core installations [8d7596c].
- **Statistics optimization**: Optimized statistics counters by reducing branches and reordering packet size comparisons based on typical internet traffic probabilities [84c292f].
- **Enic driver fix**: Removed unicast-only check in MAC address add callback, allowing multicast addresses as per DPDK API [c103585].
- **Flow table insertion type**: Added index with pattern mode to match on a pattern at a specific index [29e7c62].
- **Upcast fix**: Upcast uint16_t variable to uint32_t before alignment operations in descriptor count adjustment to prevent overflow and incorrect descriptor limits [30efe60].
- **BAR reservation fix**: Reserved BAR 2.0 for expansion ROM to fix PXE boot functionality during host warm restarts [d5c18ff].
- **NFP flower driver improvements**: Removed restrictions on number of queues for representor ports [64e472d] and added support for multiple transmit queues on representor ports [761e06e].
- **GVE driver fixes and improvements**: Added packet type parsing to DQ format and updated RX checksum validation to use mbuf [83e0cc5]; fixed memory leak in DQ Rx path by allocating correct number of mbufs [265daac]; ensured Rx refill function for DQO is always called to prevent lockups [31d2149].
- **DPAA bus and network driver fixes**: Fixed PFDR leak by draining FQRNI messages from Message Ring [b292acc]; fixed typecasting issue with channel ID [5edc61e]; fixed VSP initialization for 1G interfaces [2543483]; fixed incorrect bracket placement in statistics calculation [a87a1d0]; improved port cleanup to prevent segmentation faults in FMCLESS mode [e498f3b]; introduced function to check LS1043A errata A010022 for buffer reallocation [a978a7f]; fixed source location for copying data into new mbuf in reallocate_mbuf [7594caf].
- **Breaking change**: Made profile parameters constant in `rte_tm` functions for adding WRED and shaper profiles, preventing modification of input parameters and allowing reuse of the same parameter struct for multiple profiles without resetting. Improves safety and usability. [5d96356](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5d96356688da2f79a62a48025a017c77e90d6232) [3953323](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3953323852dfe399c0e6bdf2d35f88005b8a2135)
- **Security/critical fix**: Fixed a critical bug in the GVE driver's RX refill path that could cause memory corruption. In an edge case where the refill count exactly matched the remaining ring slots, the tail pointer was set to an invalid value, leading to buffer overflows and stale mbufs. The fix simplifies refill logic using a single loop with modular arithmetic, eliminating problematic conditional branches. [52c9b40](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/52c9b4069b216495d6e709bb500b6a52b8b2ca82)
- **Security/critical fix**: Added IO memory barriers before reading descriptors in the GVE driver's RX and TX DQO paths to prevent stale data reads. Barriers are placed after reading the generation bit to ensure proper ownership transfer from the NIC to the driver, avoiding data corruption or incorrect completions. [f8fee84](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f8fee84eb48cdf13a7a29f5851a2e2a41045813a)
- **Security/critical fix**: The `rte_pktmbuf_alloc_bulk` function in the net/memif driver's zero-copy receive path did not handle index wrap-around, causing buffer overflow, memory corruption, and duplicate mbufs. The fix doubles the mbuf ring allocation for a contiguous array and copies excess entries to the start. [b92b18b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b92b18b76858ed58ebe9c5dea9dedf9a99e7e0e2)
- **Security fix**: Fixed an issue where sending an unsupported command to the BSP could cause errors. The driver now checks whether the BSP supports the command before sending it. Specifically, it verifies that the BSP can report firmware load status before attempting to check if firmware is loaded, preventing issues on BSP versions that lack this command. [e57a531](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e57a531c9b681d88900cb1ba1d4119559c0e5bb9)
- **Security fix**: Added validation for the reset type received from firmware in the hns3 network driver to ensure it falls within the valid range. [3db8460](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3db846003734d38d59950ebe024ad6d61afe08f0)
- **Security fix**: Replaced `snprintf()` with `strlcpy()` in the NFP driver for safer string copying, as `strlcpy()` guarantees null termination and reduces buffer overflow risk. [cda9123](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cda9123ec16e08f2aa6476343733252512a3bf5e)
- **Stability fix**: Fixed Coverity warnings: null pointer dereferences, identical branches, resource leaks, error handling issues, and array overruns. [3de5fe7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3de5fe7996a399fcd92888172a733eed315d1d0d)
- **NFP driver fixes**: Fixed a bug where representor port link speed updates used an invalid index. Added a new function to retrieve the correct index for all device types and updated link update functions to use it. [441839f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/441839f1dbe12410de553095d599a060b8a37b25) Standardized port index retrieval in NFP driver functions by using a dedicated function instead of directly accessing the hardware structure's index field, ensuring consistency across functions for both flower and coreNIC firmware. [c33504d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c33504de83eedf218ba4ed3ca75a1ca377346ec2) Representor port link status now uses the stored value from the port structure instead of reading from the control BAR, ensuring correct link state reporting. [d95cf21](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d95cf21d2ed6630d21b5b1ca4abc40155720cd3f) Renamed flower service flag for code readability. [14eb71f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/14eb71f6293687e2abcabef7e2987dc9bcb394ae)
- **Testpmd SSE optimizations**: Optimized SSE MAC swap function by declaring SIMD variables with the register keyword, improving single queue performance by ~1 Mpps. [8001e1c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8001e1c8f7fecdeb2bb9193a3b7acdb870546c0d) Moved offload flag update to beginning of loop in SSE MAC swap processing, reducing cache evictions and improving RX packet throughput. [222effc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/222effc6ec29cafc7f23d4f6141b8ba2bd384531) Interleaving SSE SIMD load, shuffle, and store operations reduces pipeline stalls and increases MAC swap throughput. On AMD EPYC 9554 with Broadcom P2100 NIC, the interleaved version achieves 46.0 Mpps ingress and 45.1 Mpps egress, up from 45.75 and 43.8. [1b307e5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1b307e535643c94be10f2dadca8de354bb2def6d)
- **Tap driver improvements**: Restricts maximum MP file descriptors to TAP device's queue count instead of global maximum, preventing issues when global maximum exceeds queue capacity. [288649a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/288649a11a8a332727f2a988c676ff7dfd1bc4c5) Compile-time check ensures tap queues do not exceed file descriptor limit when passing file descriptors between primary and secondary processes. [039aded](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/039aded84451df5a2b90035474ed309569c236e2) Increased maximum queues from 16 to 64 for larger systems. [6a2e47a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6a2e47a3e26acb5ec206918c7f6454ee4aefa138)
- **Configuration restoration refactoring**: Extracted promiscuous and all multicast configuration restoration into separate functions, simplifying code and enabling easier integration of disabling these procedures for supporting PMDs. [a98bd0f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a98bd0fe444c3cdbce7d40cd5545abb38aca9469) Conditionally restores configuration based on flags from new internal API `get_restore_flags()`. Previously, MAC address, promiscuous mode, and allmulticast were restored unconditionally; now each feature is gated by specific flags, improving efficiency and flexibility. [e14ebec](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e14ebecf109ef8ffca62e173ad8147e928f54e9e) mlx5 PMD no longer requires configuration restoration when starting a device. Added `mlx5_get_restore_flags()` to `eth_dev_ops`, returning zero to indicate no restore needed. Simplifies device start and avoids unnecessary operations. [6a3446c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6a3446cf577b70edbb072be19c7466b006ee2aa2)
- **Other fixes**: Corrected type declarations in link speed conversion function: return type and parameter type updated to `size_t` and `uint32_t`, loop variable changed to `size_t`. [93ebb1e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/93ebb1e57e3ff5ce34168058d73a82ae206255cc) Renamed PF speed update function for readability; replaced hard-coded value with macro. [1b3b12c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1b3b12c4a89c12fdf33196af1cf80f3f71d7bf8a) Converted string initializations to array initializations to avoid warnings about unterminated string initialization when initializing arrays of unsigned char. [e0d947a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e0d947a1e6c2f80aa039a4f7082a8aa16797d8b9) Fixed code style. [a1c2c9d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a1c2c9db7cfeaeb6925141428b4d356c4bdb9f6f) Removed redundant call to `rte_eth_dev_release_port` from the device close function, as it is already called after `rte_eth_dev_close`. [7fa6075](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7fa6075dee49bab1441d64f75cecf9647f66100b) Added a check to verify the queue ID in the `rte_eth_tx_done_cleanup` API when the `RTE_ETHDEV_DEBUG_TX` flag is enabled, ensuring the queue ID is valid for the specified port. [707f50c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/707f50cef003a89f8fc5170c2ca5aea808cf4297)
- **Breaking change**: The internal symbol `rte_vdpa_relay_vring_used()` has been removed from the public API. [85dbfcb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/85dbfcb1be49e512fde5c7455f9872158421ca66)
- The checkpatches.sh script now forbids `__builtin_ffs` and `__builtin_ffsll`, which are unavailable in MSVC, in addition to previously detected builtins. Developers should use portable alternatives from `<rte_bitops.h>`. [65c6733](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/65c6733a679eb4cc3b6dc00af75bc65280422775)
- Replaced `__builtin_ffsll()` with `rte_bsf64()` for MSVC compatibility, as `rte_bsf64()` is portable across compilers. [5f3cd04](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5f3cd043a8e115353902b8b5d76ec0bb1928a2f5)
- **NFP driver fixes**: Fixed multiple return value handling issues: link state change functions now properly check for negative return values [0ca4f21](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0ca4f216b89162ce8142d665a98924bdf4a23a6e); `nfp_eth_set_configured()` return value is now checked when bringing ports up/down [1580387](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1580387e07cf0facf695db2b8bc23f1238810c59); pause frame setting check corrected to only treat negative returns as errors [4bb6de5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4bb6de512fbc361e16d5a7a38b704735c831540d); FEC setting logic now properly checks and logs errors [47fc5e4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/47fc5e4ee99bd8efa587ac6c6e7966318de4da1c).
- **Ice driver scheduler fixes**: Removed implicit 255 limit on child nodes by changing datatype to 16-bit [aff0693](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/aff06930682ca3f243d91bba3104c4af8169c38a); fixed VSI index inheritance for new nodes [d0c63c7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d0c63c7f0fcaedae4537910590020fe59210dbb1); changed subtree search direction to upward node validation [4376d9f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4376d9fd19f332dbabf11548fe59cda296d53a75); removed flag checks restricting topology support [5ac957b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5ac957beedfbb12749aa4cbdd76f386bd0687f94); supports initialization when DCB is disabled [55250a2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/55250a2d5b5041fb28e4d87ba20f43364c04cc72); reads VSI layer information from VSI node structure [d4ee640](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d4ee6403b58dab6935661f8b99736707fe7d108c); please provide the release note entry you'd like me to rewrite. [8ca6d8c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8ca6d8c48b73c8337bbaaf18f0458777c916c188)
- **Mlx5 driver fixes**: Fixed double removal of Rx queues from control list when closing port with shared and non-shared queues [f957ac9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f957ac99643535fd218753f4f956fc9c5aadd23c); fixed NVGRE item validation in template API [0025fd4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0025fd47f2a01997bbb41d04139b220b143e6b31); fixed full SQ detection using actual queue size instead of fixed macro [b56ba21](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b56ba2139f4dc04b97f69f0d0ece1f28725a100b); fixed use-after-free when destroying flow rules [045da18](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/045da18ec955f4ab5afe7697454965d40d9289a1); fixed matcher mask translation using DV item translation [0250832](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/02508320543df75a4dfabad83adfcd3353600c61).
- Fixed comments in control message structures to match data field definitions. [1a6c589](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1a6c58929d683bf237836583db1320fc3ddc12d6)
- Memory leak in VF initialization fixed by correcting memory allocation and adding proper error handling. [9fa4d03](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9fa4d03f746d84d8ecbb3ffb2b19f110cf79baae)
- Removed redundant queue count validation from driver configuration logic; the upper layer already ensures requested queues are within limits. [9439fee](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9439fee9187069d43536f676b566ab0b68645502)
- Fixed a blocking issue in the pcap network driver's receive function by replacing `pcap_next` (which always blocks when no packets are available) with `pcap_next_ex` (which returns immediately if no packets are present). [f5ead8f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f5ead8f84f205babb320a1d805fb436ba31a5532)
- Experimental inline bit manipulation helpers now conditionally call other experimental API only when explicitly allowed, preventing deprecation warnings. [8b65ddc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8b65ddc0522ef6d8134edbcfd05bcd7d4f748d19)
- Added bounds check to ensure control queue values are within range. [9378aa4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9378aa47f45fa5cd5be219c8eb770f096e8a4c27)
- Only the last VLAN passed traffic after a PF reset when multiple VLANs were defined. The VLAN replay process incorrectly cleared the vsi_map bitmap during iteration, breaking subsequent replays. The fix removes that clearing, allowing all VLANs to be replayed correctly. [8e191a6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8e191a67df2d217c2cbd96325b38bf2f5f028f03)
- MAC address of an iavf port managed by an i40e PF driver is lost upon re-probing due to two upstream Linux kernel changes. The fix adds the VIRTCHNL_VF_OFFLOAD_USO capability to preserve the MAC address during resource queries, and stops removing MAC addresses during device stop and error recovery, aligning with the kernel driver behavior. [3d42086](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3d42086def307be853d1e2e5b9d1e76725c3661f)
- **Breaking change**: Removed the 'flow flex init' command, a leftover debug command with no production functionality. [d5c5039](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d5c50397a1cc06419970afbea9cd1c37e3c08a5b)
- **Security fix**: Fixed improper initialization of the reverse polynomial for an LFSR in the hash library, which could have caused incorrect bit sequences, by implementing a proper polynomial reversing function. [ebf7f11](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ebf7f1188ea83d6154746e90d535392113ecb1e8)
- Soft reset of the NFP device now clears both uncorrectable and correctable error status registers in the PCIe AER register, preventing false error reporting from device memory access. [c4ced2d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c4ced2d58a542980fdbbbf3095ad6571e3e6ba14)
- Fixed a false alarm error log when destroying non-template flow rules sharing the same encapsulation action. The error occurred because the return value from the hlist unregister function was incorrectly checked; it returns 1 if the object is still referenced, not an error. The fix removes the return value check for encap, decap, modify header, and matcher resource release during flow destroy, preventing spurious error messages. [7493f6f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7493f6f8a38bc89ce53032e15018474d8a83bab1)
- Fixed flex item handling in MLX5 PMD to correctly set item flags when the flex item represents a tunnel header. Tunnel mode is detected and the appropriate flag (FLEX_TUNNEL, INNER_FLEX, or OUTER_FLEX) is assigned during item array processing, enabling proper matching for inner and outer items. [624ca89](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/624ca89b57550f13c49224d931d391680dc62d69), [e46b266](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e46b26663de964b54ed9fc2e7eade07261d8e396)
- Corrected driver limit from 4 to 8 flex parser configurations to match hardware, preventing errors when creating parse objects. [16d8f37](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/16d8f37b4ebb59a2b2d48dbd9c0f3b8302d4ab1f)
- When a flex item is encountered, the unknown next protocol caused validation failures for combinations like flex followed by ESP. The fix sets the next protocol to 0xff to allow successful validation. [3847a3b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3847a3b192315491118eab9830e695eb2c9946e2)
- Fixed incorrect endianness in field masks and non-byte-aligned shifts for pattern masks and values, which caused matches to be ignored or missed for flex items not covering the full 32-bit word or not aligned on byte boundaries. [97e19f0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/97e19f0762e5235d6914845a59823d4ea36925bb)
- Header length field description in FIELD_MODE_OFFSET mode: parameter checks, prevention of crossing dword boundaries, 6-bit mask width, and adjustment of mask left margin offset to avoid dword offset. BITMASK mode shift limit check enforced; redundant code omitted. [b04b06f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b04b06f4cb3f3bdd24228f3ca2ec5b3a7b64308d)
- Missing errno settings when ipool allocation fails are fixed. rte_errno is set to ENOMEM when mlx5_ipool_malloc fails to allocate an ID. Error handling for meter mark allocation is improved, including proper cleanup and error reporting. [e4a4087](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e4a40879922e4685bbbbf3503d6b388e2ee11044)
- Log messages now include reasons for flow rule creation failures, such as rule destruction during creation, missing user data, full queue, invalid rule status, matcher in resize, and unsupported matcher. [0573a39](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0573a3928d6239822628a81efa5d0f90ee30f4e0)
- Log CQE error syndrome and additional information (vendor error, opcode, queue pair number, WQE counter) when a queue error occurs in the mlx5 hardware steering (HWS) driver to improve debugging. [d9acab1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d9acab175085754eec705463bafa7b39b1a88e22)
- Fixed an issue where the flower firmware was not notified about PF speed, causing VF speed to be unavailable. The fix adds logic to notify the firmware when the link is updated for a PF representor. [2254813](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2254813795099aa6c05caed5e8c0dcc7a8f03b4e)
- Do not set IPv6 flag in transport mode for IPsec security sessions. Only the security protocol header is encapsulated, so the IPv6 flag is unnecessary. Clear source and destination IP addresses without setting the IPv6 flag, fixing an issue from a previous commit. [4f64ebd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4f64ebdd41ce8bb60dba95589a5cc684fb9cb89c)
- Implemented eth_link_update to update device link status using the socket's IFF_RUNNING flag, enabling applications to detect link up/down changes. [dcb035b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dcb035b0ed26c9b38d569f16310dd9326680926c)
- Modified NFP driver's Rx buffer size setting logic. Previously, hardware Rx buffer size was updated per queue, causing interference and potential packet reception issues. Now, after first initialization, buffer size only increases if a new Rx queue requires a larger size, preventing unintended interference and improving reliability. [b5fae3a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b5fae3a560c39b1a9a09c40dbeec2086f12ac53a)
- Removed newline characters from debug log messages in the fmlib base driver. [6be4899](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6be4899c951e85595c66cb13b13a229ec1268e45)
- Added cross-compilation targets for building 32-bit x86 DPDK on Fedora/RHEL, Arch Linux, and improved the build process to use cross-compilation files instead of environment variables, ensuring paths are saved across meson reconfigurations and preventing rebuild failures. [c3096d2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c3096d218f66708cdad2bc43699e08677b83e8dc), [b6105ba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b6105ba40a467f5c3463505cf210986273d1031f), [b7d0c73](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b7d0c73851aef89fb64867243015b80a138fa758)
- The device_error_handling_mode field in the testpmd port is optional, defaulting to None when absent. [618d914](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/618d9140e5efd9c59a8143b95ac6007961d4db7d)
- Port information caching in testpmd shell: the ports property returns cached results, updated by calling show_port_info_all() or show_port_info(). [85d15c7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/85d15c7c93c8f0afaba08584834047d7df0f2c79)
- Hugepage mounting commands now require admin privileges for proper unmounting and re-mounting. [224c1f0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/224c1f0ebb91f94bc462f4d3757fe2051d515ac1)
- Corrected inline markup, blank lines, indentation, and code block formatting in DTS framework docstrings to fix Sphinx documentation errors. [1e4f185](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1e4f18558427c0aa876851d72609b302c2f6b224)
- The or operator in third-party types has been replaced with Union to prevent errors when building DTS API documentation without dependencies. [4227446](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4227446306a0b97826d587ef6f0d7ecc054af781)
- Set 'package-mode = false' in DTS pyproject.toml, replacing '--no-root' in poetry install commands to prevent future errors. [72a44b2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/72a44b260f0f4f824aa473b30b8d97d84512c081)
- Fixed PCI address formatting errors in configuration comments. [5c135ce](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5c135ce8cbd22b484cea8c1e7194b6f1e4841b9a)
- Added `~/.local/bin` to PATH, replaced a deprecated flag with the current method, and changed CMD to ENTRYPOINT in the Dockerfile runner target. [4701af1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4701af16df123dc23986d5d443fe3d2858efd2a6)
- Replaced system() call with standard file I/O operations to write CPU affinity masks, eliminating security issues. [126cc1b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/126cc1b2b44a3d2967943e1ef995b9e20f9e5021)
- Updated IPv6-related structures to use `rte_ipv6_addr` instead of `uint8_t[16]` arrays: `tcp6_flow_key`, `rte_flow_tunnel`, `rte_flow_action_set_ipv6`, `rte_flow_item_icmp6_nd_na`, and `rte_flow_item_icmp6_nd_na`. Added dedicated structure members for version, traffic class, and flow label fields in the IPv6 header definition, replacing the combined `vtc_flow` member (preserved for backward compatibility). Fixed a compilation error from using `memcmp` on the new IPv6 address structure by replacing with `rte_ipv6_addr_is_unspec`. [41d71ae](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/41d71aeb56694f06d60f0de20e5ac2d718ea5328), [cc13675](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cc13675026303f1da82551deee89027cda3d7aef), [cba2799](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cba27998dc8124f0d816f6342dff7451b51fe5e0), [61938a2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/61938a2d178554a0605f8d7ec2e5b7eeaea20e43)
- Replaced typedefs for integer and network types with conditional `#define` macros to prevent type collisions. [f0d9e78](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f0d9e787747dda0715654da9f0501f54fe105868)
- Changed argparse library's flag definitions from enum to macros using `RTE_SHIFT_VAL64` and `RTE_BIT64` to fix 64-bit vs int mismatch. Fixed argument flags size by correcting `uint32_t` operations to `uint64_t`. Clarified error messages, refined test cases, and fixed double hyphens in argument names rendered as single hyphens in HTML output. [9b8df29](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9b8df29bf15575f2f0570e6f1e68294a399f6917), [bd0d68b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bd0d68bdc36d4422176e72d49eccfe88f167e3d9), [f48e4ee](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f48e4eed4aebba5b61565fbf8515fd723b53cd0c), [9684dfe](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9684dfeae3284ec93a56090e653fb74886d5a40e), [51a639c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/51a639ca804234462df0a8c72523fa71181cea24)
- Improved alignment handling by removing explicit 2-byte alignment from a header type and removing packing from another structure; added `static_asserts` to verify size and alignment at compile time. [e214d58](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e214d58eb87b8af488272f084220617ecabb510a)
- Reworked packet addressing in the DTS test suite: `_adjust_addresses` now only modifies addresses if not configured, returns copies. Exposed functionality to send packets without capturing, allowing multiple packets at once. Replaced XML-RPC server with a Python shell in the traffic generator; ScapyTrafficGenerator extends PythonShell and CapturingTrafficGenerator. DTS supports distinct instances for enum members with the same value. Made Sphinx configuration for DTS documentation optional when Read The Docs theme is unavailable. [1a18259](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1a1825962777c33c5fe6b3a547e1e61168906541), [2e69387](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2e69387a656396a7382fe27d8d9f61f1b5890573), [9974030](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/99740300890620065d06277811c81b33cd302d39), [d8a5cb4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d8a5cb4e5c19c6a00a8f9b4cff623b394b0f1fd9), [c523e86](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c523e8643b60f39eda3864af022a84203d962a94)
- Added `--noiommu-mode` option to enable unsafe no-IOMMU mode for VFIO drivers when IOMMU is unavailable. [6ca0f1c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6ca0f1c8450d6e28926cad33c6500ad487f4cfdb)
- Fixed null pointer access in `rte_dma_vchan_status` when status pointer is NULL. Moved assignment of device pointer after validity check for `dev_id` in several dmadev functions. [e5389d4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e5389d427ec43ab805d0a1caed89b63656fd7fde), [2980a27](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2980a27ecfaf2ea3a990cfa38cc310221a1c9b97)
- Enabled cast qualifier warnings in the power library; resolved the sole const cast instance with a `uintptr_t` intermediate cast. [74efd38](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/74efd38b416fcf9eae2a5b429b81b550a73a47b6)
- Corrected lcore-to-CPU ID mapping when using the `--lcores` EAL option; power management now restricted to lcores mapping to a single physical core. [5c9b07e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5c9b07eeba55d527025f1f4945e2dbb366f21215)
- Replaced a direct cast with an inline union variable in the mbuf allocator to resolve a strict aliasing warning and prevent undefined behavior. [6011b12](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6011b12f52b60565d4dfcc3b382551ec1f53b3d4)
- Added scale and zero point fields to the I/O information structure in the ML device library to share recommended scale factor and zero point for quantization. [fe8eba6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fe8eba692c59a92c9308f1fe429b101b9f2377bf)
- Parameter validation in `rte_hash_create()` now checks each parameter individually with descriptive error messages. [bf26e6f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bf26e6f4019b7ab29e2ff8263effd3695d415ef4)
- Removed dead code by eliminating an unreachable NULL callback check. [66e57df](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/66e57df338d1e7a503220e4a3c3f2b92998f3f23)
- Set GRPCFG traffic class to 1 via a macro that configures TC-A and TC-B bits, improving performance on current generation accelerators. [aa8ed90](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/aa8ed903d29ec90ced2e9dbcf1132d098de3b6f7)
- Pallavi Kadam has stepped down as maintainer for Windows support in DPDK. [7040360](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7040360ba9e2ada1ed48eeea112e01702f32b8df)
- Added a bounds check to the mvneta driver's ifnames parsing function to prevent an out-of-bounds write when too many iface pairs are provided, logging an error and returning an error code. [c705c67](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c705c67d304b9450824a169b652520c2358c6aee)
- AF_PACKET socket creation now uses protocol 0 instead of ETH_P_ALL, preventing immediate binding to any interface. A single bind call sets both interface and protocol, making the process safer and faster. [5b81eac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5b81eac5fd6f8035a2d8fdd3863eb789f77de164)
- Fixed a bug where the number of Tx queues could exceed Rx queues, causing a segfault when sending packets on a Tx queue with an index beyond the Rx queue array. Now, for every Tx queue, an Rx queue is created with an event buffer for Tx completion messages, but without allocating mbuf pool or Rx ring. RSS configuration prevents packets from being received on these additional Rx queues. Added validation to prevent RSS from directing traffic to invalid Rx queues. [e900205](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e90020535c03cf9e60448ba623cac3301f111dae)
- Fixed a bug on FreeBSD that forced a 10-second wait for link status checks even when the API caller requested no waiting. The forced wait flag was removed, allowing immediate checks when the caller does not require waiting. [f775386](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f775386d92d68e534600fcff3fc4bcaa30d3e68c)
- Fixed an issue where double-stopping a flow director queue during port shutdown caused an error. Setting the tail-register pointer to NULL on the first stop and checking that condition on subsequent stops eliminates the error and allows normal queue restart. [7b230d4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7b230d43e8061bdaba02a41f601bb8e0b5dbff03)
- Fixed a bug where a premature return prevented proper cleanup on initialization failure. Error handling now sets an error code and proceeds to the exit label to ensure resources are released. [283c97c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/283c97cffd7f58ce13ce59feface5ee94aa2acc8)
- Restricts tunnel flow rules to a single tunnel header. The device's flow director supports only one; if the rte-flow API passes more than one, the driver returns an error. Ensures compliance with hardware limitations and prevents invalid configurations. [8887c20](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8887c207b9373a1875031c5346706f698322d66d)
- The ice driver now limits queues per VSI by querying scheduler tree node capabilities, multiplying max_children for each scheduling layer beyond the VSI to compute a cap that prevents overflowing child scheduling nodes. Queue mapping uses this cap to ensure queues do not exceed hardware scheduler limits. [5117ebf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5117ebfcc0d52bdf13eb5e04997293287fe69f1f)
- Added return value check for reconfiguration function call. Previously ignored, now logs error and exits on failure. [8c1d15f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8c1d15f1c44f2deeb62fadf32c8b4ea7390964d3)
- Fixed invalid log format characters in the i40e driver's base code by changing format specifiers from PRIu64 to PRIu32 to match the reduced variable size, resolving build warnings. [ba90329](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ba90329a5eb31234c65d5bbef68ff5c88318445b)
- PMDs can now retrieve the raw device structure pointer from a device ID via an internal API, returning the pointer only if the device is attached. [3ee7a3e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3ee7a3e0e0e0f5a81a4b102a834697bc488fb32f)
- Updated bitset to conditionally compile functions using experimental API features, ensuring compatibility with GCC builds without the experimental API. [a3e126f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a3e126fd58d11aee85220480f4bf692612fbadc2)
- The header checking tool now verifies that exported headers are usable without experimental or internal API flags. Separate executables are built for stable API only, with experimental API, and with both, ensuring applications using only stable API can include headers without issues. [e8752e3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e8752e311a3d32d08c3fb04cc9a0262fbaa05b31)
- Driver header for Intel FPGA bus now uses C linkage guards for C++ compatibility. [706fb9b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/706fb9b3c6190990126fb5262accbe87a77e5790)
- Checks driver-specific headers for C++ compatibility, validating all exported headers. [90cb8ff](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/90cb8ff8196f9b9c1c2bcee1c94ea583789bb63f)
- Fixed the C++ linkage for ethdev symbols, making them available when linking with C++ objects. [20387eb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/20387ebce20ac5bc2e4e26bd008129a6c2cad9cc)
- General tunnel flow matching using the PTYPE item with PTYPE_TUNNEL (0xf000) is supported. The hardware's tunnel packet metadata bit allows matching any supported tunnel type, replacing the need for separate rules per tunnel type (VXLAN, VXLAN-GPE, GENEVE, NVGRE) over IPv4 and IPv6. A single rule can now match all tunnel packets, or combine with IPv4/IPv6 for specific IP tunnel packets. [90294fa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/90294fa5bc3ab8d0592bbde190dcc8b614850aad)
- The TX scheduler topology dump in the ice driver now recursively traverses from the root node, including all scheduler nodes. It skips middle children when there are more than 16 to keep graphs viewable, uses dot clustering for non-zero VSIs, and displays queue numbers for leaf nodes assigned to NIC TX queues. [824e68d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/824e68d4c060cc8c491e6134e764c285fa62126f)
- The ixgbe driver supports per-queue statistics with fewer queues than the maximum, removing the requirement to match the maximum. [2de2328](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2de23282c17878ffdab7fac276c2c18b608d114d)
- Enhanced CNXK net driver to handle multiple events via common mailbox for representor events. Added support for link state events and improved MTU event processing by dynamically determining representor ID from hardware function instead of passed rep_id, enabling correct processing for representors not yet created. [03b8c84](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/03b8c847479e22cf5e955bf1dff4759c4e563b44)
- Removed a bouncing email address from the hns3 driver maintainers list. [cff6dba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cff6dba7ff8521df410b40399fd8aca5b450a05b)
- Adds official maintainer names for several Intel NIC drivers. [cc1687b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cc1687b880004314b384661a1844d09e442d64a4)
- Praveen Shetty added as official maintainer for Intel IDPF and CPFL drivers. [a875eb8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a875eb89b9188dc2a5ba94798a2714e69b165758)
- Firmware support for the CPFL driver is now documented. [d006f93](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d006f936d5dac3b8a17bf1a3f24766f1783cf561)
- Reduced log level of NFP driver messages from error/warning to debug for unsupported features that do not affect functionality. [1a92d15](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1a92d15d749626f7334c19434e3e4d93f58cc037)
- Changed log level for speed update failure from WARNING to DEBUG, as the failure is acceptable and recovers once link status changes. [17d6720](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/17d6720e0477de1ee786ac9a03f689cd9e311d98)
- Log messages in the NFP driver now use full forms like "can not" and "does not" instead of abbreviations for consistency and clarity. [fb86136](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fb86136d92516499a65aa2299d575ea2da7b40d7)
- Corrected misspellings in log messages, changing "Cloud" to "Could" in error messages. [5232ed0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5232ed0f653d75e85c7712f370a29941b3175698)
- **Breaking change**: Removed single event enqueue and dequeue functions from multiple event device drivers (DLB2, OcteonTX, skeleton, and others), retaining only burst variants for improved performance and consistency. Applications using the single event API must migrate to burst operations. [dd1d439](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dd1d4398795aeaaa32b66889b64650e940d7204f), [e20e214](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e20e2148cf9268fa16ad6d0baff943a3eaae5bf0), [3cdcc0c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3cdcc0c17c6f62c4355b3adcc3191db8e7546d52), [88ca872](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/88ca872150d0b61b4e6ffcb96f5cecc9e781adb5), [e1b07dd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e1b07dd581cb487e1138e60c21159c499173352e), [813ab18](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/813ab18d5753d6bf78ec614aecc0b6bd583aab1f), [8b565b3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8b565b3445b67567a459d48e64ba5700320fc852), [5079ede](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5079ede71edeed44c6c25e9ceffcd342940b309f)
- **Security fix**: Resolved a time-of-check time-of-use (TOCTOU) vulnerability in VDUSE device creation by opening the device file first, then verifying its existence, preventing a race condition. Also fixed a file descriptor leak in reconnect code, improved error handling in reconnection log validation, and refactored reconnection log mapping to simplify device creation and cleanup. [47458d1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/47458d13d44af88f0bb3af0a327a7b06784f8480), [29ab97d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/29ab97dd8316b6ecbb1753202b87ace1c854d445), [952e494](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/952e49451600bea61214249815f241167c7c456d), [69d2e25](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/69d2e2567b04833dbb1e92f572d6d97943faee9c), [a2a05e5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a2a05e55396435db4fd06f91a829175fba885fa3)
- Fixed a double free where flow aging resources were cleaned up twice: once when the last flow with aging action was removed, and again during tear down. The fix adds a check to ensure the aging control thread is only destroyed if it hasn't already exited. [d1066ea](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d1066ea60bcb5cbd3cdcc06d21afc232d8c08407)
- Fixed data corruption in VxLAN packets by properly handling segment data in eswitch multi-segment support. [cfd5db0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cfd5db0dcdb9005a3b82c628f0ebd8677ff85ec6)
- Appends a mark ID action to flows for represented port actions to enable flow identification. [c2f3e9e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c2f3e9e76f39fc925e4f3ce7b2d0551a38a17f74)
- Dump details of a single flow. Previously, this resulted in an error; now, providing a flow handle dumps the flow's details. [abdd7a9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/abdd7a9d6be5fa1ad44cf3eecd71e812b8bffbcb)
- LDPC decoder input now saturated to 6 bits by default, improving robustness. [e71eb4f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e71eb4f7bf3e2550015914661406ff4324c0c5f8)
- Fixes missing event device stop after test_eventdev_preschedule_configure test case. Cleanup function was set to NULL instead of eventdev_stop_device, leaving device running. [b74f298](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b74f298f9bfaba19527153098546fa1011c100a1)
- Getwork write data updated during device reconfiguration to prevent stale configuration data. [6dad0bb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6dad0bb5c8621644beca86ff5f4910a943ba604d)
- Replaced the unaligned uint64_t variable used to copy 8 bytes from the IP header to the fragmentation key with a constant-sized memcpy. This avoids a compiler warning with `-Waddress-of-packed-member` and allows the compiler to choose optimal instructions for the assignment. [5763d24](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5763d240624df6e3fd4e93a9f32b3408c7774951)
- Removed unnecessary packed attributes from efd library structures, `rte_trtcm_data` structure and its substructures, and `struct rte_afu_device`, as their layouts are already naturally aligned. This allows compilation without a specific compiler flag, improving code cleanliness and portability. [1887f91](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1887f919549de2821b0eeabe808a5f2b76370c34), [eca9f4f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/eca9f4f830bfd36c615427dd71d782146d34892b), [8f99903](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8f999036e16d03d1cc704cf97f5de5b2751d8d49)
- Replaced `find_program` with an absolute path using `files()` and the Python3 variable, fixing a warning during native ARM compilation and simplifying the build configuration. [c349556](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c3495563c5e13be8baf150196645bb944230c489)
- Maximum lcore limit for AMD EPYC Zen5 processors set to 768 for target/cross builds. [892bf8c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/892bf8cf7ea1591e71f398657c2d4a29cd316d06)
- Added a helper function that verifies the returned lcore ID is valid, addressing Coverity false positives, and replaces direct calls to the lcore retrieval function in parallel test code. [46ce151](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/46ce151ce49d2e44155436adad5e39359db2889e)
- Use imported VDUSE uAPI headers instead of system headers. VDUSE support is now always built on Linux, removing conditional compilation. [9fec3f0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9fec3f0569087de06666129c7f2badaf5be2776e)
- rte_lcore_register_usage_cb API is stable, enabling applications to register a callback for lcore usage statistics. [9625d8d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9625d8dbd9248490026088a7f861c3fc03e4b139)
- The memzone segments configuration API for setting and getting the maximum number of memzones is stable. [40e6cf9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/40e6cf97d3a785645f075bc40a051502f935881d)
- Supports multiple file patterns for skipping files during forbidden token checks. [129f38c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/129f38c5b7ab4428ed94d088e3d3ef6a81807b55)
- Warn against using any `__builtin` helpers, with exceptions for EAL and some driver base code. [8fa925f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8fa925fa206ef02de104cd4707db482731cf7e76)
- Updated prefetch macros in common/dpaax library to use EAL macros. [2204658](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2204658fa80698f17698626293d2cee2b706f2e0)
- **Breaking change**: Reduces data types of `log_max_qp_sz`, `log_max_cq_sz`, `log_max_qp`, and `log_max_cq` fields in the HCA attribute structure from `int` to `uint8_t`. [ec17aa6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ec17aa6a129531601f740ff4551a367e863e97e0)
- **Security fix**: 3DES-CTR mode: replaced unconditional byte swapping with proper big-endian to CPU conversion, avoided unaligned memory accesses, and removed an unused helper function. Correct operation on big-endian CPUs. [97afd07](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/97afd07ca79c7270480a65febd7f616a4c0b07ca)
- **Critical fix**: The mlx5 driver now reports the actual maximum number of Rx and Tx descriptors from the hardware's work queue size limit instead of a fixed value of 65535, which caused integer overflow and zero ring size when allocating queues. Validation rejects requests exceeding the supported size. [4c3d796](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4c3d7961d9002bb715a8ee76bcf464d633316d4c)
- **Critical fix**: Fixed a bug where only the first burst was moved to the done list when multiple bursts completed in a single completion. Now all completed bursts are moved by iterating over the wait queue, appending each to the done list, and removing them from the wait queue. [d672152](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d67215274063cb853b6c64e218a8b6a8025adc19)
- **Critical fix**: Fixed a memory leak in the net/mlx5 driver's metering functionality. The meter profile table was always allocated during device spawn but not freed when metering was disabled. Now, the table is allocated only when metering is enabled, and the profile lookup function checks for its existence before access. [4dd46d3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4dd46d38820e0bf5e74f99b84f4b098d1b7220dd)
- **Critical fix**: Fixed a bug in the mlx5 driver where GRE flow item translation for root tables did not properly initialize the item mask when using HWS. The issue occurred because the reused DV code did not set the mask. The fix ensures correct mask initialization for proper GRE flow item translation. [25ab2cb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/25ab2cbba31d937e685f0cf9ecce0c680cc4083e)
- **Critical fix**: Correctly maps IPv6 Traffic Class (TC) to IPv4 Type of Service (TOS) in NAT64. The 8-bit TC field was incorrectly split; now TC[abcdefgh] maps to TOS[DSCP=abcdef, ECN=gh], preserving QoS information. [d3e4699](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d3e46998443e47e48bea30e116c6330bfdf5302c)
- **Critical fix**: SWS meter state initialization now correctly monitors ASO object availability via the PMD's meter 'state' variable, ensuring accurate ASO resource management and fixing a previous sync meter processing issue in HWS. [0c37d8f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0c37d8f7ba2cac289896de024d9c58a65ba3ece9)
- **Critical fix**: Adds validation to reject HWS meter operations before port configuration. Checks ensure the HWS context exists before allowing meter operations in HWS mode, preventing issues from accessing an uninitialized context. Affects operations like capability get, profile get/add, policy validate/get/add, meter create, enable, and profile update. [61a8106](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/61a810617ec864aa30b36d7aaffc0bda4cc28f54)
- **Critical fix**: Real time counter reading from PCI BAR: moved mapping to initialization and ensured atomic read of 64-bit counter. [27918f0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/27918f0d53f482fa97f2a8dcd5792c23094abcec)
- **Critical fix**: Checks the return value of `rte_pcapng_add_interface`. On failure (e.g., out of file space), the application exits with an error message indicating which interface caused the failure. [c79900e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c79900e31e3e5a16c7f5410d0800315a0491ccad)
- **Critical fix**: During range definer creation, set rte_errno to EINVAL on invalid matcher range definition to notify the caller. [84c3090](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/84c3090e517641027a7b64fe5bb6eccbcfa05a6d)
- **Critical fix**: Reports counter action initialization failures via `rte_flow_error` structure instead of integer values like -1 or errno, providing more detailed error information to upper layers. [d46f3b5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d46f3b525aafbb4c6c88d9c61b445eb0d93d2149)
- **Critical fix**: Expanded SQ flow item struct to 64 bits on 64-bit systems for memory alignment and to prevent buffer overflows during DPDK copies. [7c66fa4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7c66fa49ddcce1981c2fa3a0c024ec82b036639c)
- **Critical fix**: mlx5 net driver restricts HWS actions template validation to template API setups, avoiding errors in non-template API usage where action masks are absent. [ee76b17](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ee76b173b2e93ab4a0c9b4153191965259dae972)
- **Critical fix**: Support for the BlueField firmware configuration option `NUM_OF_PF=0`, which disables the PF on the x86 host side. This removes the HPF representor on the ARM side, leaving only the bonding device (PF0) as the RDMA port. It enables probing a PF device without associated switch ports, allowing proper device probing with representor devargs like `pf[0-1]vf[...]` on systems using this configuration. [6d1f439](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6d1f4393349fc98397b409af89642c4a2aa3ee19)
- **Critical fix**: Tx tracing in mlx5 now uses a single clock source (NIC HW clock) instead of mixing two unrelated clocks, ensuring timestamps from a common, externally synchronizable clock for improved consistency and accuracy. [0293248](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/02932480ae82d7ed3c207f02cc40b508cdda6ded)
- **Critical fix**: Added -a to dump all bursts (including incomplete) and -v [level] to dump raw records data for specified level and below. Shows incomplete Tx transfers previously lost before hardware completions, and outputs raw data for bursts, WQEs, and mbufs based on verbose level. [171360d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/171360df9f89a17f8b4177f01f11fa4473c74099)
- **Refactoring**: Updated several drivers to use EAL branch prediction macros (likely/unlikely) instead of __builtin_expect, improving code consistency and leveraging the DPDK abstraction layer. [6d73669](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6d736695a7467eddd6feeef0be78e1a3b511610c)
- **Refactoring**: The drivers use the DPDK bitops API instead of compiler builtins for bit operations. [191128d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/191128d7f6a02b816deaa86d761fbde4483724e9)
- **Refactoring**: Replaced direct use of a compiler builtin with DPDK bitops API function rte_clz64, aligning with DPDK best practices. [2a682d6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2a682d65f8dbe1e8be9cc2425095827e18c700e3)
- **Refactoring**: Control flow rules in the NIC Rx domain created by the HWS flow engine now have extended type enumeration with new types for unicast DMAC and unicast DMAC with VLAN flow rules, enabling future verification of control flow rule existence. [a977e2b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a977e2b5b2768e74019ccab52943b7c0d3c30470)
- **Refactoring**: Support for destroying unicast DMAC control flow rules and unicast DMAC with VLAN control flow rules when using the HWS flow engine, without affecting other control flow rules. [04ea846](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/04ea84684aa296659dac15892abadfa4ef083faa)
- **Refactoring**: Renames structs and enumerations used for HWS control flow rule management to remove the "hw" prefix, as they do not depend on HWS. This enables reuse with Verbs and DV flow engines and allows fine-grained creation and destruction of unicast DMAC (with VLAN) flow rules. Specifically, enum `mlx5_hw_ctrl_flow_type` becomes `mlx5_ctrl_flow_type`, its prefix changes from `MLX5_HW_CTRL_FLOW_TYPE_` to `MLX5_CTRL_FLOW_TYPE_`, struct `mlx5_hw_ctrl_flow_info` becomes `mlx5_ctrl_flow_info`, and struct `mlx5_hw_ctrl_flow` becomes `mlx5_ctrl_flow_entry`. [d6708a9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d6708a9d29e48595202a816df1aedd082003550c)
- **Refactoring**: Initialization of control flow rules lists and lock moved to a common location, enabling reuse across all flow engines. [80a5af9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/80a5af9f2adb23f1f13c34014f84abbf45abcadc)
- Fixed an out-of-bounds read in the IPv6 address mask function (reported by Coverity) by replacing memset with a loop that iterates over remaining bytes, preventing index increment after masking a byte. [1d9c6bb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1d9c6bbeb6cd8d0e3b7d54c7732199524020ea23)
- Fixed heap corruption on Windows caused by freeing `_aligned_malloc()` memory with `free()` instead of `_aligned_free()`. [9ebdbe6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9ebdbe62c2aaae8f71851483139b3b4dcfaf991b)
- Changed variable types from uint8_t to unsigned int in IPv6 address utility functions to avoid implicit downcast overflow, and replaced magic number 8 with CHAR_BIT for consistency. [b805c83](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b805c834c776c67fb52d2b84c74258c5323a7872)
- Updated getopt, getopt_long, and getopt_long_only on Windows to align with FreeBSD: function signatures now use `char *const argv[]` and `optarg` changed from `const char*` to `char*`, ensuring consistent prototypes across platforms and reducing workarounds. [a06fb0f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a06fb0fae6ab6a79fa3e8288dbb0c5166be843c2)
- The maximum size of per-lcore variables in DPDK has been reduced from 1 MB to 128 KB to address out-of-memory (OOM) issues during parallel unit tests. The previous 1 MB per lcore caused excessive memory allocation (128 MB total on x86 with default build options). The largest current user, rte_service, requires only 2112 bytes, making the new limit more reasonable. [f2fd6c2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f2fd6c2e080c0c595bcc72d8c05d9e1014d398e2)
- DMA copy and scatter-gather copy operations now return the index of the last descriptor copied on success instead of 0. Requests with zero source entries are rejected with -EINVAL. [dcb9be8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dcb9be853be46e1bdb78911f26e62ebf793844f9)
- Raw encapsulation validation in the mlx5 driver is fixed by separating checks for software steering (SWS) and hardware steering (HWS), where SWS requires both size and data parameters and HWS validates only size. [db830c4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/db830c40bc8296fc013d9328255192fb589d858a)
- The indirect list flow action callback is now correctly invoked with the specific flow action instead of the actions list. [e53e4c3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e53e4c39d2514667a7065cb0dd2d8fe3dcd843e3)
- Support for an array STE matcher in the mlx5 driver for template tables when using insertion by index with a pattern. This isolates the table from others in the same group, so packets missing the rule proceed with the default miss. Modifies table creation logic to set the matcher's isolation and match mode based on the insertion type. [e87a9b6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e87a9b609fb1eea5ebc67eca3eb379beba967e7f)
- Fixed a memory leak in the node clone function by freeing allocated memory when xstats memory allocation fails. [5e65cd4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5e65cd4a722ec7f277a70447db318953252bf6f9)
- QBMAN DQ storage logic enhanced to support multiple cores by replacing a single storage union with an array of per-core storages, preventing memory leaks when multiple storages are allocated. Macros introduced for allocating and freeing per-core DQ storage; drivers updated to use them. Memory allocation changed to use zero-initialized memory; freed pointers set to NULL to avoid dangling references. [12d98ec](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/12d98eceb8ac89d6284a2a56f9b83cca40b73e80)
- Fixed compilation error with -Wcast-align in IPv4 checksum simple function by aligning rte_ipv4_hdr to two bytes and casting from structure start instead of first field. [c14fba6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c14fba68edfa4aeba7c0dfb5dbc3b4f23affbb81)
- IPv6 header structures are aligned to two bytes, matching the natural alignment of IPv6 headers. [365b7f3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/365b7f341ca633743d44c365eccff7ae729d37c2)
- Replaced static arrays of cache-aligned structs with lcore variables for PRNG, power library, and power intrinsics state, improving cache locality and reducing false sharing. [29c39cd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/29c39cd3d54d8330ee578dd3ea27cfda1c562079), [1306433](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/13064331957930f6b6c49ad02a638d7d5516c88f), [b24bbae](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b24bbaedbba2df6ad2c25bc0bbde52fb55876fdb), [18b5049](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/18b5049ab4fecda6ad303606cc265d923b56da14)
- Logging subsystem improvements: removed duplicate initialization failure logging, improved rte_exit() output format, initialized logging earlier in startup, added a hook for custom log printing, and added Stephen Hemminger as maintainer. [72bf6da](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/72bf6da85a657dc4dd0662f1cd854dcc0da6da07), [c4e03ac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c4e03aca4a241fb7bbd7754801604171412c5a0f), [2773d39](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2773d39ffee46f66fd628cebdd401d89fce09f1f), [630cdfc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/630cdfcd69c90dcdf54fcf6be05f73fd1ac1e0a9), [84c91d9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/84c91d93da7a0e537f95cf17dde026564f35a3b7)
- Removed unused callback array from interface structure, saving significant memory from a large per-queue allocation. [9bbd44d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9bbd44d63846cf0771ec0f1c7e1b5a63ec5e9603)
- Added support for matching IP Encapsulating Security Payload (ESP) packet types in transport mode. [a371119](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a371119084b81f77400fa3aed061d570cfc0eefe)
- Derive maximum descriptor number from hardware bit width for DPAA2 QDMA driver, and add assertion to validate job index does not exceed it. [b52af62](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b52af62f825c3c41bbafae603c9ccfcda77ffbd1)
- Implemented workaround for ERR050757 on LS104x platforms: enables stride mode for qDMA operations to prevent hangs from PCIe controller buffer size limitation, avoiding dropped completions that block outbound reads. [bdcb782](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bdcb782a460108c894798ae6a1e04dd1df94c29e)
- Added software workaround for Errata-A050265 to fix QDMA stall from unaligned transactions. Adjusts prefetchable read setting in source descriptors; configure based on target memory type to balance performance and stability. [8c53b9b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8c53b9b7954d6beb16d2497a28005a7d9c5bb1c8)
- **Security fixes**: Fixed read beyond boundaries in the l3fwd example's `send_packets_multi()` function by allocating extra space and adding validation for destination port entries. [ebab0e8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ebab0e8b2257aa049dd35dedc7efd230b0f45b88), [795b634](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/795b63416b96aac4358a0b01f59d83797b94e522)  
- **Stability fixes**: Added IOVA validation before sending parameters to the Management Complex (MC) to prevent system hangs; applied in flow distribution, queue configuration, and soft parser loading. [25d0ae6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/25d0ae6242453c3e482d752495d5a30f3ada11dd)  
- Fixed a resource leak in the fslmc bus driver by properly closing the VFIO group file descriptor when adding a VFIO group fails. [3b5f8df](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3b5f8dfab7e85c180cc64e130c33cee4b5d43c28)  
- Fixed memory corruption in the DPAA2 net driver's traffic management by correcting the queue array size from a fixed 8 to the actual number of TX queues. [d77cb0c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d77cb0c44cf6b68dc71684bd302fd3138b36e5f1)  
- **Breaking change**: Marked `dpcon_close` API as internal symbol and added to version map file. [1eb72b9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1eb72b977b2cf2d82bcf116c241f03f286b5663f)  
- **Link status and flow control improvements**: Updated duplex value along with link status and link speed when setting link UP; improved error handling by returning actual error codes instead of generic -EINVAL or -1; changed log levels for link up/down messages from INFO to DEBUG. [25dd1fd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/25dd1fd045ed230f7d6388be4044ebf16912da3a)  
- Fixed an issue where link status incorrectly showed DOWN after connecting an SFP module and fiber cable if flow control was configured while the module was disconnected; replaced `dpni_get_link_state` with `dpni_get_link_cfg` to use static configuration data. [263377b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/263377be771f09a20197c30ce83ea44922a4e8fe)  
- **MTU handling**: Added debug info for max-pkt-len and configured parameters, stored MTU in device data structure; changed a log message from INFO to DEBUG, added error log when configured MTU exceeds max packet length, and ensured MTU is stored during device initialization and MTU set operations. [de08b47](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/de08b47438a75afb6f4246d7fed10a2460797b82)  
- **DPDMUX enhancements**: Introduced a function to dump DPDMUX counters, including ingress and egress frame, byte, discard, multicast, and broadcast statistics. [17eda10](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/17eda10df93e1db1344819c19832533d777e48d1)  
- Improved DPDMUX error behavior settings for compatibility with MC v10.36 or later. [00e928e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/00e928e9704c3794062b46c1c30352281e4f9cb8)  
- Enabled VLAN traffic splitting in DPDMUX by allowing rules based on VLAN IDs. [4160359](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4160359077073d148557297ac8c6c7b94ea148f9)  
- **New API and error handling**: New experimental API `rte_pmd_dpaa2_dev_is_dpaa2` to check if an Ethernet port is a DPAA2 device; error handling in flow control settings improved to return proper error codes instead of -1; flow redirect function updated to use the new API. [72cd5a4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/72cd5a480180457369d1cd369da664c4ebc7cad7)  
- **BMAN buffer acquire**: Improved by ignoring reserved bits in the response number; new mask (`BMAN_VALID_RSLT_NUM_MASK`) extracts only valid bits (0-2). [a116979](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a116979a03c6873ad3c72422175ac0325f51c846)  
- **VFIO group handling**: Obtains the VFIO group file descriptor directly from the file system instead of using the RTE VFIO API, avoiding conflicts with PCIe VFIO; new function opens the group FD, handling primary and secondary processes via multiprocess communication. [0e603d8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0e603d80cea6d546e3c0d283a5d751eab8eaa662)  
- **Management command safety**: Introduced intermediate pointer variables to safely store management command completion results before dereferencing, and added a type cast to prevent potential overflow in a calculation. [051f418](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/051f4185f98faa964b6a965b2e8e7b2da68969de)  
- **QBMAN cleanup**: Renamed local variable in QBMAN enqueue function to avoid redefinition. [98cfbbb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/98cfbbbe0f2bacdaffb12faadb984610055efc09)  
- **Macro and default values**: Removed macro `DPNI_FS_MISS_DROP` to avoid enum conflict, set default miss flow ID to 0, and added uint16_t cast when reading from environment variable. [068be45](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/068be45fb5363dc9f79821a133f13d8bd781d26d)  
- **Flow matching support**: Added GTP flow matching support in the DPAA2 network driver, enabling configuration of GTP flows for RSS and flow steering; added new GTP pattern type and mask. [146c745](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/146c745e308825bdd4280f17506a3ff92eb9c0a2)  
- Support for soft parser flow patterns in the DPAA2 net driver; existing supported pattern list renamed for hard parser; new list for soft parser patterns (VXLAN and ECPRI) introduced; validation logic checks both lists. [1cf6d18](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1cf6d181b58f83bd54c444499ec95380eb4b1c38)  
- **Packet prioritization**: The drop priority is now extracted from the frame descriptor and stored in the mbuf's hash scheduling color field, enabling packet prioritization. [7994a12](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7994a12c4eb9a6bfe05c9c7c8de1d8d0bb427293)  
- **Power management**: Power management operations are now consistently supported across all operating environments, eliminating the need for function pointer checks; these checks have been removed from applications. [b21cf7d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b21cf7d3b67b67e4c37803d8ff54839c6e6bdfd5)  
- **Parsing safety**: Replaced `parse_int()` with `parse_uint()`, which includes a maximum value parameter and returns the result via a pointer, preventing overflow. [0bc4795](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0bc4795d5994459a3d261afd7f843eb0cabdecf5)  
- **Bus scan fix**: Fixed fslmc bus scan to not return an error when the bus is unavailable on non-DPAA2 platforms; now proceeds to a failure handling path instead. [b472c50](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b472c50aeee1ca4f3a86be0580d31474653dfd8f)  
- **Gitignore**: Hidden tracked files are explicitly included in the .gitignore file to ensure their changes are visible. [58ae3be](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/58ae3be1445b9f60d7595dc8729dbb4b839a4bcf)
- **Breaking change**: Applications must check the return value of ethdev query functions that return data structures, as the contents are undefined on error. A compiler attribute warns if the return value is ignored. [1ff8b9a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1ff8b9a6ef248dddebd07a8df7b47f4de9ffab62)
- **Breaking change**: Forces TX VLAN offload on 802.1Q packets. The VSP assumes no VLAN tags, so the driver strips the tag and uses the PPI to send VLAN information through the VSP packet, ensuring proper handling of VLAN-tagged packets and maintaining compatibility with the VSP. [06c968f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/06c968f9ba8afeaf03b60871a453652a5828ff3f)
- **Breaking change**: Removed support for two ROH device IDs from the hns3 network driver. These devices are no longer available, so the corresponding entries are reverted to ensure the driver only supports currently available hardware. [feb4548](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/feb4548ffd80bf249239d99bf9053ecf78f815d1)
- Added return value checks for ethdev query functions (`rte_eth_dev_info_get`, `rte_eth_link_get`, `rte_eth_stats_get`, `rte_eth_macaddr_get`) across multiple examples and drivers. Previously unchecked calls could lead to undefined data; now failures return an error, ensuring only valid device information is displayed. Includes: [419daaa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/419daaa2794ca380db2b1267c62c2d5de516b1b3), [a4fa02e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a4fa02e06046d36c6a7340201571397d2f59a682), [fe02b98](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fe02b98cd3925d455731f0201030c587a387eef0), [7d04227](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7d04227433ede0e3fdab1319cadafa46cc28266d), [a937954](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a937954e3d1ccfcc88aad472b0e6ee67f3eb560c), [07e4dc0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/07e4dc04d99a99699d71a0a39dd2a7034049e663), [d0974e0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d0974e07f42e12626ee78cba0d285090de40d149), [6c5e32c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6c5e32c71220a69ee079284814a26d8ad29dabe4), [69559d0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/69559d0df94779b2d76f991831390400e33237fe)
- Fixes a crash in the vmxnet3 driver when configuration fails before the shared data structure is allocated. The `adapter_stopped` flag was not initialized to `TRUE` in the device initialization function, causing a segmentation fault when the close function attempts to stop the device. The fix sets `adapter_stopped` to `TRUE` during initialization to prevent stop processing from accessing unallocated shared data. [439847c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/439847c154ccf05e1a8bbb955c552921514d31e2)
- Changed variable types from `uint16_t` to `uint32_t` to fix integer overflow and prevent data truncation when accessing TQP registers, correcting handling of register offsets and counts. [b1fefe4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b1fefe40550836b58c4ec50dce14a6e6dbda8499)
- Fixed incorrect register values in hns3 network driver when retrieving multiple modules due to wrong pointer offset. Updated pointer offset of data field to correct addressing, resolving issue introduced in previous commit that added support for reporting register names. [013fdd2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/013fdd2d7b319e6a35d966f375e33ee330d9ccb5)
- Fixed incorrect use of logical AND instead of bitwise AND in hns3 driver error log, causing inaccurate messages when firmware register numbers mismatched driver's list. [f58fd22](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f58fd22240c27ea20cf41dd2aa15810712f518bf)
- Replaced `__rte_aligned(8)` with `__rte_packed` in virtual queue structures to fix alignment. [f2a3bf9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f2a3bf9ef7b33ca001fa9204d29eac2f718b01ce)
- A validation check for Rx packet descriptor consistency was added to the ENA driver. The reset logic now handles error conditions—including no space, fault, and I/O errors—each triggering an appropriate reset reason. New statistics counters for bad descriptors and unknown errors were introduced, and error reporting in stats was updated to include these counters. [7a16699](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7a166990faa46a470f16e7c96404c9288f2e1a86)
- ntnic driver now supports `RTE_FLOW_ITEM_TYPE_VOID` for use in flow rules. [57fa1d4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/57fa1d4ddb504f2f98ad318c20fb269c49105738)
- Adds register definitions for the CSU module, including control and data registers, integrated into FPGA module initialization. [bbd8b39](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bbd8b3901bcee48827933169e2c20649953c6724)
- Replaces `rte_memcpy` with `rte_ether_addr_copy` for copying Ethernet MAC addresses in MAC address setting and device creation. [94bdbf1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/94bdbf14af734b667e549c0806105e1b9262c967)
- Renamed the net/tap driver's internal netlink message structure from `nlmsg` to `tap_nlmsg` to avoid confusion with the standard `nlmsghdr` from netlink.h. [b424101](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b4241019d426114fe7adb4da892053a1dbf51261)
- Flow conversion functions now use a typed pointer (`struct convert_data *info`) instead of a void pointer, improving type safety and code clarity. [9abf92e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9abf92e01cb68b6178730433a2dd16bd5637f55c)
- Removed unnecessary type casting of `dev_private` from `void*` to `struct pmd_internals*` and replaced the compile-time `FLOW_DEBUG` macro with dynamic logging for runtime control of debug output. [e0d9b3c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e0d9b3cd6d3eec6f84ca481c8af29e36d07fbb79)
- Extracted helper functions for allocating PF and VF representor ports in the NFP driver, improving code organization and reusability. Also revised the number of PF representor ports based on multi-PF status, and control messages related to PF representors are now ignored when no PF representor port exists. Includes: [5f22bc0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5f22bc0a00bf6a5769169cbddbd5c325364258c2), [edcf048](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/edcf04893876949db915a4b662ff31adda491fa7), [619ea0b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/619ea0b6a5489de8ae9b5c45634dca78ca79cb56), [99da56d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/99da56de8c8402c38ca9985c7dd881ae33fd4a19)
- **Breaking change**: Removed outer UDP checksum offload support for tunnel packets in the txgbe driver (hardware does not support it), and removed the `RTE_ETH_TX_OFFLOAD_OUTER_UDP_CKSUM` capability flag for accurate hardware capability reporting. [25fe1c7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/25fe1c780d39ea3637ba8407f6e9a9800135becd)
- **Security fixes**:
  - Replaced `rte_memcpy` with `strlcpy` when copying the algorithm name in the OpenSSL crypto driver to fix a potential string overflow, addressing a clang/ASAN warning. [c5819b0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c5819b0d96d1a24c25aa4324913fd2566eb19ae9)
  - Fixed a potential integer overflow in `ssovf_parsekv()` where an unsigned char pointer was incorrectly accessed as an integer. The argument is now accessed as a char, and `strtol` replaces `atoi` for improved error handling, returning an error for invalid values. [3e86eee](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3e86eee028c69b98144e2c62ec48091467e790be)
  - Increased stats array sizes and enforced per-queue counter limits to fix potential out-of-bounds access in queue statistics. [d3a229d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d3a229dd493abcb29d5717c5ce37e0a0bc1777c4)
- **Tx error handling improvements**:
  - Enables Tx descriptor error interrupt handling in the txgbe driver. Adds TDM descriptor error checking to prevent hardware PCIe pending due to Tx hang. Non-fatal errors are handled by clearing the error and resetting the queue; fatal errors require manual port restart. Fixes layer length mismatches in TX descriptors to avoid false non-fatal errors when TDM descriptor checking is enabled. [0eabdfc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0eabdfcd4af44fd8b32ccdeb3d01c256572a52d0)
  - Added Tx packet length checking to prevent TDM fatal errors. Invalid packets are either set to a default length or dropped. [8d75bf0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8d75bf037aa29c865ce7ce1c891519ec5118f9df)
  - Assign a default length of 1518 bytes to packets with insufficient length in the simple Tx path; in the featured Tx path, a new function checks packet length and segment consistency and frees invalid packets.
