Similar length checks added to vectorized Tx functions. [7029832](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7029832c24f051032798c1cfaeb137ab886db094)
  - Added Tx descriptor error statistics to the net/txgbe driver, counting packets not sent due to Tx descriptor errors. A new field in the transmit queue structure is incremented when a packet is dropped or a queue reset occurs due to such errors. Statistics are accumulated and reset appropriately, improving visibility into transmission errors. [e028ec1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e028ec1b84c64891932d1b79c46d1ed41be1f77d)
  - The ngbe driver now counts packets not sent due to Tx descriptor errors per transmit queue, includes them in output error statistics, and resets the counter when statistics are reset. [3eba2f2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3eba2f2888a31dc275d37203c9d03e23604822ea)
- **Driver load bit reconfiguration after reset**:
  - The Drv_load bit in the TXGBE_PORTCTL register, reset to 0 after a hardware LAN reset, is now reconfigured after reset to inform the firmware of driver load, and cleared on device close to return control to firmware. [0a8f064](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0a8f064bbc2cf4978857eae84e86c6b2c9e65feb)
  - The driver load bit is reconfigured after hardware LAN reset to signal driver load, and cleared when the device is closed. [cb7be5b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cb7be5b510ef0995fa171832f0e0994f667e2161)
- **Device close and resource release**: Device close operation for ZXDH network driver releases resources (interrupts, PCI reset, mbufs, queues, BAR message channel, MAC addresses) for clean shutdown. [27ed58d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/27ed58d320be21f3a0a725194a0701b5b5d1c176)
- **Register and interrupt fixes**:
  - Fixed a bit definition error in the TXGBE_ICRMISC_VFMBX register to ensure proper handling of the VF-PF mailbox interrupt. [5a4ce69](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5a4ce69701fc01f23a2769c8afff055d87eff864)
  - Fixed MAC Rx registers to prevent packet reception failure after link status changes. [b8d52e1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b8d52e1084a17c7ef83624f3bbd11a090e7b2267)
  - Reads shared interrupt status and re-enables interrupts when the adapter is not stopped, preventing interrupt loss in legacy or MSI mode. [68f04c0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/68f04c0aa79316de333441e7efdadd2876412ffa)
- **VLAN and ring configuration**: The Rx ring config register cannot be written when the Rx ring is enabled. VLAN strip offload configuration in the ngbe driver is now restricted to when the device is started, preventing the ring from getting stuck under traffic. The driver checks if the adapter is stopped before allowing VLAN strip configuration. [baca8ec](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/baca8ec066dc6fdc42374e8eafd67eecfd6c9267)
- **Representor fixes**:
  - Fixed port index assignment in representor initialization and allocation for PHY, PF, and VF. [fbbfcc1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fbbfcc19e3acb5cbd0026d71399f084f4d55aeeb)
  - Introduced a helper function to check if a network representor is a physical port, replacing repeated direct type comparisons to improve code readability and maintainability. [2e3a514](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2e3a514376abc530d3f99cb7be5bd10392b619d4)
- **Memory and allocation fixes**:
  - The net/tap driver uses standard `malloc()` instead of `rte_malloc()` for process private data, improving tool coverage and caching performance. [93afd6c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/93afd6caefb7f575ed607283dcfe217bbb097294)
  - Fixed ring memory allocation for 64 MB aligned end addresses. [0c57098](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0c5709824b531e83b36ed91852cea98b1cb292e1)
- **Driver capability and configuration**:
  - vmxnet3 driver's maximum MTU adapts to virtual hardware capabilities, supporting larger MTU values when available. [a4b83a7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a4b83a747d7d21c6d61b9ae69d39db5e1c700dcd)
  - The IOVA field in mbuf is now configurable on CNXK platforms. When enabled, a warning advises disabling it to save mbuf space. Forced disable flags have been removed from the platform configuration, and documentation recommends setting `enable_iova_as_pa=false`. [592fdee](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/592fdee47dbb9b8c1141a5d679eeab4f2861731f)
  - Enables extensible bucket table feature for `rte_hash` in hns3 driver, allowing full utilization of hardware flow director table even with hash conflicts. [b8e60c3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b8e60c33168a2999604c17322dd0198a6746428f)
- **Crypto driver fixes**:
  - Added IPv6 UDP encapsulation support to the DPAA2 SEC crypto driver for NAT-T traversal in IPSEC ESP offload mode. Introduced a default NAT-T port (4500) and restructured code to handle UDP encapsulation for both IPv4 and IPv6 tunnels, including header setup and decap options. [3a38852](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3a3885276124a26cce507a6bf0a8f2f54de25ad0)
- **Miscellaneous fixes**:
  - The error code for creating a counter with an existing ID but a different indirect flag has been fixed. `ENOTSUP` is now replaced with `EINVAL` to correctly indicate an invalid argument. [585f1f6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/585f1f68f18c7acbc4f920053cbf4ba888e0c271)
  - MAC address initialization changed from string to array. [09158ba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/09158ba4cb0cefbadf45be08fa0cd587714d8813)
  - VXLAN flow matching initialization in net/dpaa2 driver now uses brace-enclosed list for VNI byte array, fixing GCC 15 build warning. [11f84bf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/11f84bf4eab350517ddd59498ae488562e3ccc23)
  - Added `__rte_assume` hints to suppress compiler warnings about array bounds when `RTE_MAX_QUEUES_PER_PORT` is set to 1. [084d0cd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/084d0cdb572f87ec6a52d32f2f7890fd337ddfba)
  - Consolidated duplicate debug log code into a common function and removed unnecessary newline characters. [662811c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/662811cba41f86af94e029230725c985a1eedb20)
- Removed dead AFM code no longer supported with TCAM manager and truflow, and eliminated unused references to AFM allocated memory. [4545fdf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4545fdf6a25f1d2d7c269eb46c200a5526028a5e)
- Guest channel API relocated to kvm_vm driver to fix build issue. [b462f27](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b462f2737eb08b07b84da4204fbd1c9b9ba00b2d)
- Updated to 24.11-rc2. [c5df7a3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c5df7a373ad07413d9652479272cd16adf989b06)
### Security & Critical Fixes
- Fixed hardware timestamping bug: received packets were 16 bytes longer because timestamp length was not subtracted from data length. In scatter-gather mode, only the first mbuf had a timestamp; subsequent mbufs had incorrect data offsets, causing missing packet data. The fix adjusts data length and offset accordingly. [4e08d33](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4e08d335554ec6d975ded8a7badf81e0edb39234)
- Fixed data corruption in the bnxt driver's TCAM manager by resetting the maximum entries per session to zero on session initialization, preventing stale data from affecting new sessions. [0bee506](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0bee506e9ec6bca60111b63e631c84080b14aec2)
- Fixed a bug in Truflow core where the slice number could be corrupted during HA entry moves because the source entry size was not copied to the destination before calling get/set message APIs. The fix ensures the destination entry size is set from the source before the move, preserving the correct slice number. [1190f2f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1190f2f8d5abf82c843ad071ad4c7d0aea202cce)
- ICE PMD: Added ready bitmap check before reading PHY timestamp to prevent incorrect Tx timestamping. The fix includes a timeout mechanism to ensure the timestamp is read only when the ready bit is set. [b55051d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b55051d1c59d8670fd59423b5af529936cf5554d)
- Fixed traffic forwarding to physical ports in the CPFL PMD, ensuring the represented_port action properly forwards traffic to physical IO ports. [b0e6aff](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b0e6aff62efa4bcc23f64b80b91709bef73e6d79)
- Send WC TCAM set messages over DMA for proper multi-slice delete handling (HWRM request buffers are read-only). Tunnel allocation APIs now provide error messages for better diagnostics. [78dcdb8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/78dcdb821cb81f4abddfd4abc0192e238d4bfcec)
### Crypto & Security Driver Fixes
- Tests that use enqueue/dequeue burst functions are now skipped in the crypto test suite when using the synchronous API, fixing incorrect calls to burst functions unavailable in synchronous mode. [251fdc5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/251fdc592da5eddc4d84a95d0c151b0134504a32)
- Correctly assign result length for modexp and modinv operations in QAT asymmetric crypto PMD modular algorithms. [5b2fe7e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5b2fe7ef3c1b731f086d9454262a530a082b0441)
- Key initialization for EC sessions now properly allocates memory and copies the private key and public key coordinates into the session. The `session_set_ec` function returns an integer to indicate success or failure, and the session configuration function checks this return value. [20e633b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/20e633b0ca15539b682539a665e8d3dc0dc2c899)
- Replaces GCC built-in __atomic_xxx intrinsics with the rte_atomic_xxx API in the event/cnxk driver, covering atomic loads, stores, fetch-and-add, fetch-and-sub, fetch-and-and, compare-and-exchange, and thread fences with appropriate memory orderings. [01f87d5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/01f87d55c7b0b61c10cc8ea22079f15d77790a91)
### Network Driver Fixes (Intel)
- The ice driver's traffic management hierarchy commit function automatically stops the port if started, applies the topology, and restarts it, instead of returning an error. [6412a8f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6412a8f741e81145479d0ea264e28db55d5a5eac)
- Increased the ice driver's maximum Rx/Tx descriptor count from 4096 to 8192 minus 32, per the datasheet maximum, to allow applications to buffer more packets and better handle processing peaks. [a378cbf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a378cbf017403b4526125e8d9d1b102059874bd1)
- The maximum number of Rx/Tx descriptors for the ixgbe driver has been increased from 4096 to 8192, as validated by the datasheet. [9fd2193](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9fd2193e56a55c6509477f8d5751c18a388f3977)
- Empty DDP search path no longer causes the driver to search for DDP packages under "/". Only valid non-empty paths (length > 0) are used. [14d66da](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/14d66da59b4a29f23f34315bf51616f5e90b16ad)
- Added missing queue interrupt mapping checks, fixing interrupt mode on E610 devices. [42e7a15](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/42e7a159f0d959f2c9e41b0b76ad281124125327)
- Made ice_alloc_lan_q_ctx non-static for external calls. [31f2a1d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/31f2a1d9460cd76733bc2ca990f2afabece9947d)
### Network Driver Fixes (Broadcom)
- Redirect flows to wildcard TCAM when exact match table insertion fails, improving flow scale. [8a2e845](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8a2e845d735aa7379de77d51026b6b580ba05078)
- Converted recipe table allocation from static to dynamic memory allocation. [4a925aa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4a925aa7bf1b622b96da193eab5ebac9943490be)
- Added action read and clear support in bnxt/tf_ulp driver, resetting flow query count after reading in ULP and updating CLI commands. Fixed MPC message padding to multiples of 16 bytes. [8499b45](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8499b456145375f3f93c04b1bf6a783ab29542c3)
- Enabled recipe ID generation and fixed a segfault in the wildcard recipe process. [ab2e230](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ab2e230e7563cbed2a708e4d1d994e937d9f7cc5)
### Build & Compilation Fixes
- Removed unnecessary warning disable flags for format errors and simplified the list to one per line, reducing compiler warning suppression to only necessary ones. [67bc46d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/67bc46de788f964af6864fe792f127aacd8c5bb5)
- The `-Wno-unused-value` compiler warning flag is removed from the list of disabled warnings, simplifying the build configuration and reducing unnecessary warning suppression. [e565dbb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e565dbbd3e86af41eb455f587fab2aa63124240d)
- Unnecessary warning disable flags removed from the build configuration. [c881a1d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c881a1d983337a12d24354ec7d10fd7b1a42a037)
- Removed the no-strict-aliasing warning flag from the iavf build configuration. [3dbd0c5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3dbd0c5dc7819919f4a3617d0ea4ac964aedc482)
- Reduces the number of compiler warning disable flags in the driver code to only three: unused parameter, unused value, and implicit fallthrough. The other flags have been removed. [ac69af6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ac69af6d1d043c937fc2b283e34cbe6dd8a72b7c)
- Removed unnecessary warning disable flags; added a compiler check to apply the remaining flag only when supported, improving portability and build safety. [914bffb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/914bffbd112127c11af7bdada236d551242e6911)
### Documentation & Maintenance
- Updated the PTP client example and documentation to replace "master" and "slave" with "timeTransmitter" and "timeReceiver" per the IEEE 1588g-2022 amendment. [b8d1d60](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b8d1d60fadc414321221843f3277445d8156c202)
- The maintainer for the Intel DLB2 driver has been updated from Abdullah Sevincer to Pravin Pathak. [78383e9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/78383e9816a8efe2ba16ec2ce65d51b94e6114e7)
- Fixes incorrect template macros used for dual workslot functions by correcting the prefix to ensure proper functionality. [49a841e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/49a841e1983acfaefdfe19957166d985ad921867)
- Added missing newline at end of file for proper formatting. [402cbd4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/402cbd45beb1428bcac3a438eb77a9c5fa5b4237)
- Fixed segfault in bnxt/tf_ulp driver by generating 64-bit recipe IDs instead of 8-bit, using default_non_ha resource when HA disabled, moving debug message to debug level for non-representor ports, and adding a bit allocator for recipe ID management. [61a7ca1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/61a7ca1f6f874286e6787b52c867f1bb25f4cd42)
- Fixed infinite loop at startup by initializing a variable to ensure the loop condition can become false. [a47272b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a47272b052dd1c8c571a1c0b89b56aaa3ebf4351)
- Fixed a spinlock handling issue where a lock was not released in an error path, potentially causing a deadlock (detected by PVS-Studio). [1f41dea](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1f41deac447d7938198a2acdd1b7862161feef91)
- Added a check to prevent array underflow or overflow when the number of interrupts is zero. [952b24b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/952b24bd0475450e548d4aafae7d8cf48258402b)
- Added validation in the transmit path: ensures the number of segments matches the mbuf count, validates buf_iova and buf_addr, and frees bad mbufs while updating the oerrors counter, preventing invalid descriptors, firmware resets, and Tx pipeline stalls. [a4fd911](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a4fd911f47d71757f10d6a3abacf49388cfdd656), [6f896ab](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6f896ab33991de71af234a9653d1b862f763a94a), [6cc5dfa](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6cc5dfa69a0335849fc0903d3ada943acb33c7ce)
- The bnxt driver enforces a minimum TSO segment size of 4; packets with a smaller segment size are dropped when TSO is requested. [9151adf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9151adf2b9eb901faf8f090ad4e612f5f20e36f5)
- Fixes TCP and UDP checksum flags for LSO capable packets in the bnxt driver by explicitly setting the TCP/UDP and IP checksum flags in the transmit descriptor for LSO packets, ensuring correct checksum computation on older chip variants for both tunnel and non-tunnel packets. [4c04511](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4c0451197e5a88531c30398b58b7e5601be90080)
- The high part of an action table entry offset in the Tx BD was not set correctly, causing the PDCU to abort a fetch and stall the pipeline; both low and high parts are now properly masked and assigned. [b019ddf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b019ddf9b1de65491b4c07c25bbab3dc70c15f79)
- Ensured proper locking for parent-child counters until stats are retrieved, maintained reference counts for F1 flows due to multiple flows per tunnel, and resolved name conflicts for class and action tables by appending port IDs to hash table names. [8782e4d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8782e4de3ef2e55bd4aed98dc18e26d2bfc83868)
- Registers for RSS change events and updates VNIC QCAPS flags accordingly, preventing upstream drivers from sending unsupported RSS configurations that would cause firmware failures; also fixes a bug where the QCAPS update code only set new flags without clearing previously cleared ones. [643e7ee](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/643e7ee39879083ec8a9b86c2b60ca9f50182ed7)
- LRO offload for P7 devices is now only exported when compressed Rx CQE mode is disabled, as TPA is unsupported in that mode. [019c181](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/019c181687371f24196f437b648dc80f67519b72)
- On P7 platforms with TruFlow enabled, VLAN filtering and stripping are disabled; the VLAN offload set operation returns early, and advertised offload capabilities exclude VLAN filter, extend, and strip. [2c03331](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2c033311ec730dfbe519e059c0c53373e5133e8a)
- Removes the VNIC async event handler; the driver now uses SVIF instead of VNIC during port initialization, as VNIC can be invalid for representor ports when the VF link is down. Updated default VNIC/SVIF retrieval to use SVIF as VNIC ID when VF is down, with a minor fix in representor free logic. [ef9e424](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ef9e424aa72477cc5fd35efeb31c923a67383241)
- Adds support for generic template items on the Thor2 platform, including jump action, dynamic tunnels, and flow priority. Fixes the generic application template by enabling queue action, adding rejection checks for every action template, and correcting dynamic VXLAN IP tunnel configuration. Fixes VXLAN IP customer tunnel to correctly populate the upar ID for the tunnel ID in the TCAM table. Resolves several compiler warnings. [30d7102](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/30d7102d9e71f57b32d4c65689726f0786a5890b)
- Fixed an issue where SFF-8436 SFP EEPROMs were not fully read; the diagnostic support offset was incorrectly set to zero, causing Digital Diagnostic Monitoring (DDM) data to be skipped. The fix removes the line that zeroed the offset, enabling proper DDM data reading and correct EEPROM length handling. [7b84004](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7b8400464f14637ed2669dbf732c256bf2447de6)
- Corrected a logical OR to AND in interrupt mode condition, which was always true; single ISR mode now only enables for INTx or MSI, not MSI-X. [fb6b0e9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fb6b0e9a36326a4f13f496b00f7f92aaffe1d5f4)
- Fix duplicate branch in bnx2x driver where both if-else conditions were identical (Coverity); removed conditional and directly assigned reset code. DPDK does not support Wake On LAN, unlike the kernel driver. [87e210e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/87e210eb086f49f32733c579003b9565e46535d7)
- Removed redundant or unreachable code: error log messages for buffer allocation failures (driver tracks via statistics), unnecessary #ifdef blocks for IEEE 1588 (placeholder TODO comments), and an unreachable conditional check (Coverity). [9342a8d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9342a8d977b7f91588bc61844fe16c94ffd615c3), [07e3beb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/07e3bebf3c93750c52ed9be87edd212fbc22da0a), [3868c0c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3868c0ce5ce83eacc9611cc4a83d20120ae3442e)
- Removed redundant NULL checks before calling free(), rte_free(), and rte_ring_free() as these functions safely handle NULL pointers, simplifying code. [8e469ec](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8e469ecf509b38e0c9a738864236c2d22f262839), [e6bf325](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e6bf3256b95c77ee4d0b2874e1896d01c41c2d7c), [165bed4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/165bed470a6d7bad077f178b696f441772182797)
- Fixed build warnings on Ubuntu 24.04 by adding proper casts to resolve datatype mismatches and changing three variables from uint8_t to uint16_t to prevent unsigned-to-signed conversion wrapping. [20c29a0](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/20c29a0e4602b9c7be5ea299457f909846c3785d), [b9799fb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b9799fb5e7a38c824c91b88d3c89250d23c783e6)
- **Breaking change**: MTU settings on mlx5 PCI Virtual Functions (VFs) do not affect the maximum receivable packet size; it is determined by the MTU of the associated Physical Function (PF). DPDK applications using VFs must handle packets up to the PF's MTU, regardless of VF MTU settings. [82caf3d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/82caf3da8a7048a9268fc58ec5647ae969fab688)
- **Breaking change**: Modified the act get API to accept a physical address instead of a virtual pointer, eliminating costly virt2phys conversion. Adjusted mutex locks and delays in the main loop, set thread affinity and naming. Added new bucket factors (32 and 64) and precomputed IOVA addresses to reduce overhead. [ca827d4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ca827d42ad72f90d045716e688b539e53e31a7cc)
- **Breaking change**: Introduce reference counting for Rx queue control structures, freeing them only when no references remain. This replaces a possessor flag workaround. Control structures are now managed in the shared context instead of per-device private data, preventing use-after-free when queues are shared across representors in a domain. Additionally, fixed Rx queue reference count management during flow rule flushing: when a port is stopped, the driver correctly decrements the reference count of queues associated with QUEUE or RSS actions, even if the port is not started. A per-device flag indicates user flow flushing in progress, allowing reference count decrease during flow rule deletion. [3c9a82f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3c9a82fa6edc06c1d4dc6c0ac53609002c4d9462), [1ea333d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1ea333d2de220d5bad600ed50b43f91f7703c123)
- **Security-related**: Removed loop in counter service function to prevent infinite loop; thread now respects configured cycle time. Applications must allocate enough counters to accommodate cycle time. Also ensures proper error codes on flow rule creation failure due to counter allocation, adds debug logging, and adds documentation for the counter service thread. [c0e2996](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c0e29968294c92ca15fdb34ce63fbba01c4562a6)
- **Security-related**: Fixed miniCQE count calculation in compressed CQEs: used CQE's byte_cnt instead of title packet's data_len to avoid segfaults in rxq_cq_decompress_v() due to mbuf corruption. [a7ae9ba](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a7ae9ba1f8c888a7ed546a88a954426477cd24a4)
- **Security-related**: Adds missing unlock call for a frame queue lock in error handling paths of QMAN driver core routines before the escape statement, preventing a resource from being blocked. Also enables compile-time thread safety checks for frame queue locks via Clang's annotation system, replacing FQLOCK and FQUNLOCK macros with inline functions carrying lock annotations. [c7c3a32](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c7c3a329750b81bdaeb3f7ceffac0ec3a65f61f8), [68508c1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/68508c18a91064ced34c664697ce0c6e25b5f787)
- **Security-related**: Fixed representor flow deletion during VFR cleanup and resolved stats counter thread lockup by removing a mutex and adjusting the thread loop to check context availability with appropriate sleep. [67ad400](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/67ad40007cd6bb6ce9f0b3eefe2af611848d10dc)
- **Security-related**: Fixed MSVC implicit conversion warnings in the RCU library by using the RTE_BIT64 macro for 64-bit shifts, correcting a bug where shifting 1 by a thread ID greater than 31 produced incorrect results, and ensuring proper handling of 64-bit bitmaps and improved portability. [ffe827f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ffe827f38e6e0be8a307d7ef9c0e1347874f0af7)
- **Security-related**: Replaced non-thread-safe strtok with reentrant strtok_r to improve thread safety. [9236e5b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9236e5b31d909b45fa52c5e19adf02108a2052d1)
- **Security-related**: RSA now prehashes plain text in asymmetric crypto validation, fixing an oversight where only ECDSA did so. [733c786](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/733c7861492d67eb5fba8ee50fb08d7db82a176d)
- **Security-related**: Corrected signature sizes for EdDSA curves. [2cf2f84](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2cf2f84442e80ff382b05dfd0db62bd969422da4)
- Allocation of STCs (a limited HW resource) is now fixed by allowing users to define the size of STCs allocated per context. Previously, each context allocated a default number, which could exhaust STCs when many representors are used. A new configuration option lets users specify the STC array size, with a default used if not set. For representors, a smaller value is assigned to conserve STCs. [691326d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/691326d15da263d068de71c468c74c225c4f75c3)
- Fixed incorrect CQE indexing in vectorized Rx burst routine to retrieve the proper shared Rx queue port number, ensuring correct port assignment for each packet. [3638f43](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3638f431b9ff39003e31c3a761d407e04b25576a)
- Moved temporary parameter structures from the stack to the queue structure to fix a dangling pointer and eliminate a GCC warning about storing the address of a local variable. [d135766](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d1357665a85b30066c8b69996ddf601332a198f8)
- Fixed ASan-reported misalignment in three structures: added `__rte_aligned(16)` to `mlx5_list`, rounded up ipool size to 8-byte boundary in `mlx5_flow_handle`, and added `__rte_packed` to `mlx5_modification_cmd`. Slight improvement in insertion and deletion rates. [9096753](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/90967539d0d1afcfd5237ed85efdc430359a0e6b)
- Fix creation order of default RSS flows in net/mlx5 driver. Default ingress RSS flows on root table are now synchronized between SWS and HWS: first IPv4(6)+TCP/UDP, then IPv4(6) only, then non-IP traffic. This ensures consistent behavior. [9a66bb7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9a66bb734e1311bcc2bf3b286f7ab6d28975c5c7)
- Fixes several issues in bnxt net driver's tf_ulp template files: ensures L3 protocol type is not included in wildcard key when already matched in profile TCAM; checks ethertype for L2-only flows in Thor2 generic template; resolves layer3 protocol overlap causing match failures; adds flows with non-default priority to wildcard TCAM instead of exact match table. [334f347](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/334f34706e30d448db125007f98fc6926e842aae)
- Segment-length check in iavf Tx prep function ensures single-buffer packets have data length equal to packet length, preventing invalid packet sizes from being transmitted. [4523e07](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4523e0753b243066357f98fd9739fde72605d0fb)
- Updated lcore variables documentation to reflect that the entire API (including macros) is experimental, not just `rte_lcore_var_alloc()`. Corrected the documented default buffer size from 1 MB to 128 kB. [37dda90](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/37dda90ee15b7098bc48356868a87d34f727eecc)
- Windows build instructions now include guidance for producing 64-bit binaries when Visual Studio defaults to 32-bit. [5d5dcd8](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5d5dcd8492f8b2863a2de0f5bad223df27749c09)
- The argparse library can now be compiled with MSVC, enabling broader platform support. [85a9a58](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/85a9a589da099f8da3230b38a0eb5e92e458b90c)
- The mempool library can now be compiled with MSVC, and the build exclusion has been removed. [2cd0c96](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2cd0c96f5b73e81317e97ac719862c9e9149e4ed)
- Aligned the "dispatch" and "xstat_off" members of the rte_node structure to the minimum cache line size for future expansion and performance. [ba0a0e4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ba0a0e44f361cbc4667088a0c0e2d0b63f8dee20)
- Removes unnecessary null checks before EVP_PKEY_CTX_free and EVP_PKEY_free, as these functions safely handle NULL pointers. [0e361f5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0e361f5a0ca96d4e228191ceaf800636e7beeea0)
- **Breaking changes:**  
  - `i40e_get_outer_vlan()` now verifies the return value of `i40e_aq_debug_read_register()`, returns an error on failure (return type changed to `int`), and passes `tpid` via an out parameter. Callers are updated to handle the error and check `tpid`, preventing unsupported ether types in flow rules. [c11c52d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c11c52dd5d2a19c97616ac32a1d4911c48f157d4)  
  - Remote sessions now accept `PurePath` objects for remote paths and `Path` objects for local paths, with OS-aware translation only on the remote side. The `copy_from` and `copy_to` methods now take `destination_dir` instead of `destination_file`, clarifying that the destination is a directory. [441c5fb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/441c5fbf939b55f635d42ad9b7dcc3741e1c2a7c)  
  - Removed the git ref option from DTS. The entire DPDK tree directory can now be copied directly to nodes, giving users control without a git reference. [187a944](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/187a944772c9665a7a10e439560d6a505c4e46a2)  
  - Renamed `ports` to `allowed_ports` and added `blocked_ports` attribute in `EalParams`. [0264e40](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0264e4087f9504a2847258c99c4dec0e9c42922e)  
- **Security-related fixes:**  
  - Re-enabled fallthrough warnings to catch potential bugs. [277552e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/277552e175b3529863adec9bbd8bb6288164506e)  
  - Fixed fallthrough in switch statement found by PVS Studio and re-enabled compiler warning. [11a5adb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/11a5adba21237d5905bcb3f5f695aa5a5cfecd9f)  
- **Other important bug fixes:**  
  - Fixed a bug in testpmd where the "flow update" command did not update the age action context, causing "flow aged destroy" to fail. The fix generates a new port flow entry, sets the age context if an age action is present, and replaces the flow in the flow list. [5b7d82e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5b7d82e817afad123c8ff5f9f0e53ef36fadac3d)  
  - Fixed a bug in testpmd where destroying aged flows could incorrectly remove the first flow entry due to use-after-free of the flow ID. Now a local copy is used for destruction. [098f949](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/098f949f8a70f7618f5390f9c1e9edfb9e5469c4)  
  - Fixed a bug in the member library where bucket selection for displacement always used the primary bucket due to misuse of the logical AND operator instead of bitwise AND. This ensures correct random selection between primary and secondary buckets. [33f5b0d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/33f5b0dcb11580be8091f3b589845e512008e2f0)  
  - When launched with the `-m proc-info` option, the application exited without calling `rte_eal_cleanup()`, causing a memory leak. The fix ensures cleanup is called before exit by using a `goto cleanup` label. [8a171e5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8a171e52ed8b26f768ced79a22286914ebd30180)  
  - Added a 1-second sleep to the async sniffer callback to prevent a race condition where the callback fires before the sniffer is ready. Extended sniffing duration by 1 second to avoid timeout errors. Fixes Bugzilla ID 1573. [1f01c07](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1f01c07d3dab801c70b034c5b22da6212b3466be)  
  - Improves error handling in DMA performance test. Clarifies error message for incorrect NUMA configuration, fixing "acture" to "actual" and identifying whether source, destination, or both NUMA nodes are incorrect. Replaces `printf` with `PRINT_ERR` macro for consistent error reporting. [ff92d10](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ff92d10af68e87561046ba1c0b41a44d6a1dea5c)  
  - A tunable now includes mapped contigmem buffers in core dumps by clearing the `OBJ_FICTITIOUS` flag when enabled. Previously, these buffers were excluded due to the flag. [cbe57f3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cbe57f351b4e6541eb7b50a5c0d6f7e77b45c9db)  
  - Accept `'y'` and `'Y'` values when reading the vfio-pci `unsafe_noiommu_mode` flag, in addition to `'1'`, to match Linux kernel sysfs output. [c77887a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c77887ad87931526768ae501596fcba15c21db82)  
  - Fixed `set_forward_mode` to condition verification on the `verify` argument, allowing callers to skip verification. [d718142](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d7181426eda8b123a0bed505ed8388b7ac3726ab)  
  - `_adjust_addresses` now operates only on packet copies, leaving originals untouched. [a97f8cc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a97f8cc4efef6dd88352c823d98f89a8a8183e58)  
  - Supports preparing multiple expected packets simultaneously; existing functionality updated to use this capability. [8d46662](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8d46662d93e45ab301a5e4dbb1187d26760aed6a)  
- **Minor fixes and improvements:**  
  - A typo in the power management PM QoS documentation was corrected, changing "very except" to "request" and "Some service" to "Some services". [b3477a6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b3477a6bae7e889955edd8b82f359868dc0abb01)  
  - Replaced direct test function calls with a structured unit test suite using `unit_test_suite_runner`, improving test organization and maintainability with named test cases. [a79d5ce](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a79d5ce97b578cdc981445519f6a34c6c06f323b)  
  - Extracts the "with keys" parsing test into a standalone function and adds it to the test suite's list of test cases. [a04d13a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a04d13aa619d1cefb9e2ab67faf8cd326a781cc9)  
  - Separated the test for parsing list values in kvargs into its own test case. [c189450](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c189450b37639bd8b7535e527fc424706db71e0d)  
  - Extracted parsing of empty elements in kvargs into a standalone test case, improving test organization and maintainability. [8b1656a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8b1656a9d951823d35ce0e51b31cf61fa191ac94)  
  - DTS API documentation generation corrected: Doxygen link now uses proper config data scope. DTS docs are built within the main API documentation directory and depend on main docs for installation, preventing standalone builds. Sphinx wrapper now creates destination path if missing. [dfef829](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dfef829263561809e20d0000dd4cf628a20ba9b2)  
  - The ability to link to online Python documentation has been removed, making the documentation build process self-contained and no longer requiring an internet connection. [a0d77f2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a0d77f208c4fc58b6af558efb703c3cc3d062abf)  
  - `autodoc-pydantic` Sphinx extension formats Pydantic models correctly in documentation. [6597fa4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6597fa4a30add6e0790f0e25833c3e073d76a877)  
  - Autodoc mocks missing packages during doc builds without dependencies, causing DTS's custom enum type to fail recognition of enums inheriting from it. This fix extends the type-checking mechanism to apply during doc building by checking an environment variable, making custom enums appear as builtin enums. [949d148](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/949d1488e50f0516df065232640617b5a921ed95)  
  - `DPDKBuildInfo` and `NodeInfo` classes refactored. `NodeInfo` moved to `os_session` and renamed to `OSSESSIONInfo`. `DPDKBuildInfo` moved to `sut_node`. Resolves circular dependencies and improves code organization. [c72ff85](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c72ff85d259287c7ec9880b94891310050fe54e6)  
  - Methods for querying and modifying port queue state and configuration, and capturing forwarding statistics from the testpmd "stop" command. [c986c33](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c986c3393e95f096ec76fa7a54b0a258ea4c0d5b)  
  - Add null-pointer check for scheduler node lookup in Tx queue setup function to prevent dereference and fix Coverity defect. [19a02bc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/19a02bc972759a0fc1f40753ccfe0d8152d68d1e)  
  - Linux excludes hugepages from core dumps by default. Override by setting bit 6 in `coredump_filter`. Including hugepages increases core dump size and may expose sensitive data. [036f72d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/036f72d07249aa36c2d2d61bdaf84a492044d0d9)
- **Breaking:** Reverted import of the VDUSE uAPI header due to license incompatibility; VDUSE support is now conditionally compiled with fallback stubs. [4025e36](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4025e36fa5b7e44df07d53f8e7ddfeab5f1512a2)
- **Breaking:** Renamed FIB library configuration flag from `RTE_FIB_F_NETWORK_ORDER` to `RTE_FIB_F_LOOKUP_NETWORK_ORDER`. [df8b5bf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/df8b5bf744f9aa9a1fbb790dac14a32d9cc9f236)
- **Breaking:** Relocated API header file to the main driver directory; build configuration updated accordingly. [de9f35e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/de9f35ebf2c40cbcdac4ca64888704619b84f8f6)
- **Breaking:** Changed vport action in Rx flow to go to wire (JUMP_TO_UPLINK) instead of DROP, leveraging firmware support. [7911783](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7911783578f5bc6458c2af0670efeb617980ad9c)
- **Security:** Fixed a deadlock in the vhost Rx async path by correcting lock release (write lock was incorrectly released with a read unlock). [22aa9a9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/22aa9a9c7099e1f4b297899c33b4fea1131d3ac7)
- **Security:** Corrected reference counting and condition checking in the mlx5 driver for shared Rx queue control release to ensure proper memory management. [f8f294c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f8f294c66b5ff6ee89590cce56a3d733513ff9a0)
- **Security:** Fixed misplaced parentheses in IPv6 extension header loop that caused comparison to take precedence over assignment, leading to incorrect handling. [0151b80](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0151b80786ebbc62f0ead73bd4708665228a093d)
- **Security:** Fixed DSCP field modification for IPv4 and IPv6 flows in the net/ntnic driver by copying the DSCP value to the flow handler. [9141a19](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/9141a191d40b9cc7c669588cc6dad4c8bafba373)
- **Security:** Corrected byte order of Toeplitz secret key when programming into FPGA to fix incorrect RSS hash values. [ef6ed52](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ef6ed529b220b74b5ca52bc7619f21522cd6a874)
- Fixed mbuf offload flags in bnxt network driver: now reset before populating received packet details (changed from OR-equals to direct assignment). [3e9a43b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3e9a43bad2ce1413be2456c7e53945444aac99f9)
- Cleared the `tx_fault` interrupt to prevent repeated MISC interrupts when an SFP module is unplugged. [916aa13](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/916aa13f4a198aebf5383f9680cb5cd527518f2c)
- Added missing case labels for the E610 device in PTP initialization and fixed RSS redirection table configuration. [d797d98](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d797d98e6313127e5735e069c3e7057b49205bb7), [a80016c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a80016c8b8d1995db5853b980cc8a7af6b5ce863)
- Initialized PTP timestamps to system time instead of zero in ixgbe, i40e, and e1000 drivers, aligning with kernel driver behavior. [d2394b2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d2394b2790f36becf3fef5ff979a988855f1024f), [77e90b1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/77e90b1da5d31a6731b33bf1661f32353df35a48), [41927fd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/41927fde0889abb24aecc3538aed06f3ae1ae09e)
- Changed buffer declaration from `uint8_t` to `uint32_t` and adjusted block size macro to ensure proper alignment for 32-bit header values. [0cbf275](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0cbf27521b0d6e7cb79f41a5e699d82562b09c03)
- Added missing error messages, switched initializers from order-based to index-based, and added static assertions in the net/ntnic driver. [c4e84cd](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c4e84cd7f77ae44598576d88357138a08734a494)
- Documented that in switch mode with the `repr_matching_en` devarg flag enabled, match with compare result item is not supported for ingress rules due to an implicit REPRESENTED_PORT conflict. [c07dbef](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c07dbef7e0208d4f5e36aa241ce360fc470f203b)
- Added L4 port information (UDP and TCP destination ports) to the verbose packet dump in testpmd, making packet metadata more uniform. [e2bce04](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e2bce04b48ab7e9fb184322a4ffa5a58705cae45)
- Added loopback support for the E610 device. [62fd579](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/62fd579fcd15f13f2bed84a003d284d04ddbd9cf)
- Enhanced CPU layout tool and devbind tool to display NUMA node information, following PEP-484 type hints, using f-strings, and reading valid CPU IDs from the "online" sysfs node. [fa269c9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/fa269c9ec1167a43598cb8ab8aeb497b171b5979), [2b091de](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2b091dea3d37d076b6f11e6e676924be77122770), [f63ca24](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f63ca249b8a13171000d348c31d14bda1b3ac0f0), [a7d69ce](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a7d69cef8f20a9aeab4657c20ca3e424823163cd)
- Revised i40e documentation. [0fdf973](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0fdf973cdb1dcef6513fb36f3105c8123844d937)
- DTS documentation build no longer treats Sphinx warnings as errors; warnings are checked manually during the DTS approval process. [f4ccce5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f4ccce58c1a33cb41e1e820da504698437987efc)
- Fixed bugs in bonding unit test: corrected loop variable reuse and MAC address comparison. [112ce39](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/112ce3917674b7e316776305d7e27778d17eb1b7), [f7f8563](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f7f85632daf6d6f525d443f90a0ac3c8a3e40b72)
- **Security fixes:** Fixed TLS zero-length record validation to allow application data and reject other content types, aligning with TLS protocol requirements. [c6f484a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c6f484adf173567a66b72bfebdda41499e723952)  
  Fixed a potential out-of-bounds array access by swapping the order of the index check and null check, preventing undefined behavior. [f86085c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f86085caab0c6c5dc630b9d6ad20d1c728e7703e)
- **Other bug fixes:**  
  - Fixed event device test configuration by replacing manual memset and assignments with a C99 compound literal, ensuring all fields are initialized once and enabling compiler detection of duplicate assignments. [8c08b10](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8c08b10d047ac64fb98709871b192698663af7d7)  
  - Fixed EAL test suite lcore check for cores 0–7, which incorrectly missed lcore 6; now properly validates all eight cores. [357f915](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/357f915ef5e1280d921fb103ea33066e7a888ed2)  
  - Removed a redundant condition check in the policy action loop, as the loop always exits upon encountering the flow end action. [4c2e746](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4c2e7468426ae6be3f2a8f2d15e7d1222083eb9d)  
  - Added return value checks for `remove()` calls in test temporary file cleanup, with assertions to verify success, fixing multiple Coverity issues. [554e802](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/554e802ee3204a24149f76946c8ad81b2582349c)  
  - Fixed NUMA node display exception on systems with NUMA disabled in BIOS by checking for NUMANode keys in lspci output before enabling NUMA node output. [b456bf5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b456bf50063deeecbf3eaedfbd0949a0e7cbe19f)  
  - Restored active marker accidentally omitted when NUMA node printouts were added, to indicate the currently active device. [828fe9d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/828fe9de4c186ca3715a068ea59f689dde853e7f)  
  - Included PCI ID for E610 Virtual Function (VF) to ensure proper device recognition. [f678f3d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f678f3dea8fd2a5e7fe2d78b5889c141ac263f7a)  
  - Reverted frequency adjustment addition to ptpclient example due to GPL-licensed code; removed PI servo controller and its command-line option. Basic PMD support for high-precision clocks remains, but accuracy is reduced to microsecond level. [5357e22](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5357e228f3fc9db861a45fa512db6decfb29352b)
- **Script and tooling fixes:**  
  - Excluded 'stdio' from codespell's bad word list in checkpatch to prevent false positives with stdio.h; validated input parameter in build-dict.sh to ensure it points to a valid codespell git clone. [5f33036](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5f33036cbc59d943ecc272916a722268e293cac3)  
  - Fixed a bug in the script that validates multiple commits from "Fixes:" lines; now all commit hashes are validated before proceeding. [dbee696](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dbee69686b63fab960a98295a7de542d45de9b6d)  
  - Deduplicated functions for marking commits with 'Cc: stable@dpdk.org' and 'Fixes:' patterns into a single generic function. [e2e4775](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e2e4775cc7a0699a66283ed6ddb0c2a51daae1ef)
- **Documentation and example fixes:**  
  - Corrected "Stats per Queue" documentation to reflect current usage: per-queue statistics support, not stat mapping; clarified that statistics are provided only for the first 16 queues (zero if unsupported). [71eae7f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/71eae7fe3eac90b70200460c714d1c13ee43dc25)  
  - Fixed grammar and phrasing in simple_mp, symmetric_mp, and client-server examples by simplifying wording, updating cross-references, restructuring lists, and removing redundancy. [c0f5a9d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c0f5a9dd74f41688660e4ef84487a175ee44a54a)
- **Inclusive language updates:**  
  - Replaced "master" with "test structure" and "segregate" with "separate" in comments to align with inclusive naming recommendations. [bccabd1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bccabd15fed89e12de5fdee022ca92ffea6e83d0), [f81cdd7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f81cdd729bdfd362ab834d4b3efe78f5ec06b56a), [f2b1510](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f2b1510f19d7bfd386d130fa38123d6e2152cf80)
- **Code cleanup and build improvements:**  
  - Removed dead code methods left after test suite removal, fixing mypy errors from references to removed symbols. [0c81db5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0c81db58701c244638757ee342f38be002b53a83)  
  - Removed the nested 'html' directory from the API documentation build process to simplify deployment and make paths more consistent. [497cf54](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/497cf54829c28859482998957d75477ae2b1bc1c)  
  - Removed release candidate designation, finalizing release. [1b3bf11](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1b3bf1128d9bda5595861814792f74b8f57160c8)
- **Other:**  
  - I understand. Please provide the release note entry you'd like me to rewrite for conciseness. [a4f4555](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/a4f455560f747af8cd7fa99db86e757b7ff2fe79)
## ♻️ refactor
- Consolidated PMD function definitions into common code for cn9k and cn10k platforms; changed security session parameter type to void pointer to reflect internal driver structure status. [99e540d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/99e540d65c9adbc1515543a635734055046a3c18)
- Unified mbox ID for representor events (port state, PF/VF state, MTU changes). Consolidated event handling in a single dispatcher. Data structures updated with a union for event data and a new link state type. [e66a6e5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e66a6e5401ed8dc1e03e42af516deb8b8b2dfac8)
- Removed single-event enqueue and dequeue function pointers from the eventdev library. They were internal and offered no performance benefit, and the API is unaffected as applications do not call them directly. [34e3ad3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/34e3ad3a1e423a874d0d2388efa04d5d6ebee340)
- Per-driver AVX-512 support checks replaced with standard variables from config/x86, using `cc_has_avx512` and `cc_avx512_flags` to reduce duplication and ensure consistent detection. [ef7a402](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ef7a4025cd714189dc333bb19ea60c2abdeffb7d)
- Consolidated algorithm enumeration definitions from platform-specific headers for 9k and 10k platforms into a single common header, eliminating redundancy and ensuring consistent naming. Updated all references to use the new common names. [c31a946](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/c31a94655944a03716257e01376123cde65f7b35)
- Frame Attribute Flags (FAF) parser uses dedicated flags at offset 2 instead of previous protocol type extracts, enabling soft protocol distribution with user-defined flags. New functions handle FAF extracts, existing flow configuration functions are updated to use FAF for protocol identification, and a previous workaround is removed. [200a33e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/200a33e4c2b0f401a9bfb3cc4a8f3fe8ad8923ad)
- Refactored NFP driver: flow item calculate function unified arguments via common parameter structure; initialization accepts single pointer to `pf_dev`; extracted helper functions for PHY representor port allocation and PF representor port initialization; updated VF check logic. [2a71691](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2a716911fd746b55694fb9daf419a873cdc99e24) [6f708e5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6f708e5215517879b8f05905c90a674d06206daf) [0006b08](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0006b08758a29c64671883eabc4c2822a70ebe93) [608bc94](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/608bc946e25fc8ca94331c64adcd5750b49446b0)
- Log messages now capitalize the first letter of each sentence. [f6272c7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f6272c7aa61f65f71fb376cb0cb3890e502f3521)
- Reorganized FPGA register definitions into alphanumeric order for better readability and variant support. [bf53e46](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/bf53e467b4a347a3b9d37b3e9ee64803246af791)
## 📦 build
- Fixes an issue where installing PMD drivers without a subdirectory caused symlink creation to produce invalid library files or fail with a "same file" error. Skips this step when no subdirectory is specified, preventing installation errors. [dae002f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dae002ff55a416206e710b05a06f050d5bc4dc6d)
- Enabled the zero-length array warning after removing marker fields causing false positives, and limited the '-Wno-address-of-packed-member' warning flag to the drivers subfolder, moving it from global configuration. Libraries and applications are built without suppression, except for vhost and ipsec-secgw. This enables the warning for most components, improving code quality, while hardware drivers may need it long-term. [1435e94](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1435e94f9ea79ae1d3748af90895ed53ef220d6c) [63c9142](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/63c9142b3d634c2abf5a9ef0594ffb652517791c)
- Replaced get_cross_property with get_external_property in build files for cross-compilation, and added support for VERSION file, built-in options, and pkg_config_libdir in cross-build files. [6f3dbd3](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/6f3dbd306de03410cffb40a0f0b47a2cdcfcf362) [b0d0c84](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b0d0c84b3c0ffcdd0c3ef307c4966f06bd296db7)
- Centralizes AVX-512 support checks in the build system, defining flags and verifying compiler support for AVX-512F, AVX-512VL, AVX-512DQ, and AVX-512BW. Ensures consistent detection across all drivers and libraries, reducing duplication and potential inconsistencies. [979f59d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/979f59decf69023bdba93d16900c45c57fbdd9c0)
- Removed unnecessary checks for the compiler.links() function, version checks, and replaced shell command with built-in Meson feature to read a file, simplifying build configuration. [2909f9a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2909f9afbfd1b54ace204d40d57b68e6058aca28) [3019b11](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3019b11fc1fc7c2ed8a405a551391e51fd94a087) [1980543](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/198054305b95b9568017571baf49327d23834e9a)
- Configuration output includes source and build directory paths at the end to help identify build locations. [2169d01](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2169d012477c41e619a1485bf19ea42ea1d2b2f2)
## 👷 ci
- Added CI jobs: testing vm_power_manager example compilation and running additional checks in private repositories before submitting patches to the mailing list. These checks require git history or source code and identify potential issues early without blocking compilation tests in other jobs. [d3a214a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d3a214acdfc2016d748760cf54727fc013c54d21) [2233925](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2233925d78b2c36fa2db610fd41ca7848b89e0c3)
- Updated CI environment: upgraded Meson to 0.57.2 to fix compatibility with Python 3.11, resolving unit test failures in Fedora 37, and removed a workaround that reduced ASLR entropy for AddressSanitizer (ASan) in Ubuntu GitHub Actions images, as it is now directly applied in the images, simplifying CI configuration. [dbcd72f](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/dbcd72f3fba0babd2d801c2c63ee537df459df66) [5744e91](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5744e912341ee26a0dd5b9ec28b16b8a4e45d1bc)
## 🧪 test
- **Breaking change**: Removed unused resource API from test framework. Originally used for cfgfile tests, it was never built after the meson build system conversion. Will be replaced by a simpler method for cfgfile tests. [e3052c1](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/e3052c189f8a6f5bc260b263f3b3be0306751768)
- **Security**: Added negative test cases for `rte_fib_rcu_qsbr_add()` covering invalid arguments (NULL fib, NULL config), error code checks, unsupported FIB types, and duplicate RCU attachments. Also added a NULL fib check in the function itself, returning `-EINVAL`. [259deb7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/259deb73d989d91cb1ddb8c60c24de58aa09609a)
- **Bug fix**: Fixed alignment macro tests: MAX_NUM lacked parentheses, causing operator precedence issues. After correction, the test space was too large, so tests switched from exhaustive to random sampling. Bug existed since 2013. [b3e64fe](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b3e64fe596a3117edf6d3a79a6c5238a9b92dc4f)
- **Bug fix**: Configuring DCB in testpmd no longer loses RSS settings. The DCB configuration function now uses RSS configuration from software instead of reading from hardware registers, which were cleared by some PMDs during port stop. This ensures RSS and DCB can be enabled simultaneously, particularly for Intel 82599 NICs. [34847a7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/34847a73034566ed1dab8bbc6882a12492b7f7fd)
- **Bug fix**: The cksum performance test was not assigned to any test suite, causing a meson warning. Fixed by registering it with the perf suite using REGISTER_PERF_TEST instead of REGISTER_TEST_COMMAND. [99ed5e9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/99ed5e931f59ebaf48cf4ab045f75466a2c388cd)
- **Bug fix**: Removed type casts from builtin cmdline commands that blocked compiler type checking. [0a3901a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0a3901aa624a690faa49ca081c468320d4edcb7a)
- **New tests**: Adds positive tests, fixes set/cancel test interval, and uses standard TEST macros. Verifies correct alarm set/cancel, proper timing, and suitability for CI fast-tests. [31fcb7d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/31fcb7d7dbf04b3ce4ae66602485c9797f6dbcd8)
- **New tests**: Register a callback for error recovery with cryptodev. A unit test verifies recovery by generating an error with a heap-allocated mbuf, invoking the callback, and performing a simple crypto operation to confirm recovery. [462ba59](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/462ba592e53f74057bbf944c9e3e553317d7c32d)
- **New tests**: Test measures distribution of hash LSBs for a random tuple with variable bits. Generates a random hash key, measures worst distribution for a given RETA size, adjusts the key using a new API, and measures again. Also compares with a well-known RSS hash key used in some PMDs. [89398ec](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/89398ecec0486f91e1d9302ae2fb454f3b2dd012)
- **New tests**: Extracts basic token count tests from test_valid_kvargs into a new standalone test case, test_basic_token_count, covering various valid inputs including empty strings and edge cases. [ce7e937](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ce7e937f1aae7aecca5de5ffa4c051103020ca5e)
- **New tests**: Separates parsing without valid keys tests into a new standalone test case, improving code readability and maintainability. The new test verifies parsing of key-value arguments without a valid keys list, including processing and counting of keys. [7dd7102](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7dd71026216ec94436c69d91ac812c70f5a7712d)
- **New tests**: Restores cfgfile unit tests disabled after Meson migration. Tests now use a common unit test macro, test data is in an include file, and temporary files are generated per test. Build system updated to handle auto-generated files without scanning for test input. Re-enables cfgfile tests and improves maintainability. [be22019](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/be22019a58c484c0b43ec32de8cd4e58a91aa53f)
- **New tests**: Added MAC filter test suite to DTS. Tests MAC address filtering: add/remove, invalid addresses, and multicast filtering. Vendor-agnostic, uses TestPMD to verify ethdev API calls. [4f7b228](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4f7b228f30a43d0a1ff27cbe0745cce85285d55c)
- **New tests**: Test suite validates port blocklisting in testpmd with three test cases: verifying no ports are blocked when none are specified, blocking a single port, and blocking all but one port. It uses TestPmdShell to run testpmd with specified blocked ports and checks that blocked ports are absent from the allowed list. [125d85e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/125d85e72f9b85043fd75e4027cee76bf2c2c4ac)
- **Performance**: Mempool performance test handles larger burst sizes (up to 256 and RTE_MEMPOOL_CACHE_MAX_SIZE) and more kept objects (up to 32768). Test iteration duration reduced from 5 to 1 second, rate calculation precision improved by timing actual duration, and cache guard added to per-lcore stats. Test now exercises mempool driver when kept objects exceed typical cache size. Test split into separate functions for 1 core, 2 cores, and all cores, with new test registration. [7775adc](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7775adc618811cd3713403cae7d6acc5b296d558)
- **Framework**: Decorators for functional and performance test cases add attributes to test functions, shifting discovery from regex-based name matching to attribute checking. Test case filtering logic moves from the runner to the TestSuite class, and decorators enable adding variables to test cases. [b6eb500](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/b6eb5004460e1e96269a29a54ae70c24611ad4b0)
- **Framework**: Test cases and suites can be skipped when irrelevant to the testing environment. A protocol extends them with attributes to track skip status and reason. Execution logic checks these attributes, skips accordingly, and logs the reason. Results handling includes a new SKIP result, with pass rate statistics excluding SKIP results. [566201a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/566201aebdda2aa2b290d551b8f01cb329daf428)
- **Framework**: Each DTS test run now uses only one DPDK build, simplifying configuration. Previously, multiple builds were allowed, but a pre-built DPDK repository eliminates the need for multiple targets, streamlining setup and teardown. [11b2279](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/11b2279afbb5e628e9cff26b4b3fff4127711949)
- **Framework**: Uses test suite specification class imports, directly using class objects from TestSuiteSpec instead of re-importing modules. [5ea59af](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/5ea59af40b6d53676f2b369d06047f2ad2685e90)
- **Cleanup**: Moved test configuration files out of a separate subdirectory to simplify the file structure. [58bf7cb](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/58bf7cb18f75f83d4a9cc5fb47211c22bef52bfb)
## 📚 docs
- New guide covering security protocols in DPDK: MACsec, IPsec, TLS, PDCP, PSP, Wireguard, and QUIC, with details on supported algorithms, pros/cons, and related documentation. [8711af2](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8711af290f353f727989684de2e75c7c41d2779a)
- Removed a confusing instruction from the contributor guide that suggested patches could be sent without Cc'ing maintainers. The change clarifies that maintainers should always be included, as this is good practice. [d5f8103](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/d5f81030df75c587885245ff1b14f123448a97c7)
- New document with guidelines for DPDK driver contributors: early community engagement, progressive upstreaming, splitting patches by feature, best practices, and suggestions for test tools and dependencies. [ad6833e](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ad6833e5accbf67b4e1e8c9ca4911ba1163d3cb5)
- Reorganized the programmer's guide for better navigation and consistency. Added categories to the index, sorted chapters, and clarified titles. Created subdirectories for ethdev and eventdev to group related files. Removed unnecessary link anchors and replaced `:ref:` references with `:doc:` links for proper cross-referencing. [41dd9a6](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/41dd9a6bc2d9c6e20e139ad713cc9d172572dd43)
- Reviews and improves sample application guides for grammar, clarity, and formatting consistency. Adds "Overview" sections, corrects punctuation and phrasing, and standardizes references to DPDK APIs and functions for enhanced readability and uniform structure. [8750576](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8750576fb2a9a067ffbcce4bab6481f3bfa47097)
- Corrected grammar, spelling, and formatting; removed instructional comments; improved descriptions for clarity and consistency. [84339a7](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/84339a739845272045ce2a5e077def38c0a2170a)
- Ice driver documentation revised to reflect current recommendations. [ae52bdf](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/ae52bdf2ce7a6a32202ddfdb1369f415a067fd3b)
- In the ntnic driver, added new log modules with suffixes, fixed per-module log level configuration, adjusted log levels for initialization functions to reduce startup noise, and replaced NIM and PMD log modules with NTNIC for consistency. [3489b87](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3489b87b497ed477257f5ed5b112c27c1407a68d)
- New options for building DPDK externally have been added to the DTS documentation, including support for specifying a DPDK source tree directory or tarball, with options to indicate if the source is on the SUT node and to use a pre-compiled build directory. The help text and usage examples clarify that DTS can now accept a pre-compiled build or compile DPDK on the SUT node, and the minimum requirements now include a config file and a DPDK source location. [0ae3214](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0ae32140331f61e6c7e6fe59b4c27e6a2059f395)
- API documentation sources for the DTS framework, generated with sphinx-apidoc and modified to improve appearance, especially toctree definitions and module titles. [1e472b5](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/1e472b5746aeb6189fa254ab82ce4cd27999f868)
- Sphinx generates DTS API documentation from Python docstrings using the Google docstring format and sphinx.ext.napoleon extension. The documentation is built with the standard DPDK build system and is accessible from the DPDK API doxygen page. [7f93264](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/7f9326423a045f7346a459280dc98fed4afd6811)
- Added documentation sources for DTS capabilities and topology. [64fdb62](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/64fdb622e3f15da32dee0feffb18e552ff14c044)
- Moved docstrings directly to each Pydantic model field using the `#:` syntax, so the generated API documentation now shows a single, correct entry per field instead of two conflicting entries. [3fbb93c](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3fbb93cff3be23a45fc1ec524f83d001a30df273)
## 🔧 chore
- Breaking change: Release notes empty; previous ABI exceptions removed; CI ABI checks turned off (backward compatibility not maintained). [cb9187b](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/cb9187bc5c2b4bab0ad80194ac3b60491de14e8c)
- Dependency updates: Intel IPsec Multi-buffer library now required; Warlock package superseded by pydantic. [8484d74](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8484d74bd656bc0e951a3ed4e0816ee0fea5e593), [f94100d](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/f94100da8ead989c6ff070da1c9a8754d69207e9)
- Driver updates: added macro for E610 VF HV device and included latest bug fixes and enhancements for ENA driver. [659e367](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/659e36767e77b31088dc1f543ecba94562ecf08f), [3a2509a](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/3a2509ab1c524a7d0f106eb24f5f839db9e5bf82)
- Code refactoring: moved firmware version data to central location, refactored VDUSE reconnection logic, removed unused GITS translator VFIO mapping, and updated template files for Thor 2 VFR changes. [000feb4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/000feb4c417c9776236342e524d98e61e43e2f12), [5597769](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/559776944efc6a7f7024cf6bc8583662134a4eb6), [8831b45](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/8831b45622a9bb4a76269854518a556b17ac825f), [2d1f4d4](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/2d1f4d49e2234671fb9d3884bc7fbd0a5567cb5e)
- Documentation updates: added link status feature to ntnic driver and removed documentation for obsolete "normal_llq_hdr" devarg. [4ea73e9](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/4ea73e921f9ceea16a8165c9d477fd1442373a92), [0525b49](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/0525b496a4ecdc3b53b91fd36893d5d2541b056c)
- Cross-compilation improvements: added configuration for 32-bit on 64-bit Debian/Ubuntu and replaced deprecated cross-compilation function. [08cf111](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/08cf11121e06ea1b1fc14127ae8fe08888e77324), [530c0ac](https://github.com/DPDK/dpdk/commit/530c0ac654ff89de3f3e83e6ce6c6693739eb670)
